`nx/shaders/tools/wrap_raw_shader.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Wrap exact ring microcode in a conservative register-linked 2008 container.

This is for D3D-generated shaders for which no original container survived.  It does not guess a
shader identity: VS fetches/exports and PS interpolators are exposed by hardware register number.
"""

import argparse
import pathlib
import struct


def u32(data: bytes | bytearray, offset: int) -> int:
    return struct.unpack_from(">I", data, offset)[0]


def put(data: bytearray, offset: int, value: int) -> None:
    struct.pack_into(">I", data, offset, value)


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("raw", type=pathlib.Path)
    parser.add_argument("template", type=pathlib.Path)
    parser.add_argument("output", type=pathlib.Path)
    parser.add_argument("--stage", choices=("vs", "ps"), required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--depth", action="store_true", help="declare a pixel depth export")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    raw = args.raw.read_bytes()
    if not raw or len(raw) % 4:
        raise SystemExit("raw microcode must contain whole big-endian words")
    source = args.template.read_bytes()
    if len(source) < 36 or (u32(source, 0) & 0xFFFFFF00) != 0x102A1100:
        raise SystemExit("template is not a 2008 Xenos shader container")
    shader = u32(source, 24)
    if shader + (36 if args.stage == "vs" else 32) > u32(source, 4):
        raise SystemExit("template shader header is truncated")

    # Keep the known-good empty CTAB and definition area.  The physical part is replaced entirely.
    virtual_size = u32(source, 4)
    if args.stage == "ps":
        virtual_size = max(virtual_size, shader + 32 + 16 * 4)
    container = bytearray(virtual_size + len(raw))
    container[: min(u32(source, 4), len(source))] = source[: min(u32(source, 4), len(source))]
    put(container, 4, virtual_size)
    put(container, 8, len(raw))
    put(container, shader, 0)
    put(container, shader + 4, len(raw))

    # No semantic table is trusted. XenosRecomp's empty-CTAB path links these registers directly.
    put(container, shader + 20, (16 << 5) if args.stage == "ps" else 0)
    if args.stage == "vs":
        put(container, shader + 24, 0)
        put(container, shader + 28, 0)
        put(container, shader + 32, 0)
    else:
        put(container, shader + 24, 0)
        put(container, shader + 28, 0x11 if args.depth else 0x01)
        for register in range(16):
            put(container, shader + 32 + register * 4, register << 8)
    container[virtual_size:] = raw
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_bytes(container)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`nx/tools/experiments/perf_shared_memory_hot.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Perf patch for the ReXGlue / masseffect-nx SDK shared memory: stop write-watching "hot" pages.

Guest memory uploaded to the GPU copy is write-protected so a CPU write invalidates it (page fault -> unprotect ->
invalidate -> re-upload -> protect again). Sleeping Dogs rewrites its dynamic vertex / index data every frame, many
times per page (ring buffers advance through a page draw by draw): ~11 000 faults/s and ~40 000 protect calls/s;
VirtualProtect plus the global lock the fault handlers hold cost ~25-30% of the "GPU Commands" thread on the
desktop bench, and on the Switch both are Horizon svcs.

A page that faults N times (cvar sdnx_hot_page_faults, default 4) within a window (sdnx_hot_page_window_ms,
default 2000) becomes hot: it is no longer marked valid nor protected after an upload, so every request uploads
its current CPU contents again (always correct, costs copies). Hot pages are forgotten every window so pages that
calm down get cached again. sdnx_hot_pages = false restores the original behaviour.
Needs perf_shared_memory_coalesce.py applied first.

Usage: perf_shared_memory_hot.py <sdk root>      (idempotent)
"""
import sys

root = sys.argv[1].rstrip("/\\")
mark = "SDNX perf: hot"


def patch(path, edits):
    s = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read().replace("\r\n", "\n")
    if mark in s:
        print("already patched:", path)
        return
    for old, new in edits:
        if s.count(old) != 1:
            sys.exit("pattern not found (%d) in %s:\n%s" % (s.count(old), path, old))
        s = s.replace(old, new)
    open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(s)
    print("patched:", path)


patch(root + "/include/rex/graphics/shared_memory.h", [(
    "  std::vector<uint64_t> system_page_flags_stale_;\n",
    "  std::vector<uint64_t> system_page_flags_stale_;\n"
    "  // %s pages: rewritten by the CPU often - neither cached as valid nor write-watched.\n"
    "  std::vector<uint64_t> system_page_flags_hot_;\n"
    "  std::vector<uint8_t> page_fault_count_;\n"
    "  uint64_t hot_window_start_ms_ = 0;\n" % mark,
)])

patch(root + "/src/graphics/shared_memory.cpp", [
    ("#include <rex/memory.h>\n",
     "#include <rex/memory.h>\n#include <rex/cvar.h>\n\n#include <chrono>\n\n"
     "// %s pages (see nx/tools/perf_shared_memory_hot.py in the Sleeping Dogs port).\n"
     "REXCVAR_DEFINE_BOOL(sdnx_hot_pages, true, \"GPU\",\n"
     "                    \"Do not write-watch guest pages rewritten often, re-upload them on use\");\n"
     "REXCVAR_DEFINE_INT32(sdnx_hot_page_faults, 4, \"GPU\", \"Write faults within the window that make a page hot\");\n"
     "REXCVAR_DEFINE_INT32(sdnx_hot_page_window_ms, 2000, \"GPU\", \"Hot page window, ms\");\n" % mark),
    ("  system_page_flags_stale_.assign(num_system_page_flags_, 0);  // SDNX perf: stale\n",
     "  system_page_flags_stale_.assign(num_system_page_flags_, 0);  // SDNX perf: stale\n"
     "  system_page_flags_hot_.assign(num_system_page_flags_, 0);\n"
     "  page_fault_count_.assign(size_t(num_system_page_flags_) * 64, 0);\n"),
    ("  system_page_flags_stale_.clear();\n",
     "  system_page_flags_stale_.clear();\n  system_page_flags_hot_.clear();\n  page_fault_count_.clear();\n"),
    # MakeRangeValid: hot pages stay invalid and unwatched.
    ("""  {
    auto global_lock = global_critical_region_.Acquire();

    for (uint32_t i = valid_block_first; i <= valid_block_last; ++i) {
      uint64_t valid_bits = UINT64_MAX;""",
     """  bool skip_hot = !written_by_gpu && REXCVAR_GET(sdnx_hot_pages);
  bool any_hot = false;
  {
    auto global_lock = global_critical_region_.Acquire();

    for (uint32_t i = valid_block_first; i <= valid_block_last; ++i) {
      uint64_t valid_bits = skip_hot ? ~system_page_flags_hot_[i] : UINT64_MAX;
      any_hot |= skip_hot && system_page_flags_hot_[i] != 0;"""),
    ("""  if (memory_invalidation_callback_handle_) {
    memory().EnablePhysicalMemoryAccessCallbacks(
        valid_page_first << page_size_log2_,
        (valid_page_last - valid_page_first + 1) << page_size_log2_, true, false);
  }
}""",
     """  if (memory_invalidation_callback_handle_) {
    if (!any_hot) {
      memory().EnablePhysicalMemoryAccessCallbacks(
          valid_page_first << page_size_log2_,
          (valid_page_last - valid_page_first + 1) << page_size_log2_, true, false);
    } else {
      // SDNX perf: hot pages are not watched - watch the runs between them.
      uint32_t run_first = UINT32_MAX;
      for (uint32_t page = valid_page_first; page <= valid_page_last + 1; ++page) {
        bool hot = page > valid_page_last || ((system_page_flags_hot_[page >> 6] >> (page & 63)) & 1);
        if (!hot && run_first == UINT32_MAX) {
          run_first = page;
        } else if (hot && run_first != UINT32_MAX) {
          memory().EnablePhysicalMemoryAccessCallbacks(run_first << page_size_log2_,
                                                       (page - run_first) << page_size_log2_, true, false);
          run_first = UINT32_MAX;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}"""),
    # Invalidation: count faults of the exact pages, make them hot.
    ("""  auto global_lock = global_critical_region_.Acquire();

  if (!exact_range) {""",
     """  auto global_lock = global_critical_region_.Acquire();

  if (REXCVAR_GET(sdnx_hot_pages) && !page_fault_count_.empty()) {
    // SDNX perf: hot pages - count the CPU writes to pages that were valid.
    uint32_t threshold = uint32_t(std::max(REXCVAR_GET(sdnx_hot_page_faults), int32_t(1)));
    for (uint32_t page = page_first; page <= page_last; ++page) {
      uint64_t bit = uint64_t(1) << (page & 63);
      if (!(system_page_flags_valid_[page >> 6] & bit) ||
          (system_page_flags_valid_and_gpu_written_[page >> 6] & bit)) {
        continue;
      }
      uint8_t& count = page_fault_count_[page];
      if (count < 255) {
        ++count;
      }
      if (count >= threshold) {
        system_page_flags_hot_[page >> 6] |= bit;
      }
    }
  }

  if (!exact_range) {"""),
    # Window reset at the start of RequestRanges.
    ("""  SCOPE_profile_cpu_f("gpu");

  std::sort(merged_ranges.begin(), merged_ranges.end(),""",
     """  SCOPE_profile_cpu_f("gpu");

  if (REXCVAR_GET(sdnx_hot_pages)) {
    // SDNX perf: hot pages - forget them every window, so pages that calm down are cached and watched again.
    uint64_t now_ms = uint64_t(std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
                                   std::chrono::steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch())
                                   .count());
    if (now_ms - hot_window_start_ms_ >= uint64_t(std::max(REXCVAR_GET(sdnx_hot_page_window_ms), int32_t(100)))) {
      auto global_lock = global_critical_region_.Acquire();
      std::fill(system_page_flags_hot_.begin(), system_page_flags_hot_.end(), uint64_t(0));
      std::fill(page_fault_count_.begin(), page_fault_count_.end(), uint8_t(0));
      hot_window_start_ms_ = now_ms;
    }
  }

  std::sort(merged_ranges.begin(), merged_ranges.end(),"""),
])

```

`nx/tools/fix_draw_extent.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""ReXGlue / masseffect-nx SDK: execute_unclipped_draw_vs_on_cpu defaults to false (Xenia: true). Set it to true.

Why it matters for Sleeping Dogs: the game clears stencil with a full-screen triangle drawn without clipping and
with the full 8192x8192 scissor. Without running its vertex shader on the CPU, the render target cache assumes the
draw covers all 8192 rows; that wraps around the 2048-tile EDRAM and takes ownership of the tiles of the light
accumulation buffer. The following resolve of the light buffer then dumps the 4x MSAA depth buffer instead
(texture full of (0, 1, 1, 0)): blue picture, garbage on the floor, no proper lighting. Found 2026-11-08 with
SDNX_LOG_RESOLVE logging in the Vulkan render target cache. Idempotent.

Usage: fix_draw_extent.py <sdk root>
"""
import sys

path = sys.argv[1].rstrip("/\\") + "/src/graphics/util/draw_extent_estimator.cpp"
s = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read().replace("\r\n", "\n")
bad = "REXCVAR_DEFINE_BOOL(execute_unclipped_draw_vs_on_cpu, false, \"GPU\","
good = "REXCVAR_DEFINE_BOOL(execute_unclipped_draw_vs_on_cpu, true, \"GPU\","
if good in s:
    print("already fixed:", path)
elif bad in s:
    open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(s.replace(bad, good))
    print("fixed:", path)
else:
    sys.exit("pattern not found: " + path)

```

`nx/tools/fix_resolve_uint.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""ReXGlue / masseffect-nx SDK (and upstream Xenia): resolves to an integer number format saturate.

Xenos resolves honour RB_COPY_DEST_INFO.copy_dest_number: with kUnsignedInteger the colour is multiplied by
2^copy_dest_exp_bias and stored as an integer. The resolve shaders always pack fixed formats as normalized
(value * 65535 for 16 bits, * 255 for 8 bits) and ignore the number format, so value * 2^bias saturates to 1.0.

Sleeping Dogs resolves its 7e3 light buffer to k_16_16_16_16 with number = unsigned integer and exp_bias = +11
(1.0 -> 2048, range 0..32) and samples it as UNORM. In Xenia every lit texel became 1.0 or 0: flat ambient-only
picture, no sunlight or sun shafts. Found 2026-11-08 (SDNX_LOG_RESOLVE: "dest_fmt=26 exp_bias=11 number=2").

Fix: for an unsigned integer destination the normalized packer gets exp_bias - 16 (16-bit) or - 8 (8-bit), i.e.
value * 2^bias / 65536 * 65535 ~ value * 2^bias (relative error 1/65536). Idempotent.

Usage: fix_resolve_uint.py <sdk root>
"""
import sys

path = sys.argv[1].rstrip("/\\") + "/src/graphics/util/draw.cpp"
s = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read().replace("\r\n", "\n")
mark = "SDNX: integer resolve destination"
if mark in s:
    print("already fixed:", path)
    sys.exit(0)
anchor = "  // Patch and write RB_COPY_DEST_INFO.\n"
if s.count(anchor) != 1:
    sys.exit("pattern not found: " + path)
add = """  // %s: the hardware stores value * 2^exp_bias as an integer, the resolve
  // shaders pack fixed formats as normalized - divide by the normalization factor (2^16 or 2^8).
  if (!is_depth &&
      rb_copy_dest_info.copy_dest_number == xenos::SurfaceNumberFormat::kUnsignedInteger) {
    switch (dest_format) {
      case xenos::TextureFormat::k_16:
      case xenos::TextureFormat::k_16_16:
      case xenos::TextureFormat::k_16_16_16_16:
        exp_bias -= 16;
        break;
      case xenos::TextureFormat::k_8:
      case xenos::TextureFormat::k_8_8:
      case xenos::TextureFormat::k_8_8_8_8:
        exp_bias -= 8;
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
    exp_bias = std::max(exp_bias, int32_t(-32));
  }
""" % mark
s = s.replace(anchor, add + anchor)
open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(s)
print("fixed:", path)

```

`nx/tools/fix_stencil_bit.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""ReXGlue / masseffect-nx SDK (and upstream Xenia): Vulkan depth -> stencil-bit EDRAM transfers never discard.

Without VK_EXT_shader_stencil_export (NVIDIA on Windows, NVK 26.x on the Switch) stencil is transferred by 8 draws,
one per bit, each killing the fragment when that bit of the source stencil is 0. The transfer shader generator only
fills `packed` (the value the kill test reads) inside the "source depth loaded" branch, but kDepthToStencilBit
does not load depth at all, so `packed` stays empty, the kill is never emitted and every bit is written: after
any depth transfer without stencil export the destination stencil is 0xFF everywhere.

Sleeping Dogs: the game clears stencil to 0 in a 4x MSAA view of the depth buffer, switches back to 1x (transfer)
and draws its full-screen scene composite with stencil == 0. With stencil 0xFF the pass draws nothing, and the
picture left in EDRAM is the 7e3 HDR scene bits reinterpreted as 8888: "psychedelic" colours. Found 2026-11-08
in RenderDoc (frame_frame14618, transfer ps 1297 has no OpKill). Fix: take the stencil directly. Idempotent.

Usage: fix_stencil_bit.py <sdk root>
"""
import re
import sys

path = sys.argv[1].rstrip("/\\") + "/src/graphics/vulkan/render_target_cache.cpp"
s = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read().replace("\r\n", "\n")
mark = "SDNX: depth -> stencil bit"
if mark in s:
    print("already fixed:", path)
    sys.exit(0)
pat = re.compile(r"(builder\.makeUintConstant\(8\),\s*builder\.makeUintConstant\(24\)\);\n\s*\}\n\s*\}\n)(\s*)\}\n(\s*switch \(mode\.output\) \{)")
m = pat.search(s)
if not m:
    sys.exit("pattern not found: " + path)
ind = m.group(2)
add = (ind + "} else if (mode.output == TransferOutput::kStencilBit && source_stencil[0] != spv::NoResult) {\n" +
       ind + "  // " + mark + " transfers don't load depth, the kill test needs the stencil in 0:7.\n" +
       ind + "  packed = source_stencil[0];\n" +
       ind + "}\n")
s = s[:m.start()] + m.group(1) + add + m.group(3) + s[m.end():]
open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(s)
print("fixed:", path)

```

`nx/tools/fix_tfetch_lod.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Fix a merge bug in the masseffect-nx SDK SPIR-V translator (sdk/src/graphics/pipeline/shader/spirv_translator.cpp).

In v0.2.1 the declarations of two function-local variables are interleaved:

    var_main_tfetch_lod_ =
    var_main_vfetch_bound_ =
        createVariable(int,   "xe_var_vfetch_bound");
        createVariable(float, "xe_var_tfetch_lod");      <- result discarded

so the texture-fetch LOD and the vertex-fetch bound are the same int variable and float LODs are stored into it:
invalid SPIR-V (spirv-val: "OpStore ... type does not match"). The NVIDIA desktop compiler crashes on it; NVK on the
Switch accepts it, but texture LOD computations are then wrong. Idempotent.

Usage: fix_tfetch_lod.py <sdk root>
"""
import sys

path = sys.argv[1].rstrip("/\\") + "/src/graphics/pipeline/shader/spirv_translator.cpp"
s = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read()
bad = """    var_main_tfetch_lod_ =
    var_main_vfetch_bound_ =
        builder_->createVariable(spv::NoPrecision, spv::StorageClassFunction, type_int_,
                                 "xe_var_vfetch_bound", const_int_0_);
        builder_->createVariable(spv::NoPrecision, spv::StorageClassFunction, type_float_,
                                 "xe_var_tfetch_lod", const_float_0_);
"""
good = """    var_main_vfetch_bound_ =
        builder_->createVariable(spv::NoPrecision, spv::StorageClassFunction, type_int_,
                                 "xe_var_vfetch_bound", const_int_0_);
    var_main_tfetch_lod_ =
        builder_->createVariable(spv::NoPrecision, spv::StorageClassFunction, type_float_,
                                 "xe_var_tfetch_lod", const_float_0_);
"""
s2 = s.replace("\r\n", "\n")
if good in s2:
    print("already fixed:", path)
elif bad in s2:
    open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(s2.replace(bad, good))
    print("fixed:", path)
else:
    sys.exit("pattern not found: " + path)

```

`nx/tools/fix_tfetch_numformat.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""ReXGlue / masseffect-nx SDK (and upstream Xenia): texture fetch ignores the integer number format.

Xenos texture fetch constants have num_format (word 3 bit 0): 0 = fraction (normalized), 1 = integer. With integer
the fetch returns the raw stored integer (e.g. 0..65535 for 16 bits) before exp_adjust. Xenia always samples fixed
formats as normalized, so an integer fetch comes back 65535 (or 255) times too small.

Sleeping Dogs samples its light buffer (k_16_16_16_16, resolved as unsigned integer with exp_bias +11) with
num_format = int and exp_adjust = -11: integer * 2^-11 = the original HDR value. In Xenia the scene composite got
value / 2^16 - no sunlight, no sun shafts, flat ambient-only picture. Found 2026-11-08 (frame_frame6851, fetch
constant slot 4: fmt 26, base 0F867000, num_format int, exp_adjust -11). Works together with
fix_resolve_uint.py (the resolve side).

Fix: in the tfetch result exponent bias, multiply by 65535 for k_16 / k_16_16 / k_16_16_16_16 and by 255 for
k_8 / k_8_8 / k_8_8_8_8 when num_format is integer. Idempotent.

Usage: fix_tfetch_numformat.py <sdk root>
"""
import sys

path = sys.argv[1].rstrip("/\\") + "/src/graphics/pipeline/shader/spirv_translator_fetch.cpp"
s = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read().replace("\r\n", "\n")
mark = "SDNX: integer num_format"
if mark in s:
    print("already fixed:", path)
    sys.exit(0)
old = """        spv::Id result_exponent_bias = builder_->createBinBuiltinCall(
            type_float_, ext_inst_glsl_std_450_, GLSLstd450Ldexp, const_float_1_,
            builder_->createTriOp(spv::OpBitFieldSExtract, type_int_, fetch_constant_word_3_signed,
                                  builder_->makeUintConstant(13), builder_->makeUintConstant(6)));
"""
if s.count(old) != 1:
    sys.exit("pattern not found: " + path)
new = """        // %s: word 3 bit 0 = 1 - the fetch returns the raw integer, while the
        // host image is sampled as normalized - scale by the maximum value of the format (word 1 bits 0:5).
        spv::Id result_number_scale;
        {
          id_vector_temp_.clear();
          id_vector_temp_.push_back(const_int_0_);
          id_vector_temp_.push_back(
              builder_->makeIntConstant(int((fetch_constant_word_0_index + 1) >> 2)));
          id_vector_temp_.push_back(
              builder_->makeIntConstant(int((fetch_constant_word_0_index + 1) & 3)));
          spv::Id fetch_constant_word_1_format = builder_->createBinOp(
              spv::OpBitwiseAnd, type_uint_,
              builder_->createLoad(builder_->createAccessChain(spv::StorageClassUniform,
                                                               uniform_fetch_constants_,
                                                               id_vector_temp_),
                                   spv::NoPrecision),
              builder_->makeUintConstant(63));
          spv::Id is_integer = builder_->createBinOp(
              spv::OpINotEqual, type_bool_,
              builder_->createBinOp(
                  spv::OpBitwiseAnd, type_uint_,
                  builder_->createUnaryOp(spv::OpBitcast, type_uint_, fetch_constant_word_3_signed),
                  builder_->makeUintConstant(1)),
              builder_->makeUintConstant(0));
          auto format_is = [&](uint32_t format) {
            return builder_->createBinOp(spv::OpIEqual, type_bool_, fetch_constant_word_1_format,
                                         builder_->makeUintConstant(format));
          };
          spv::Id is_16bpc = builder_->createBinOp(
              spv::OpLogicalOr, type_bool_,
              builder_->createBinOp(spv::OpLogicalOr, type_bool_,
                                    format_is(uint32_t(xenos::TextureFormat::k_16)),
                                    format_is(uint32_t(xenos::TextureFormat::k_16_16))),
              format_is(uint32_t(xenos::TextureFormat::k_16_16_16_16)));
          spv::Id is_8bpc = builder_->createBinOp(
              spv::OpLogicalOr, type_bool_,
              builder_->createBinOp(spv::OpLogicalOr, type_bool_,
                                    format_is(uint32_t(xenos::TextureFormat::k_8)),
                                    format_is(uint32_t(xenos::TextureFormat::k_8_8))),
              format_is(uint32_t(xenos::TextureFormat::k_8_8_8_8)));
          spv::Id format_max = builder_->createTriOp(
              spv::OpSelect, type_float_, is_16bpc, builder_->makeFloatConstant(65535.0f),
              builder_->createTriOp(spv::OpSelect, type_float_, is_8bpc,
                                    builder_->makeFloatConstant(255.0f), const_float_1_));
          result_number_scale =
              builder_->createTriOp(spv::OpSelect, type_float_, is_integer, format_max, const_float_1_);
        }
        spv::Id result_exponent_bias = builder_->createBinBuiltinCall(
            type_float_, ext_inst_glsl_std_450_, GLSLstd450Ldexp, result_number_scale,
            builder_->createTriOp(spv::OpBitFieldSExtract, type_int_, fetch_constant_word_3_signed,
                                  builder_->makeUintConstant(13), builder_->makeUintConstant(6)));
""" % mark
s = s.replace(old, new)
open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(s)
print("fixed:", path)

```

`nx/tools/perf_shared_memory_coalesce.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Perf patch for the ReXGlue / masseffect-nx SDK shared memory (GPU copy of guest memory): re-upload whole stale runs.

When the guest writes a watched page, the write-watch fault invalidates a widened run of pages (up to the 64-page
block, 256 KB). Draws then request only the pieces they need, so one fault turned into ~5.5 separate uploads, each
with its own render pass break (SubmitBarriers) and its own VirtualProtect / RexGmProtect call when the range is
made valid again. Sleeping Dogs: ~56 000 protect calls/s, 15.7% of the "GPU Commands" thread in VirtualProtect
(desktop bench, 2026-11-08).

Now an upload range is extended to the neighbouring pages of the same 64-page block that are stale - were valid
(so their guest memory is mapped) and got invalidated by a CPU write, are not GPU-written - and the whole run is
uploaded and protected at once. Data uploaded is current CPU memory, so it is always correct.

Usage: perf_shared_memory_coalesce.py <sdk root>      (idempotent)
"""
import sys

root = sys.argv[1].rstrip("/\\")
mark = "SDNX perf: stale"


def patch(path, edits):
    s = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read().replace("\r\n", "\n")
    if mark in s:
        print("already patched:", path)
        return
    for old, new in edits:
        if s.count(old) != 1:
            sys.exit("pattern not found (%d) in %s:\n%s" % (s.count(old), path, old))
        s = s.replace(old, new)
    open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(s)
    print("patched:", path)


patch(root + "/include/rex/graphics/shared_memory.h", [(
    "  std::vector<uint64_t> system_page_flags_valid_;\n",
    "  std::vector<uint64_t> system_page_flags_valid_;\n"
    "  // %s pages: were valid, then invalidated by a CPU write (memory known to be mapped).\n"
    "  std::vector<uint64_t> system_page_flags_stale_;\n" % mark,
)])

patch(root + "/src/graphics/shared_memory.cpp", [
    ("  system_page_flags_valid_.assign(num_system_page_flags_, 0);\n",
     "  system_page_flags_valid_.assign(num_system_page_flags_, 0);\n"
     "  system_page_flags_stale_.assign(num_system_page_flags_, 0);  // %s\n" % mark),
    ("  system_page_flags_valid_.clear();\n",
     "  system_page_flags_valid_.clear();\n  system_page_flags_stale_.clear();\n"),
    ("  std::fill(system_page_flags_valid_.begin(), system_page_flags_valid_.end(), uint64_t(0));\n",
     "  std::fill(system_page_flags_valid_.begin(), system_page_flags_valid_.end(), uint64_t(0));\n"
     "  std::fill(system_page_flags_stale_.begin(), system_page_flags_stale_.end(), uint64_t(0));\n"),
    ("      system_page_flags_valid_[i] |= valid_bits;\n",
     "      system_page_flags_valid_[i] |= valid_bits;\n      system_page_flags_stale_[i] &= ~valid_bits;\n"),
    ("    system_page_flags_valid_[i] &= ~invalidate_bits;\n",
     "    system_page_flags_stale_[i] |= system_page_flags_valid_[i] & invalidate_bits &\n"
     "                                   ~system_page_flags_valid_and_gpu_written_[i];\n"
     "    system_page_flags_valid_[i] &= ~invalidate_bits;\n"),
    # Extend the upload ranges (still under the global lock), then merge.
    ("""      if (range_start != UINT32_MAX) {
        append_upload_range(range_start, page_last + 1 - range_start);
      }
    }
  }
""", """      if (range_start != UINT32_MAX) {
        append_upload_range(range_start, page_last + 1 - range_start);
      }
    }
    // SDNX perf: stale runs - grow each upload range over the stale pages of its 64-page block.
    auto is_stale = [this](uint32_t page) {
      return (system_page_flags_stale_[page >> 6] >> (page & 63)) & 1;
    };
    for (std::pair<uint32_t, uint32_t>& upload_range : upload_ranges_) {
      uint32_t first = upload_range.first;
      uint32_t end = upload_range.first + upload_range.second;
      while ((first & 63) && is_stale(first - 1)) {
        --first;
      }
      while ((end & 63) && end < (kBufferSize >> page_size_log2_) && is_stale(end)) {
        ++end;
      }
      upload_range.first = first;
      upload_range.second = end - first;
    }
    if (upload_ranges_.size() > 1) {
      size_t write = 0;
      for (size_t i = 1; i < upload_ranges_.size(); ++i) {
        std::pair<uint32_t, uint32_t>& previous = upload_ranges_[write];
        const std::pair<uint32_t, uint32_t>& current = upload_ranges_[i];
        if (current.first <= previous.first + previous.second) {
          previous.second = std::max(previous.first + previous.second, current.first + current.second) -
                            previous.first;
        } else {
          upload_ranges_[++write] = current;
        }
      }
      upload_ranges_.resize(write + 1);
    }
  }
  for (const std::pair<uint32_t, uint32_t>& upload_range : upload_ranges_) {
    if (!EnsureHostGpuMemoryAllocated(upload_range.first << page_size_log2_,
                                      upload_range.second << page_size_log2_)) {
      return false;
    }
  }
"""),
])

```

`nx/tools/sd_build_nro.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Sleeping Dogs -> Switch: build the NRO in the devkitpro/devkita64 container (run inside WSL Ubuntu, after
# nx/tools/sd_codegen.sh).
#
#   SUDO_PW=... bash /mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/nx/tools/sd_build_nro.sh        (docker needs sudo here; or be in group docker)
#
# Output: ~/masseffect-nx/out/nx-sd/sdretail.nro, copied to D:\CODE\SDNX\nx\sdretail.nro
# Environment: JOBS (default 4: each job needs 1-2 GB, WSL has ~7 GB), NRO_OUT (default out/nx-sd)
set -euo pipefail

ME="$HOME/masseffect-nx"
MESA=/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/mesa-sdk
NRO_OUT="${NRO_OUT:-out/nx-sd}"
JOBS="${JOBS:-4}"
DEST=/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/nx

docker_cmd() {
  if docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; then docker "$@";
  elif [[ -n "${SUDO_PW:-}" ]]; then echo "$SUDO_PW" | sudo -S -p "" docker "$@";
  else sudo docker "$@"; fi
}

[[ -f "$ME/sdretail/generated/rexglue.cmake" ]] || { echo "error: run nx/tools/sd_codegen.sh first" >&2; exit 1; }
[[ -f "$MESA/opt/devkitpro/portlibs/switch/lib/libvulkan.a" ]] || { echo "error: no NVK SDK in $MESA" >&2; exit 1; }

echo "== build $NRO_OUT (-j$JOBS)"
docker_cmd run --rm -v "$ME:$ME" -v "$MESA:$MESA:ro" -w "$ME" \
  -e ME="$ME" -e MESA="$MESA" -e NRO_OUT="$NRO_OUT" -e JOBS="$JOBS" \
  devkitpro/devkita64:latest bash -euo pipefail -c '
    cmake -S sdretail -B "$NRO_OUT" -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
      -DCMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE="$ME/tools/switch/cmake/switch-devkitA64.cmake" \
      -DDEVKITPRO=/opt/devkitpro \
      -DREXGLUE_SWITCH_NVK_SDK="$MESA/opt/devkitpro/portlibs/switch" > "$NRO_OUT.configure.log" 2>&1 \
      || { tail -40 "$NRO_OUT.configure.log"; exit 1; }
    cmake --build "$NRO_OUT" -j "$JOBS"
    # crt0 must be first in .text or the NRO does not boot (masseffect-nx tools/build_nro.sh)
    /opt/devkitpro/devkitA64/bin/aarch64-none-elf-nm "$NRO_OUT/sdretail" | grep "^0000000000000000 T _start$" > /dev/null
    echo "OK: _start at 0"
  '
cp -f "$ME/$NRO_OUT/sdretail.nro" "$DEST/sdretail.nro"
ls -la "$DEST/sdretail.nro"

```

`nx/tools/sd_codegen.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Sleeping Dogs -> Switch: code generation + post-codegen steps (run inside WSL Ubuntu).
#
#   bash /mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/nx/tools/sd_codegen.sh
#   SKIP_CODEGEN=1 bash ...   only sync + post-codegen patches (after changing hooks in src/)
#
# 1. copies the project sources from D:\CODE\SDNX\nx\sdretail (the canonical copy) to ~/masseffect-nx/sdretail
#    (the SDK tree it builds against), keeping generated/;
# 2. runs the host code generator (~/masseffect-nx/sdk/out/host/rexglue);
# 3. post-codegen patches from masseffect-nx/tools (generic, only their masseffect_ file prefix is replaced):
#      direct_calls.py     direct calls to functions our sources do not hook (REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_X) in src/)
#      pch_no_volatile.py  guest loads/stores without volatile
# 4. register-use checks (read_before_write.py) for non_volatile_as_local / non_argument_as_local: every
#    function listed there needs share_registers in perf_overrides.toml, or a review.
set -euo pipefail

ME="$HOME/masseffect-nx"
SRC=/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/nx/sdretail
APP="$ME/sdretail"
GEN="$APP/generated/default"
TOOLS_SD="$APP/.tools_sd"
REXGLUE="${REXGLUE:-$ME/sdk/out/host/rexglue}"

echo "== sync sources $SRC -> $APP"
mkdir -p "$APP/src"
for f in CMakeLists.txt sdretail_manifest.toml overrides.toml perf_overrides.toml sdretail.toml; do
  sed 's/\r$//' "$SRC/$f" > "$APP/$f"
done
for f in "$SRC"/src/*; do sed 's/\r$//' "$f" > "$APP/src/$(basename "$f")"; done

if [[ "${SKIP_CODEGEN:-0}" != 1 ]]; then
  echo "== codegen"
  ( cd "$APP" && "$REXGLUE" codegen sdretail_manifest.toml ) 2>&1 | tee "$APP/codegen.log" | tail -5
fi

echo "== post-codegen patches"
mkdir -p "$TOOLS_SD"
for t in direct_calls.py pch_no_volatile.py pch_common.py read_before_write.py; do
  sed 's/masseffect_/sdretail_/g' "$ME/tools/$t" > "$TOOLS_SD/$t"
done
# Functions our sources hook must keep being called through sub_X (the hook), not __imp__sub_X.
cat "$APP"/src/*.cpp "$APP"/src/*.h 2>/dev/null | grep -o 'REX_HOOK_RAW(sub_[0-9A-F]*' | grep -o 'sub_[0-9A-F]*' \
  | sort -u > "$TOOLS_SD/hooked.txt" || true
echo "hooked: $(paste -sd' ' "$TOOLS_SD/hooked.txt")"
python3 "$TOOLS_SD/direct_calls.py" --gen "$GEN" --undo > /dev/null
python3 "$TOOLS_SD/direct_calls.py" --gen "$GEN" --hooked-list "$TOOLS_SD/hooked.txt"
python3 "$TOOLS_SD/pch_no_volatile.py" "$GEN/sdretail_pch.h"

echo "== register-use checks (read_before_write.py)"
# Expected (2026-11-08, see perf_overrides.toml): non-volatile = 5 harmless functions; --volatile = none.
python3 "$TOOLS_SD/read_before_write.py" "$GEN" > "$APP/read_before_write.txt" 2>&1
python3 "$TOOLS_SD/read_before_write.py" "$GEN" --volatile > "$APP/read_before_write_volatile.txt" 2>&1
echo "-- non-volatile registers:"; grep -v '^#' "$APP/read_before_write.txt" || true
echo "-- argument registers (--volatile):"; grep -v '^#' "$APP/read_before_write_volatile.txt" || true
echo "lists: $APP/read_before_write*.txt"

```

`nx/tools/sd_sync.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Copy the NRO sources from D:\CODE\SDNX\nx\sdretail to ~/masseffect-nx/sdretail without touching generated/
# (no codegen, no post-codegen patches): for changes in src/ or the toml/CMake files only. Unchanged files keep
# their timestamps, so the next sd_build_nro.sh rebuilds only what changed.
set -euo pipefail
APP="$HOME/masseffect-nx/sdretail"
SRC=/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/nx/sdretail
mkdir -p "$APP/src"
copy() {  # src dst
  sed 's/\r$//' "$1" > "$2.tmp"
  if cmp -s "$2.tmp" "$2"; then rm "$2.tmp"; else mv "$2.tmp" "$2"; echo "updated $2"; fi
}
for f in CMakeLists.txt sdretail_manifest.toml overrides.toml perf_overrides.toml sdretail.toml; do
  copy "$SRC/$f" "$APP/$f"
done
for f in "$SRC"/src/*; do copy "$f" "$APP/src/$(basename "$f")"; done

```

`nx/tools/sdnx_sync_sdk.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Sleeping Dogs -> Switch: copy the native renderer (sdnx backend) from the desktop SDK fork (D:\CODE\me-sdk, where it
# is developed and tested on the bench) into the SDK the NRO builds against (~/masseffect-nx/sdk). Run inside WSL,
# then nx/tools/sd_build_nro.sh (the NRO build compiles the SDK from source: REXSDK_DIR).
#
# Copied: src/graphics/sdnx/, include/rex/graphics/sdnx/, and the three files that hook it in:
#   src/graphics/CMakeLists.txt (the sdnx sources), src/graphics/plugin_main.cpp (backend choice: cvar
#   sdnx_native_renderer), include/rex/ui/vulkan/functions/device_1_0.inc (vkCmdBlitImage, vkCmdCopyImage, ...).
# The other differences between the two SDKs are emulation/debug changes of the desktop bench and stay out.
# Unchanged files keep their timestamps (no needless rebuild).
set -euo pipefail
SRC=/mnt/d/CODE/me-sdk
DST="$HOME/masseffect-nx/sdk"
copy() {  # relative path
  mkdir -p "$(dirname "$DST/$1")"
  sed 's/\r$//' "$SRC/$1" > "$DST/$1.tmp"
  if cmp -s "$DST/$1.tmp" "$DST/$1"; then rm "$DST/$1.tmp"; else mv "$DST/$1.tmp" "$DST/$1"; echo "updated $1"; fi
}
for d in src/graphics/sdnx include/rex/graphics/sdnx; do
  (cd "$SRC" && find "$d" -type f) | while read -r f; do copy "$f"; done
done
copy src/graphics/CMakeLists.txt
copy src/graphics/plugin_main.cpp
copy include/rex/ui/vulkan/functions/device_1_0.inc

```

`port/shaders/internal/ps_A4A965C189287B99.hlsl`:

```hlsl
// SDNX: replacement for the run-time Direct3D clear pixel shader A4A965C189287B99 (9 words: one ALU export,
// oC0 = r0 = interpolator 0). The colour comes from vs_477B525CE86A19A5 (the clear colour of the vertices,
// TEXCOORD0 = location 0). The earlier hand-built version read no interpolator at all (only FragCoord /
// FrontFacing) and filled the clears with a position-dependent colour (green-yellow, split along the diagonal).
float4 main(float4 iTexCoord0 : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0
{
    return iTexCoord0;
}

```

`port/shaders/internal/template_vs.hlsl`:

```hlsl
// SDNX (Sleeping Dogs port): run-time vertex fetch swizzles, see fetchApply / g_FetchSwizzle.
#define SDNX_FETCH_SWIZZLE 1
#ifndef SHADER_COMMON_H_INCLUDED
#define SHADER_COMMON_H_INCLUDED

#define SPEC_CONSTANT_R11G11B10_NORMAL  (1 << 0)
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_TEST        (1 << 1)
// MASSEFFECT: constants arrive through a dynamic UBO (a constant bank on Maxwell) instead of being read
// through a 64-bit pointer. High bit so as not to clash with the UNLEASHED_RECOMP ones.
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_CONSTANTS_UBO    (1 << 8)
// The tfetch offset is scaled with 1/size taken from the shared constants instead of querying the
// texture for its size. A size query is another texture unit operation.
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_INV_TEX_SIZE    (1 << 9)
/*
 * The alpha test function, specialized (bits 16-18).
 *
 * g_AlphaFunction used to come from the shared constants, that is, at run time, so alphaTestValue
 * was a 7-case switch: ~8 branches in every pixel shader that does alpha testing. It was not needed
 * at all: the app already knows the function when it creates the pipeline (it comes from RB_COLORCONTROL, which is already in the key), so with three more bits the compiler
 * keeps the one comparison that applies and drops the other six. The output is bit-identical.
 */
// Bits 15, 19 and 23 are reserved (unused here; left free for the app's own use).
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_FUNC_SHIFT  16
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_FUNC_MASK   (7 << 16)

#ifdef UNLEASHED_RECOMP
    #define SPEC_CONSTANT_BICUBIC_GI_FILTER (1 << 2)
    #define SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_TO_COVERAGE (1 << 3)
    #define SPEC_CONSTANT_REVERSE_Z         (1 << 4)
#endif

#if !defined(__cplusplus) || defined(__INTELLISENSE__)

#define FLT_MIN asfloat(0xff7fffff)
#define FLT_MAX asfloat(0x7f7fffff)

#ifdef __spirv__

struct PushConstants
{
    uint64_t VertexShaderConstants;
    uint64_t PixelShaderConstants;
    uint64_t SharedConstants;
};

[[vk::push_constant]] ConstantBuffer<PushConstants> g_PushConstants;

// MASSEFFECT: the same blocks of the upload buffer, also as dynamic UBOs in set 4. With
// -fvk-use-dx-layout each float4 takes 16 contiguous bytes, so any 4-byte word of the shared block is
// a component: v[B / 16][(B % 16) / 4], and asuint reads it without changing a bit.
struct MassEffectBlockVs { float4 v[256]; };
struct MassEffectBlockPs { float4 v[224]; };
#ifdef SDNX_FETCH_SWIZZLE
// SDNX: the block grows to 688 bytes: _InvSize of slots 16-31 reach byte 616, the vertex fetch swizzle
// codes take bytes 624-687, see g_FetchSwizzle.
struct MassEffectSharedBlock { float4 v[43]; };
#else
struct MassEffectSharedBlock { float4 v[31]; };
#endif
[[vk::binding(0, 4)]] ConstantBuffer<MassEffectBlockVs> g_UboVertex;
[[vk::binding(1, 4)]] ConstantBuffer<MassEffectBlockPs> g_UboPixel;
[[vk::binding(2, 4)]] ConstantBuffer<MassEffectSharedBlock> g_UboShared;
#define MASSEFFECT_UBO ((g_SpecConstants & SPEC_CONSTANT_CONSTANTS_UBO) != 0)
#define MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(B)  asuint(g_UboShared.v[(B) / 16][((B) % 16) / 4])
#define MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(B) g_UboShared.v[(B) / 16][((B) % 16) / 4]

#define g_Booleans                 (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(256) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 256))
#define g_SwappedTexcoords         (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(260) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 260))
#define g_HalfPixelOffset          (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(264), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(268)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 264))
#define g_AlphaThreshold           (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(272) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 272))
// MASSEFFECT: alpha test function (RB_COLORCONTROL.alpha_func): 0 never, 1 <, 2 ==, 3 <=,
// 4 >, 5 !=, 6 >=, 7 always.
#define g_AlphaFunction            (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(276) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 276))
// MASSEFFECT: position to host clip space, like ndc_scale/ndc_offset in the
// emulation (graphics/util/draw.cpp). (1, 1) and (0, 0) for normal draws;
// with Xenos clipping disabled it converts from pixels to NDC.
#define g_NdcScale                 (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(280), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(284)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 280))
#define g_NdcOffset                (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(288), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(292)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 288))
// MASSEFFECT: where each component of the vertex input at that location comes from
// (D3D patches the fetch swizzle according to the declaration). 0xFFF = as is.
#define g_InputRemap(LOC)          (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(296 + (LOC) * 4) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 296 + (LOC) * 4))
// SDNX: the 12-bit destination swizzle Direct3D wrote into vertex fetch number K of the loaded vertex
// shader (K = order of first emission, listed by the "SDNX_FETCH_ORDER" comment at the end of the HLSL),
// two per word: bits 0-11 even K, bits 16-27 odd K, at byte 624 + (K / 2) * 4 of the shared constants.
#define g_FetchSwizzle(K)          (((MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(624 + ((K) / 2) * 4) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 624 + ((K) / 2) * 4)) >> (((K) & 1) * 16)) & 0xFFF)

[[vk::constant_id(0)]] const uint g_SpecConstants = 0;

#define g_SpecConstants() g_SpecConstants

#else

#define DEFINE_SHARED_CONSTANTS() \
    uint g_Booleans : packoffset(c16.x); \
    uint g_SwappedTexcoords : packoffset(c16.y); \
    float2 g_HalfPixelOffset : packoffset(c16.z); \
    float g_AlphaThreshold : packoffset(c17.x); \
    uint g_AlphaFunction : packoffset(c17.y); \
    float2 g_NdcScale : packoffset(c17.z); \
    float2 g_NdcOffset : packoffset(c18.x);

uint g_SpecConstants();

#define g_InputRemap(LOC) 0xFFF
#define g_FetchSwizzle(K) 0x688

#endif

Texture2D<float4> g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[] : register(t0, space0);
Texture3D<float4> g_Texture3DDescriptorHeap[] : register(t0, space1);
TextureCube<float4> g_TextureCubeDescriptorHeap[] : register(t0, space2);
SamplerState g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[] : register(s0, space3);

uint2 getTexture2DDimensions(Texture2D<float4> texture)
{
    uint2 dimensions;
    texture.GetDimensions(dimensions.x, dimensions.y);
    return dimensions;
}

// The native renderer packs the four post-swizzle Xenos TextureSign values into bits 24-31 of the
// descriptor index. Values: 0 unsigned, 1 signed (the renderer still warns for it), 2 unsigned biased,
// 3 Xenos piecewise-linear gamma. Descriptor heaps use the low 24 bits.
float masseffectGammaToLinear(float gamma)
{
    gamma = saturate(gamma);
    float scale;
    float offset;
    if (gamma >= 96.0 / 255.0)
    {
        if (gamma >= 192.0 / 255.0) { scale = 8.0 / 1024.0; offset = -1024.0; }
        else                         { scale = 4.0 / 1024.0; offset = -256.0; }
    }
    else
    {
        if (gamma >= 64.0 / 255.0) { scale = 2.0 / 1024.0; offset = -64.0; }
        else                        { scale = 1.0 / 1024.0; offset = 0.0; }
    }
    float linearValue = gamma * ((255.0 * 1024.0) * scale) + offset;
    linearValue += trunc(linearValue * scale);
    return linearValue * (1.0 / 1023.0);
}

float4 masseffectApplyTextureSigns(float4 value, uint packedSigns)
{
    for (uint i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
    {
        uint sign = (packedSigns >> (i * 2)) & 3;
        if (sign == 2)
            value[i] = value[i] * 2.0 - 1.0;
        else if (sign == 3)
            value[i] = masseffectGammaToLinear(value[i]);
    }
    return value;
}

float4 tfetch2D(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float2 texCoord, float2 offset, float2 invSize)
{
    uint packedSigns = resourceDescriptorIndex >> 24;
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    Texture2D<float4> texture = g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex];
    // With the bit set, the size comes from a constant and the texture does not have to be queried. It
    // is the same computation with the same number: the renderer writes 1/size of the host image. With a
    // ternary, DXC evaluates both branches and the size query stays: an if with [branch] is needed for
    // the dead branch to disappear when the pipeline is specialized.
    float2 displacement;
    [branch] if (g_SpecConstants() & SPEC_CONSTANT_INV_TEX_SIZE)
        displacement = offset * invSize;
    else
        displacement = offset / getTexture2DDimensions(texture);
    // SDNX: vertex shaders have no derivatives - sample level 0 there (2 Sleeping Dogs vertex shaders).
#if defined(__SHADER_TARGET_STAGE) && __SHADER_TARGET_STAGE == __SHADER_STAGE_VERTEX
    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(
        texture.SampleLevel(g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex], texCoord + displacement, 0.0), packedSigns);
#else
    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(
        texture.Sample(g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex], texCoord + displacement), packedSigns);
#endif
}

float2 getWeights2D(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float2 texCoord, float2 offset)
{
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    Texture2D<float4> texture = g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex];
    return select(isnan(texCoord), 0.0, frac(texCoord * getTexture2DDimensions(texture) + offset - 0.5));
}

float w0(float a)
{
    return (1.0f / 6.0f) * (a * (a * (-a + 3.0f) - 3.0f) + 1.0f);
}

float w1(float a)
{
    return (1.0f / 6.0f) * (a * a * (3.0f * a - 6.0f) + 4.0f);
}

float w2(float a)
{
    return (1.0f / 6.0f) * (a * (a * (-3.0f * a + 3.0f) + 3.0f) + 1.0f);
}

float w3(float a)
{
    return (1.0f / 6.0f) * (a * a * a);
}

float g0(float a)
{
    return w0(a) + w1(a);
}

float g1(float a)
{
    return w2(a) + w3(a);
}

float h0(float a)
{
    return -1.0f + w1(a) / (w0(a) + w1(a)) + 0.5f;
}

float h1(float a)
{
    return 1.0f + w3(a) / (w2(a) + w3(a)) + 0.5f;
}

float4 tfetch2DBicubic(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float2 texCoord, float2 offset)
{
    uint packedSigns = resourceDescriptorIndex >> 24;
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    Texture2D<float4> texture = g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex];
    SamplerState samplerState = g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex];
    uint2 dimensions = getTexture2DDimensions(texture);
    
    float x = texCoord.x * dimensions.x + offset.x;
    float y = texCoord.y * dimensions.y + offset.y;

    x -= 0.5f;
    y -= 0.5f;
    float px = floor(x);
    float py = floor(y);
    float fx = x - px;
    float fy = y - py;

    float g0x = g0(fx);
    float g1x = g1(fx);
    float h0x = h0(fx);
    float h1x = h1(fx);
    float h0y = h0(fy);
    float h1y = h1(fy);

    float4 r =
        g0(fy) * (g0x * texture.Sample(samplerState, float2(px + h0x, py + h0y) / float2(dimensions)) +
            g1x * texture.Sample(samplerState, float2(px + h1x, py + h0y) / float2(dimensions))) +
        g1(fy) * (g0x * texture.Sample(samplerState, float2(px + h0x, py + h1y) / float2(dimensions)) +
            g1x * texture.Sample(samplerState, float2(px + h1x, py + h1y) / float2(dimensions)));

    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(r, packedSigns);
}

float4 tfetch3D(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float3 texCoord)
{
    uint packedSigns = resourceDescriptorIndex >> 24;
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(
        g_Texture3DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex].Sample(g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex], texCoord),
        packedSigns);
}

struct CubeMapData
{
    float3 cubeMapDirections[2];
    uint cubeMapIndex;
};

float4 tfetchCube(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float3 texCoord, inout CubeMapData cubeMapData)
{
    uint packedSigns = resourceDescriptorIndex >> 24;
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(
        g_TextureCubeDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex].Sample(g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex], cubeMapData.cubeMapDirections[texCoord.z]),
        packedSigns);
}

float4 tfetchR11G11B10(uint4 value)
{
    if (g_SpecConstants() & SPEC_CONSTANT_R11G11B10_NORMAL)
    {
        return float4(
            (value.x & 0x00000400 ? -1.0 : 0.0) + ((value.x & 0x3FF) / 1024.0),
            (value.x & 0x00200000 ? -1.0 : 0.0) + (((value.x >> 11) & 0x3FF) / 1024.0),
            (value.x & 0x80000000 ? -1.0 : 0.0) + (((value.x >> 22) & 0x1FF) / 512.0),
            0.0);
    }
    else
    {
        return asfloat(value);
    }
}

float4 tfetchTexcoord(uint swappedTexcoords, float4 value, uint semanticIndex)
{
    return (swappedTexcoords & (1ull << semanticIndex)) != 0 ? value.yxwz : value;
}

// SDNX: a vertex fetch with a run-time destination swizzle (Xenos FetchDestinationSwizzle, 3 bits per
// component: 0-3 = data component, 4 = 0, 5 = 1, 6 and 7 = keep the register's component).
float4 fetchApply(float4 previous, float4 value, uint code)
{
    float4 result = previous;
    for (uint i = 0; i < 4; i++)
    {
        uint source = (code >> (i * 3)) & 7;
        if (source < 4)
            result[i] = value[source];
        else if (source == 4)
            result[i] = 0.0;
        else if (source == 5)
            result[i] = 1.0;
    }
    return result;
}

// MASSEFFECT: 3 bits per component: 0-3 = data component, 4 = 0, 5 = 1, 7 = unchanged.
float4 remapInput(float4 value, uint code)
{
    if (code == 0xFFF)
        return value;

    float4 result = value;
    for (uint i = 0; i < 4; i++)
    {
        uint source = (code >> (i * 3)) & 7;
        if (source < 4)
            result[i] = value[source];
        else if (source == 4)
            result[i] = 0.0;
        else if (source == 5)
            result[i] = 1.0;
    }
    return result;
}

// MASSEFFECT: Xenos alpha test with its comparison function. Positive if the pixel passes.
float alphaTestValue(float alpha)
{
    bool pass = true;
    switch ((g_SpecConstants() & SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_FUNC_MASK) >> SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_FUNC_SHIFT)
    {
    case 0: pass = false; break;
    case 1: pass = alpha < g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 2: pass = alpha == g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 3: pass = alpha <= g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 4: pass = alpha > g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 5: pass = alpha != g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 6: pass = alpha >= g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    default: break;
    }
    return pass ? 1.0 : -1.0;
}

float4 cube(float4 value, inout CubeMapData cubeMapData)
{
    uint index = cubeMapData.cubeMapIndex;
    cubeMapData.cubeMapDirections[index] = value.xyz;
    ++cubeMapData.cubeMapIndex;
    
    return float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, index);
}

float4 dst(float4 src0, float4 src1)
{
    float4 dest;
    dest.x = 1.0;
    dest.y = src0.y * src1.y;
    dest.z = src0.z;
    dest.w = src1.w;
    return dest;
}

float4 max4(float4 src0)
{
    return max(max(src0.x, src0.y), max(src0.z, src0.w));
}

float2 getPixelCoord(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, float2 texCoord)
{
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    return getTexture2DDimensions(g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex]) * texCoord;
}

float computeMipLevel(float2 pixelCoord)
{
    float2 dx = ddx(pixelCoord);
    float2 dy = ddy(pixelCoord);
    float deltaMaxSqr = max(dot(dx, dx), dot(dy, dy));
    return max(0.0, 0.5 * log2(deltaMaxSqr));
}

#endif

#endif

#ifdef __spirv__

#ifndef s0_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s0_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(0) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 0))
#define s0_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(64) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 64))
#define s0_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(128) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 128))
#define s0_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(192) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 192))
#define s0_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(360), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(364)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 360))
#endif
#ifndef s1_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s1_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(4) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 4))
#define s1_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(68) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 68))
#define s1_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(132) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 132))
#define s1_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(196) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 196))
#define s1_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(368), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(372)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 368))
#endif
#ifndef s2_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s2_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(8) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 8))
#define s2_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(72) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 72))
#define s2_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(136) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 136))
#define s2_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(200) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 200))
#define s2_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(376), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(380)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 376))
#endif
#ifndef s3_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s3_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(12) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 12))
#define s3_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(76) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 76))
#define s3_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(140) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 140))
#define s3_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(204) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 204))
#define s3_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(384), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(388)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 384))
#endif
#ifndef s4_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s4_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(16) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 16))
#define s4_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(80) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 80))
#define s4_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(144) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 144))
#define s4_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(208) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 208))
#define s4_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(392), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(396)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 392))
#endif
#ifndef s5_SamplerDescriptorIndex

#define s31_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(608), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(612)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 608))
#endif

#else

cbuffer VertexShaderConstants : register(b0, space4)
{
};

cbuffer SharedConstants : register(b2, space4)
{
	DEFINE_SHARED_CONSTANTS();

#endif

// SDNX_FETCH_ORDER:

```

`port/shaders/internal/vs_477B525CE86A19A5.hlsl`:

```hlsl
// SDNX: replacement for the run-time Direct3D clear vertex shader 477B525CE86A19A5, from DeepSeek's
// internal-shader template (port/tools/make_internal_vs.py output, XenosRecomp interface).
// SDNX (Sleeping Dogs port): run-time vertex fetch swizzles, see fetchApply / g_FetchSwizzle.
#define SDNX_FETCH_SWIZZLE 1
#ifndef SHADER_COMMON_H_INCLUDED
#define SHADER_COMMON_H_INCLUDED

#define SPEC_CONSTANT_R11G11B10_NORMAL  (1 << 0)
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_TEST        (1 << 1)
// MASSEFFECT: constants arrive through a dynamic UBO (a constant bank on Maxwell) instead of being read
// through a 64-bit pointer. High bit so as not to clash with the UNLEASHED_RECOMP ones.
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_CONSTANTS_UBO    (1 << 8)
// The tfetch offset is scaled with 1/size taken from the shared constants instead of querying the
// texture for its size. A size query is another texture unit operation.
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_INV_TEX_SIZE    (1 << 9)
/*
 * The alpha test function, specialized (bits 16-18).
 *
 * g_AlphaFunction used to come from the shared constants, that is, at run time, so alphaTestValue
 * was a 7-case switch: ~8 branches in every pixel shader that does alpha testing. It was not needed
 * at all: the app already knows the function when it creates the pipeline (it comes from RB_COLORCONTROL, which is already in the key), so with three more bits the compiler
 * keeps the one comparison that applies and drops the other six. The output is bit-identical.
 */
// Bits 15, 19 and 23 are reserved (unused here; left free for the app's own use).
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_FUNC_SHIFT  16
#define SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_FUNC_MASK   (7 << 16)

#ifdef UNLEASHED_RECOMP
    #define SPEC_CONSTANT_BICUBIC_GI_FILTER (1 << 2)
    #define SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_TO_COVERAGE (1 << 3)
    #define SPEC_CONSTANT_REVERSE_Z         (1 << 4)
#endif

#if !defined(__cplusplus) || defined(__INTELLISENSE__)

#define FLT_MIN asfloat(0xff7fffff)
#define FLT_MAX asfloat(0x7f7fffff)

#ifdef __spirv__

struct PushConstants
{
    uint64_t VertexShaderConstants;
    uint64_t PixelShaderConstants;
    uint64_t SharedConstants;
};

[[vk::push_constant]] ConstantBuffer<PushConstants> g_PushConstants;

// MASSEFFECT: the same blocks of the upload buffer, also as dynamic UBOs in set 4. With
// -fvk-use-dx-layout each float4 takes 16 contiguous bytes, so any 4-byte word of the shared block is
// a component: v[B / 16][(B % 16) / 4], and asuint reads it without changing a bit.
struct MassEffectBlockVs { float4 v[256]; };
struct MassEffectBlockPs { float4 v[224]; };
#ifdef SDNX_FETCH_SWIZZLE
// SDNX: the block grows to 688 bytes: _InvSize of slots 16-31 reach byte 616, the vertex fetch swizzle
// codes take bytes 624-687, see g_FetchSwizzle.
struct MassEffectSharedBlock { float4 v[43]; };
#else
struct MassEffectSharedBlock { float4 v[31]; };
#endif
[[vk::binding(0, 4)]] ConstantBuffer<MassEffectBlockVs> g_UboVertex;
[[vk::binding(1, 4)]] ConstantBuffer<MassEffectBlockPs> g_UboPixel;
[[vk::binding(2, 4)]] ConstantBuffer<MassEffectSharedBlock> g_UboShared;
#define MASSEFFECT_UBO ((g_SpecConstants & SPEC_CONSTANT_CONSTANTS_UBO) != 0)
#define MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(B)  asuint(g_UboShared.v[(B) / 16][((B) % 16) / 4])
#define MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(B) g_UboShared.v[(B) / 16][((B) % 16) / 4]

#define g_Booleans                 (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(256) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 256))
#define g_SwappedTexcoords         (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(260) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 260))
#define g_HalfPixelOffset          (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(264), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(268)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 264))
#define g_AlphaThreshold           (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(272) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 272))
// MASSEFFECT: alpha test function (RB_COLORCONTROL.alpha_func): 0 never, 1 <, 2 ==, 3 <=,
// 4 >, 5 !=, 6 >=, 7 always.
#define g_AlphaFunction            (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(276) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 276))
// MASSEFFECT: position to host clip space, like ndc_scale/ndc_offset in the
// emulation (graphics/util/draw.cpp). (1, 1) and (0, 0) for normal draws;
// with Xenos clipping disabled it converts from pixels to NDC.
#define g_NdcScale                 (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(280), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(284)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 280))
#define g_NdcOffset                (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(288), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(292)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 288))
// MASSEFFECT: where each component of the vertex input at that location comes from
// (D3D patches the fetch swizzle according to the declaration). 0xFFF = as is.
#define g_InputRemap(LOC)          (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(296 + (LOC) * 4) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 296 + (LOC) * 4))
// SDNX: the 12-bit destination swizzle Direct3D wrote into vertex fetch number K of the loaded vertex
// shader (K = order of first emission, listed by the "SDNX_FETCH_ORDER" comment at the end of the HLSL),
// two per word: bits 0-11 even K, bits 16-27 odd K, at byte 624 + (K / 2) * 4 of the shared constants.
#define g_FetchSwizzle(K)          (((MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(624 + ((K) / 2) * 4) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 624 + ((K) / 2) * 4)) >> (((K) & 1) * 16)) & 0xFFF)

[[vk::constant_id(0)]] const uint g_SpecConstants = 0;

#define g_SpecConstants() g_SpecConstants

#else

#define DEFINE_SHARED_CONSTANTS() \
    uint g_Booleans : packoffset(c16.x); \
    uint g_SwappedTexcoords : packoffset(c16.y); \
    float2 g_HalfPixelOffset : packoffset(c16.z); \
    float g_AlphaThreshold : packoffset(c17.x); \
    uint g_AlphaFunction : packoffset(c17.y); \
    float2 g_NdcScale : packoffset(c17.z); \
    float2 g_NdcOffset : packoffset(c18.x);

uint g_SpecConstants();

#define g_InputRemap(LOC) 0xFFF
#define g_FetchSwizzle(K) 0x688

#endif

Texture2D<float4> g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[] : register(t0, space0);
Texture3D<float4> g_Texture3DDescriptorHeap[] : register(t0, space1);
TextureCube<float4> g_TextureCubeDescriptorHeap[] : register(t0, space2);
SamplerState g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[] : register(s0, space3);

uint2 getTexture2DDimensions(Texture2D<float4> texture)
{
    uint2 dimensions;
    texture.GetDimensions(dimensions.x, dimensions.y);
    return dimensions;
}

// The native renderer packs the four post-swizzle Xenos TextureSign values into bits 24-31 of the
// descriptor index. Values: 0 unsigned, 1 signed (the renderer still warns for it), 2 unsigned biased,
// 3 Xenos piecewise-linear gamma. Descriptor heaps use the low 24 bits.
float masseffectGammaToLinear(float gamma)
{
    gamma = saturate(gamma);
    float scale;
    float offset;
    if (gamma >= 96.0 / 255.0)
    {
        if (gamma >= 192.0 / 255.0) { scale = 8.0 / 1024.0; offset = -1024.0; }
        else                         { scale = 4.0 / 1024.0; offset = -256.0; }
    }
    else
    {
        if (gamma >= 64.0 / 255.0) { scale = 2.0 / 1024.0; offset = -64.0; }
        else                        { scale = 1.0 / 1024.0; offset = 0.0; }
    }
    float linearValue = gamma * ((255.0 * 1024.0) * scale) + offset;
    linearValue += trunc(linearValue * scale);
    return linearValue * (1.0 / 1023.0);
}

float4 masseffectApplyTextureSigns(float4 value, uint packedSigns)
{
    for (uint i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
    {
        uint sign = (packedSigns >> (i * 2)) & 3;
        if (sign == 2)
            value[i] = value[i] * 2.0 - 1.0;
        else if (sign == 3)
            value[i] = masseffectGammaToLinear(value[i]);
    }
    return value;
}

float4 tfetch2D(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float2 texCoord, float2 offset, float2 invSize)
{
    uint packedSigns = resourceDescriptorIndex >> 24;
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    Texture2D<float4> texture = g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex];
    // With the bit set, the size comes from a constant and the texture does not have to be queried. It
    // is the same computation with the same number: the renderer writes 1/size of the host image. With a
    // ternary, DXC evaluates both branches and the size query stays: an if with [branch] is needed for
    // the dead branch to disappear when the pipeline is specialized.
    float2 displacement;
    [branch] if (g_SpecConstants() & SPEC_CONSTANT_INV_TEX_SIZE)
        displacement = offset * invSize;
    else
        displacement = offset / getTexture2DDimensions(texture);
    // SDNX: vertex shaders have no derivatives - sample level 0 there (2 Sleeping Dogs vertex shaders).
#if defined(__SHADER_TARGET_STAGE) && __SHADER_TARGET_STAGE == __SHADER_STAGE_VERTEX
    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(
        texture.SampleLevel(g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex], texCoord + displacement, 0.0), packedSigns);
#else
    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(
        texture.Sample(g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex], texCoord + displacement), packedSigns);
#endif
}

float2 getWeights2D(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float2 texCoord, float2 offset)
{
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    Texture2D<float4> texture = g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex];
    return select(isnan(texCoord), 0.0, frac(texCoord * getTexture2DDimensions(texture) + offset - 0.5));
}

float w0(float a)
{
    return (1.0f / 6.0f) * (a * (a * (-a + 3.0f) - 3.0f) + 1.0f);
}

float w1(float a)
{
    return (1.0f / 6.0f) * (a * a * (3.0f * a - 6.0f) + 4.0f);
}

float w2(float a)
{
    return (1.0f / 6.0f) * (a * (a * (-3.0f * a + 3.0f) + 3.0f) + 1.0f);
}

float w3(float a)
{
    return (1.0f / 6.0f) * (a * a * a);
}

float g0(float a)
{
    return w0(a) + w1(a);
}

float g1(float a)
{
    return w2(a) + w3(a);
}

float h0(float a)
{
    return -1.0f + w1(a) / (w0(a) + w1(a)) + 0.5f;
}

float h1(float a)
{
    return 1.0f + w3(a) / (w2(a) + w3(a)) + 0.5f;
}

float4 tfetch2DBicubic(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float2 texCoord, float2 offset)
{
    uint packedSigns = resourceDescriptorIndex >> 24;
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    Texture2D<float4> texture = g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex];
    SamplerState samplerState = g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex];
    uint2 dimensions = getTexture2DDimensions(texture);
    
    float x = texCoord.x * dimensions.x + offset.x;
    float y = texCoord.y * dimensions.y + offset.y;

    x -= 0.5f;
    y -= 0.5f;
    float px = floor(x);
    float py = floor(y);
    float fx = x - px;
    float fy = y - py;

    float g0x = g0(fx);
    float g1x = g1(fx);
    float h0x = h0(fx);
    float h1x = h1(fx);
    float h0y = h0(fy);
    float h1y = h1(fy);

    float4 r =
        g0(fy) * (g0x * texture.Sample(samplerState, float2(px + h0x, py + h0y) / float2(dimensions)) +
            g1x * texture.Sample(samplerState, float2(px + h1x, py + h0y) / float2(dimensions))) +
        g1(fy) * (g0x * texture.Sample(samplerState, float2(px + h0x, py + h1y) / float2(dimensions)) +
            g1x * texture.Sample(samplerState, float2(px + h1x, py + h1y) / float2(dimensions)));

    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(r, packedSigns);
}

float4 tfetch3D(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float3 texCoord)
{
    uint packedSigns = resourceDescriptorIndex >> 24;
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(
        g_Texture3DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex].Sample(g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex], texCoord),
        packedSigns);
}

struct CubeMapData
{
    float3 cubeMapDirections[2];
    uint cubeMapIndex;
};

float4 tfetchCube(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, uint samplerDescriptorIndex, float3 texCoord, inout CubeMapData cubeMapData)
{
    uint packedSigns = resourceDescriptorIndex >> 24;
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    return masseffectApplyTextureSigns(
        g_TextureCubeDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex].Sample(g_SamplerDescriptorHeap[samplerDescriptorIndex], cubeMapData.cubeMapDirections[texCoord.z]),
        packedSigns);
}

float4 tfetchR11G11B10(uint4 value)
{
    if (g_SpecConstants() & SPEC_CONSTANT_R11G11B10_NORMAL)
    {
        return float4(
            (value.x & 0x00000400 ? -1.0 : 0.0) + ((value.x & 0x3FF) / 1024.0),
            (value.x & 0x00200000 ? -1.0 : 0.0) + (((value.x >> 11) & 0x3FF) / 1024.0),
            (value.x & 0x80000000 ? -1.0 : 0.0) + (((value.x >> 22) & 0x1FF) / 512.0),
            0.0);
    }
    else
    {
        return asfloat(value);
    }
}

float4 tfetchTexcoord(uint swappedTexcoords, float4 value, uint semanticIndex)
{
    return (swappedTexcoords & (1ull << semanticIndex)) != 0 ? value.yxwz : value;
}

// SDNX: a vertex fetch with a run-time destination swizzle (Xenos FetchDestinationSwizzle, 3 bits per
// component: 0-3 = data component, 4 = 0, 5 = 1, 6 and 7 = keep the register's component).
float4 fetchApply(float4 previous, float4 value, uint code)
{
    float4 result = previous;
    for (uint i = 0; i < 4; i++)
    {
        uint source = (code >> (i * 3)) & 7;
        if (source < 4)
            result[i] = value[source];
        else if (source == 4)
            result[i] = 0.0;
        else if (source == 5)
            result[i] = 1.0;
    }
    return result;
}

// MASSEFFECT: 3 bits per component: 0-3 = data component, 4 = 0, 5 = 1, 7 = unchanged.
float4 remapInput(float4 value, uint code)
{
    if (code == 0xFFF)
        return value;

    float4 result = value;
    for (uint i = 0; i < 4; i++)
    {
        uint source = (code >> (i * 3)) & 7;
        if (source < 4)
            result[i] = value[source];
        else if (source == 4)
            result[i] = 0.0;
        else if (source == 5)
            result[i] = 1.0;
    }
    return result;
}

// MASSEFFECT: Xenos alpha test with its comparison function. Positive if the pixel passes.
float alphaTestValue(float alpha)
{
    bool pass = true;
    switch ((g_SpecConstants() & SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_FUNC_MASK) >> SPEC_CONSTANT_ALPHA_FUNC_SHIFT)
    {
    case 0: pass = false; break;
    case 1: pass = alpha < g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 2: pass = alpha == g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 3: pass = alpha <= g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 4: pass = alpha > g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 5: pass = alpha != g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    case 6: pass = alpha >= g_AlphaThreshold; break;
    default: break;
    }
    return pass ? 1.0 : -1.0;
}

float4 cube(float4 value, inout CubeMapData cubeMapData)
{
    uint index = cubeMapData.cubeMapIndex;
    cubeMapData.cubeMapDirections[index] = value.xyz;
    ++cubeMapData.cubeMapIndex;
    
    return float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, index);
}

float4 dst(float4 src0, float4 src1)
{
    float4 dest;
    dest.x = 1.0;
    dest.y = src0.y * src1.y;
    dest.z = src0.z;
    dest.w = src1.w;
    return dest;
}

float4 max4(float4 src0)
{
    return max(max(src0.x, src0.y), max(src0.z, src0.w));
}

float2 getPixelCoord(uint resourceDescriptorIndex, float2 texCoord)
{
    resourceDescriptorIndex &= 0x00FFFFFF;
    return getTexture2DDimensions(g_Texture2DDescriptorHeap[resourceDescriptorIndex]) * texCoord;
}

float computeMipLevel(float2 pixelCoord)
{
    float2 dx = ddx(pixelCoord);
    float2 dy = ddy(pixelCoord);
    float deltaMaxSqr = max(dot(dx, dx), dot(dy, dy));
    return max(0.0, 0.5 * log2(deltaMaxSqr));
}

#endif

#endif

#ifdef __spirv__

#ifndef s0_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s0_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(0) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 0))
#define s0_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(64) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 64))
#define s0_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(128) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 128))
#define s0_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(192) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 192))
#define s0_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(360), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(364)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 360))
#endif
#ifndef s1_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s1_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(4) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 4))

#define s28_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(112) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 112))
#define s28_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(176) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 176))
#define s28_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(240) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 240))
#define s28_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(304) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 304))
#define s28_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(584), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(588)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 584))
#endif
#ifndef s29_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s29_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(116) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 116))
#define s29_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(180) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 180))
#define s29_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(244) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 244))
#define s29_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(308) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 308))
#define s29_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(592), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(596)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 592))
#endif
#ifndef s30_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s30_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(120) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 120))
#define s30_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(184) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 184))
#define s30_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(248) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 248))
#define s30_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(312) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 312))
#define s30_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(600), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(604)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 600))
#endif
#ifndef s31_SamplerDescriptorIndex
#define s31_Texture2DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(124) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 124))
#define s31_Texture3DDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(188) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 188))
#define s31_TextureCubeDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(252) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 252))
#define s31_SamplerDescriptorIndex (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? MASSEFFECT_SHARED_UINT(316) : vk::RawBufferLoad<uint>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 316))
#define s31_InvSize (MASSEFFECT_UBO ? float2(MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(608), MASSEFFECT_SHARED_FLOAT(612)) : vk::RawBufferLoad<float2>(g_PushConstants.SharedConstants + 608))
#endif

#else

cbuffer VertexShaderConstants : register(b0, space4)
{
};

cbuffer SharedConstants : register(b2, space4)
{
	DEFINE_SHARED_CONSTANTS();
};

#endif

#ifndef __spirv__
[shader("vertex")]
#endif
void main(
	[[vk::location(0)]] in float4 iPosition0 : POSITION0,
	[[vk::location(8)]] in float4 iColor0 : COLOR0,
	out float4 oPos : SV_Position,
	out float4 oTexCoord0 : TEXCOORD0,
	out float4 oTexCoord1 : TEXCOORD1,
	out float4 oTexCoord2 : TEXCOORD2,
	out float4 oTexCoord3 : TEXCOORD3,
	out float4 oTexCoord4 : TEXCOORD4,
	out float4 oTexCoord5 : TEXCOORD5,
	out float4 oTexCoord6 : TEXCOORD6,
	out float4 oTexCoord7 : TEXCOORD7,
	out float4 oTexCoord8 : TEXCOORD8,
	out float4 oTexCoord9 : TEXCOORD9,
	out float4 oTexCoord10 : TEXCOORD10,
	out float4 oTexCoord11 : TEXCOORD11,
	out float4 oTexCoord12 : TEXCOORD12,
	out float4 oTexCoord13 : TEXCOORD13,
	out float4 oTexCoord14 : TEXCOORD14,
	out float4 oTexCoord15 : TEXCOORD15,
	out float4 oColor0 : COLOR0,
	out float4 oColor1 : COLOR1,
	out float4 oColor2 : COLOR2,
	out float4 oColor3 : COLOR3,
	out float4 oColor4 : COLOR4,
	out float4 oColor5 : COLOR5)
{
	oTexCoord0 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord1 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord2 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord3 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord4 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord5 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord6 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord7 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord8 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord9 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord10 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord11 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord12 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord13 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord14 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oTexCoord15 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oColor0 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oColor1 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oColor2 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oColor3 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oColor4 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
	oColor5 = float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);

	float4 r0 = 0.0;
	float4 r1 = 0.0;
	float4 r2 = 0.0;
	float4 r3 = 0.0;
	float4 r4 = 0.0;
	float4 r5 = 0.0;
	float4 r6 = 0.0;
	float4 r7 = 0.0;
	float4 r8 = 0.0;
	float4 r9 = 0.0;
	float4 r10 = 0.0;
	float4 r11 = 0.0;
	float4 r12 = 0.0;
	float4 r13 = 0.0;
	float4 r14 = 0.0;
	float4 r15 = 0.0;
	float4 r16 = 0.0;
	float4 r17 = 0.0;
	float4 r18 = 0.0;
	float4 r19 = 0.0;
	float4 r20 = 0.0;
	float4 r21 = 0.0;
	float4 r22 = 0.0;
	float4 r23 = 0.0;
	float4 r24 = 0.0;
	float4 r25 = 0.0;
	float4 r26 = 0.0;
	float4 r27 = 0.0;
	float4 r28 = 0.0;
	float4 r29 = 0.0;
	float4 r30 = 0.0;
	float4 r31 = 0.0;
	int a0 = 0;
	int aL = 0;
	bool p0 = false;
	float ps = 0.0;

	// SDNX: hand-written body of the run-time Direct3D vertex shader 477B525CE86A19A5 (27 words; the rectangle
	// list clears, e.g. the stencil clears before the UI masks): vfetch r1 = position (k_32_32_32_FLOAT, swizzle
	// xyz1), vfetch r0 = colour (k_32_32_32_32_FLOAT at +12 bytes), export oPos = r1, interpolator 0 = r0.
	r1 = fetchApply(r1, float4(iPosition0), g_FetchSwizzle(0));
	r0 = fetchApply(r0, float4(iColor0), g_FetchSwizzle(1));
	oTexCoord0 = r0;
	oPos = r1;
	oPos.xy = oPos.xy * g_NdcScale + g_NdcOffset * oPos.w;
	oPos.xy += g_HalfPixelOffset * oPos.w;
	return;
}
// SDNX_FETCH_ORDER:
```
