`port/tools/aba_scan.py`:

```py
import re, glob, sys, collections
files = glob.glob(sys.argv[1] + '/sdretail_recomp.*.cpp')
func_re = re.compile(r'^DEFINE_REX_FUNC\((sub_[0-9A-F]+)\)')
hits = collections.OrderedDict()
total = 0
for fn in files:
    lines = open(fn, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n')
    func = None
    i = 0
    while i < len(lines):
        m = func_re.match(lines[i])
        if m: func = m.group(1)
        if 'reserved.u32 = *(uint32_t*)' in lines[i] or 'reserved.u64 = *(uint64_t*)' in lines[i]:
            total += 1
            # destination register of the lwarx (next line: X.u64 = __builtin_bswapNN(reserved...))
            m2 = re.match(r'\s*((?:ctx\.)?r\d+)\.u64 = __builtin_bswap', lines[i + 1]) if i + 1 < len(lines) else None
            dst = m2.group(1) if m2 else None
            j = i + 2
            dep_load = False
            while j < len(lines) and j < i + 60 and 'compare_and_swap' not in lines[j]:
                if dst and re.search(r'REX_LOAD\w*\(\s*' + re.escape(dst) + r'\.u32', lines[j]):
                    dep_load = True
                j += 1
            if dep_load:
                hits.setdefault(func, 0)
                hits[func] += 1
        i += 1
print('lwarx total', total, 'with a load through the reserved value before stwcx:', sum(hits.values()), 'in', len(hits), 'functions')
for f, n in list(hits.items())[:40]: print(f, n)

```

`port/tools/apply_branding.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""SDNX branding patch: "Nintendo Switch" text and Switch button glyphs in the Sleeping Dogs X360 data.

Works on the user's own extracted game data (the game_root folder). Changes:
  * localisation tables (all 7 languages): the strings listed in port/branding/switch_text.json;
  * UI textures BUTTON_* (perm 0x14B0D2FB + temp 0xFBEDD2FF): Switch-style glyphs from make_switch_buttons.py;
  * Settings > Display movie (0xEBCA65F0): an "FPS LIMITER" row 30 / 60 / OFF (fps_menu_patch.py), read by the NRO.

How an archive is patched: each changed entry is written uncompressed at the end of NAME.big (4 KB aligned) and
its NAME.bix record is pointed there (compressed_size = 0, as the game's own stored entries). The original bytes
stay in place, so --restore only truncates the .big and puts back the saved .bix.

Usage:
  apply_branding.py GAME_ROOT [--by-letter] [--no-text] [--no-buttons] [--no-fps-menu]
  apply_branding.py GAME_ROOT --restore
  apply_branding.py GAME_ROOT --verify OUT_DIR      (decode patched glyphs + changed strings for review)

Face buttons are labelled by position by default (Xbox A = bottom = Switch B, B -> A, X -> Y, Y -> X), matching
the NRO's default input mapping (input_xbox_layout = true). --by-letter: labels A/B/X/Y as on the Xbox, for
input_xbox_layout = false.
"""
import json
import os
import shutil
import struct
import sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)

import numpy as np  # noqa: E402

import fps_menu_patch  # noqa: E402
import sdbig  # noqa: E402
import sdtex  # noqa: E402
import sdtext  # noqa: E402
import xtex  # noqa: E402

ARCHIVES = ["UI", "SeekFreeBoot"]
BUTTONS_PERM = 0x14B0D2FB
BUTTONS_TEMP = 0xFBEDD2FF
TEXT_JSON = os.path.join(HERE, "..", "branding", "switch_text.json")
STATE_SUFFIX = ".sdnx_branding.json"
ALIGN = 0x1000


def load_text_table():
    raw = json.load(open(TEXT_JSON, encoding="utf-8"))
    out = {}
    for k, v in raw.items():
        if k.startswith("_"):
            continue
        out[int(k, 16)] = {lang: s.encode("utf-8") for lang, s in v.items() if not lang.startswith("_")}
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- state / restore

def state_path(bi):
    return bi.base + STATE_SUFFIX


def restore(base, quiet=False):
    bi_base = base
    st = bi_base + STATE_SUFFIX
    if not os.path.exists(st):
        if not quiet:
            print("  %s: not patched" % os.path.basename(base))
        return False
    s = json.load(open(st))
    with open(bi_base + ".big", "r+b") as f:
        f.truncate(s["big_size"])
    shutil.copyfile(bi_base + ".bix.orig", bi_base + ".bix")
    os.remove(bi_base + ".bix.orig")
    os.remove(st)
    print("  %s: restored (big %d bytes)" % (os.path.basename(base), s["big_size"]))
    return True


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- patchers

def patch_text(data: bytes, table) -> (bytes, list):
    """Rebuild every localisation chunk of an entry that contains one of the listed hashes."""
    chunks = list(sdtext.iter_text_chunks(data))
    if not chunks:
        return None, []
    out = bytearray()
    last = 0
    changed = []
    for p, ln in chunks:
        t = sdtext.TextTable(data[p:p + ln])
        lang = t.name.split("_", 1)[0]
        hit = False
        for ent in t.entries:
            rep = table.get(ent[0], {}).get(lang)
            if rep is not None and ent[1] != rep:
                changed.append((t.name, ent[0]))
                ent[1] = rep
                hit = True
        out += data[last:p]
        out += t.build() if hit else data[p:p + ln]
        last = p + ln
    out += data[last:]
    return (bytes(out) if changed else None), changed


def patch_buttons(perm: bytes, temp: bytes, glyphs) -> (bytes, list):
    temp = bytearray(temp)
    done = []
    for t in sdtex.iter_textures(perm):
        img = glyphs.get(t["name"])
        if img is None:
            continue
        if sdtex.FORMATS.get(t["fmt"]) != "DXT5" or img.shape[1] != t["w"] or img.shape[0] != t["h"]:
            raise SystemExit("unexpected texture %s: fmt %d %dx%d" % (t["name"], t["fmt"], t["w"], t["h"]))
        wb, hb = t["w"] // 4, t["h"] // 4
        orig = xtex.swap16(bytes(temp[t["pos"]:t["pos"] + t["size"]]))
        lin = xtex.encode_dxt5(img)
        tiled = xtex.tile_blocks(lin, wb, hb, 16, t["size"], base=orig)
        temp[t["pos"]:t["pos"] + t["size"]] = xtex.swap16(tiled)
        done.append(t["name"])
    return bytes(temp), done


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- archive writer

def append_entries(bi: sdbig.BigIndex, new_data: dict):
    with open(bi.big_path, "r+b") as f:
        f.seek(0, 2)
        end = f.tell()
        for uid, data in sorted(new_data.items()):
            pos = (end + ALIGN - 1) & ~(ALIGN - 1)
            f.seek(end)
            f.write(b"\0" * (pos - end))
            f.write(data)
            end = pos + len(data)
            e = bi.by_uid[uid]
            e.off4, e.load_offset, e.csize, e.cextra, e.usize = pos // 4, 0, 0, 0, len(data)
            bi.write_entry(e)
    bi.save_bix()


def patch_archive(base, table, glyphs, fps_menu=True):
    restore(base, quiet=True)
    bi = sdbig.BigIndex(base)
    big_size = os.path.getsize(bi.big_path)
    new_data = {}
    text_changes = []
    with open(bi.big_path, "rb") as f:
        if table:
            for e in bi.entries:
                d = bi.read(e, f)
                nd, ch = patch_text(d, table)
                if nd is not None:
                    new_data[e.uid] = nd
                    text_changes += ch
        btn = []
        if glyphs and BUTTONS_PERM in bi.by_uid and BUTTONS_TEMP in bi.by_uid:
            perm = bi.read(bi.by_uid[BUTTONS_PERM], f)
            temp = bi.read(bi.by_uid[BUTTONS_TEMP], f)
            nt, btn = patch_buttons(perm, temp, glyphs)
            new_data[BUTTONS_TEMP] = nt
        fps = False
        if fps_menu and fps_menu_patch.ENTRY_UID in bi.by_uid:
            d = bi.read(bi.by_uid[fps_menu_patch.ENTRY_UID], f)
            if not fps_menu_patch.is_patched(d):
                new_data[fps_menu_patch.ENTRY_UID] = fps_menu_patch.patch_entry(d)
                fps = True
    if not new_data:
        print("  %s: nothing to change" % os.path.basename(base))
        return
    shutil.copyfile(bi.bix_path, bi.bix_path + ".orig")
    json.dump({"big_size": big_size, "entries": ["%08X" % u for u in sorted(new_data)]},
              open(state_path(bi), "w"), indent=1)
    append_entries(bi, new_data)
    langs = sorted({n.split("_", 1)[0] for n, _ in text_changes})
    print("  %s: %d strings (%s), %d glyphs, fps menu %s, %d entries rewritten, +%d KB" % (
        os.path.basename(base), len(text_changes), ", ".join(langs), len(btn), "yes" if fps else "no", len(new_data),
        (os.path.getsize(bi.big_path) - big_size) // 1024))


def verify(root, out):
    from PIL import Image
    os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
    table = load_text_table()
    for arc in ARCHIVES:
        base = os.path.join(root, arc)
        if not os.path.exists(base + ".bix"):
            continue
        bi = sdbig.BigIndex(base)
        with open(bi.big_path, "rb") as f, open(os.path.join(out, arc + "_strings.txt"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as tf:
            for e in bi.entries:
                d = bi.read(e, f)
                for p, ln in sdtext.iter_text_chunks(d):
                    t = sdtext.TextTable(d[p:p + ln])
                    for h, s in t.entries:
                        if h in table or b"xbox" in s.lower():
                            tf.write("%s %08X %s\n" % (t.name, h, s.decode("utf-8", "replace")))
            if BUTTONS_PERM in bi.by_uid:
                perm = bi.read(bi.by_uid[BUTTONS_PERM], f)
                temp = bi.read(bi.by_uid[BUTTONS_TEMP], f)
                tiles = []
                for t in sdtex.iter_textures(perm):
                    if not t["name"].startswith("BUTTON"):
                        continue
                    raw = xtex.swap16(temp[t["pos"]:t["pos"] + t["size"]])
                    lin = xtex.untile_blocks(raw, t["w"] // 4, t["h"] // 4, 16)
                    tiles.append(xtex.decode_dxt(lin, t["w"], t["h"], "DXT5"))
                cell, cols = 136, 8
                rows = (len(tiles) + cols - 1) // cols
                sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (cols * cell, rows * cell), (90, 90, 90, 255))
                for i, im in enumerate(tiles):
                    sheet.alpha_composite(Image.fromarray(im, "RGBA"), ((i % cols) * cell + 4, (i // cols) * cell + 4))
                sheet.save(os.path.join(out, arc + "_buttons.png"))
    print("verify output in", out)


def main(argv):
    if len(argv) < 2:
        print(__doc__)
        return 1
    root = argv[1]
    if "--restore" in argv:
        for arc in ARCHIVES:
            if os.path.exists(os.path.join(root, arc + ".bix")):
                restore(os.path.join(root, arc))
        return 0
    if "--verify" in argv:
        verify(root, argv[argv.index("--verify") + 1])
        return 0
    table = None if "--no-text" in argv else load_text_table()
    glyphs = None
    if "--no-buttons" not in argv:
        import make_switch_buttons
        glyphs = make_switch_buttons.build("--by-letter" in argv)
    for arc in ARCHIVES:
        base = os.path.join(root, arc)
        if os.path.exists(base + ".bix"):
            patch_archive(base, table, glyphs, "--no-fps-menu" not in argv)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

```

`port/tools/check_text_roundtrip.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rebuild every localisation table of the given archives and compare byte for byte with the original."""
import os
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import sdbig  # noqa: E402
import sdtext  # noqa: E402

ASSETS = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "assets")

for arc in sys.argv[1:] or ["UI", "SeekFreeBoot", "Global"]:
    bi = sdbig.BigIndex(os.path.join(ASSETS, arc))
    ok = bad = 0
    names = set()
    with open(bi.big_path, "rb") as f:
        for e in bi.entries:
            d = bi.read(e, f)
            for p, ln in sdtext.iter_text_chunks(d):
                t = sdtext.TextTable(d[p:p + ln])
                names.add(t.name.split("_", 1)[0])
                if t.build() == d[p:p + ln]:
                    ok += 1
                else:
                    bad += 1
                    print("BAD", arc, "%08X" % e.uid, t.name, ln, len(t.build()))
    print(arc, "ok", ok, "bad", bad, "langs", sorted(names))

```

`port/tools/compile_internal_hlsl.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Compiles a hand-written internal-shader HLSL (port/shaders/internal/<name>.hlsl) with the shader library flags,
# validates it and copies the SPIR-V into the desktop package. Usage: compile_internal_hlsl.sh <name> <vs|ps>
set -eu
name=$1; kind=$2
src=/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/port/shaders/internal
out=${PKG_OUT:-/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/nx/desktop/out/sdnx_shaders}
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="$HOME/tools/dxc/lib"
profile=vs_6_6; extra=-fvk-invert-y
[ "$kind" = ps ] && { profile=ps_6_6; extra=; }
"$HOME/tools/dxc/bin/dxc" -spirv -T $profile -E main -HV 2021 -fspv-target-env=vulkan1.2 -fvk-use-dx-layout \
  -Werror=parameter-usage $extra -Fo "$src/$name.spv" "$src/$name.hlsl"
"$HOME/tools/spvtools/tools/spirv-val" --target-env vulkan1.2 --scalar-block-layout "$src/$name.spv"
cp "$src/$name.spv" "$out/$name.spv"
echo "ok: $out/$name.spv"
```

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Compile the hand-built pixel-shader container's HLSL with the library flags, then validate.
set -u
export DXC="$HOME/tools/dxc/bin/dxc"
export VAL="$HOME/tools/spvtools/tools/spirv-val"
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="$HOME/tools/dxc/lib"
cd "$HOME/sd-intps/out" || exit 1
rm -f ps_internal.spv ps_internal.err
"$DXC" -spirv -T ps_6_6 -E main -HV 2021 -fspv-target-env=vulkan1.2 -fvk-use-dx-layout \
  -Werror=parameter-usage -Fo ps_internal.spv ps_internal.hlsl 2>ps_internal.err
rc=$?
echo "dxc rc=$rc"
[ -s ps_internal.err ] && head -5 ps_internal.err
ls -l ps_internal.spv 2>/dev/null || echo NO_SPV
"$VAL" --target-env vulkan1.2 --scalar-block-layout ps_internal.spv && echo VALID
```

`port/tools/debug_composite.py`:

```py
# Debug variant of the final composite pixel shader ps_195084cd918f58f5 (native renderer gameplay frame is near
# black). Writes port/shots/ps_195084cd918f58f5_debug.hlsl: the screen is split into a 3x2 grid, each cell shows an
# intermediate value instead of the result:
#   top:    pre-tonemap colour c | lit colour (r4) | fog rgb
#   bottom: fog alpha            | diffuse texture | ambient texture
# mode 1 (argument 1): collector rgb | collector alpha | ambient2 rgb / ambient2 alpha | diffuse2 | cbEnvironmentSettings(3)
# mode 2: ambient decode r12.x | collector.z | r4 after r12*r7 / r5.yzw | r10.xyz | r11.xyz
# mode 3: diffuse2 everywhere; mode 4: diffuse2 x1, /32, /1024 / ambient | lit | colour
# mode 5: screen position gradient (checks that the debug shader is what runs)
# mode 10 + m: the cells of mode m as six full-width horizontal bands of 118 rows (vertical seams per value)
# Usage: python debug_composite.py  (then compile it in WSL and swap the .spv in the package, see portlog 16.28)
import re

src = r'D:\CODE\SDNX\port\shots\ps_195084cd918f58f5.hlsl'
import sys
MODE = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0
dst = r'D:\CODE\SDNX\port\shots\ps_195084cd918f58f5_debug%d.hlsl' % MODE
s = open(src, encoding='utf-8').read()


def after(pattern, text):
    global s
    m = re.search(pattern, s)
    assert m, pattern
    line_end = s.index('\n', m.end())
    s = s[:line_end + 1] + '\t' + text + '\n' + s[line_end + 1:]


after(r'r8\.xyz = tfetch2D\(_texDiffuse_Texture2D', 'float3 dDiffuse = r8.xyz;')
after(r'r7\.w = tfetch2D\(_texCollector_Texture2D', 'float4 dCollector = tfetch2D(_texCollector_Texture2DDescriptorIndex, _texCollector_SamplerDescriptorIndex, r4.xy, float2(0, 0), _texCollector_InvSize);')
after(r'r10\.xyzw = tfetch2D\(_texAmbient2_Texture2D', 'float4 dAmbient2 = r10;')
after(r'r11\.xyz = tfetch2D\(_texDiffuse2_Texture2D', 'float3 dDiffuse2 = r11.xyz;')
after(r'r5\.xyz = tfetch2D\(_texAmbient_Texture2D', 'float3 dAmbient = r5.xyz;')
after(r'r1\.xyzw = tfetch3D\(_texFogCube', 'float4 dFog = r1;')
after(r'r0\.xyz = r4\.xyz \* r0\.www \+ r2\.xyz;', 'float3 dLit = r4.xyz;')
after(r'r1\.xyz = r4\.xyz \* r0\.www \+ r1\.xyz;', 'float3 dColour = r1.xyz;')
after(r'r12\.x = r8\.w \* r2\.w;', 'float dAmbDecode = r12.x;')
after(r'r4\.xyzw = r12\.xyzw \* r7\.wyww;', 'float3 dR4 = r4.xyz; float dCollZ = r7.w;')
after(r'r5\.yzw = r10\.xyz \* r9\.xyz \+ r11\.xyz;', 'float3 dR5 = r5.yzw; float3 dR10 = r10.xyz; float3 dR11 = r11.xyz;')
final = 'oC0.xyz = r1.xyz * r0.xyz;'
assert s.count(final) == 1
s = s.replace(final, final + '''
	{
		uint cx = uint(iPos.x) / 400u, cy = uint(iPos.y) / 352u;
		uint cell = min(cx, 2u) + 3u * min(cy, 1u);
		if (%d >= 10) cell = min(uint(iPos.y) / 118u, 5u);  // bands: every value spans the full width
		float3 d;
		if (%d == 5) d = float3(iPos.x / 1200.0, iPos.y / 704.0, 0.0);
		else if (%d == 3) d = dDiffuse2;
		else if (%d == 4) d = cell == 0u ? dDiffuse2 : cell == 1u ? dDiffuse2 / 32.0 : cell == 2u ? dDiffuse2 / 1024.0 :
		                   cell == 3u ? dAmbient : cell == 4u ? dLit : dColour;
		else if (%d == 0) d = cell == 0u ? dColour : cell == 1u ? dLit : cell == 2u ? dFog.rgb :
		                   cell == 3u ? dFog.aaa : cell == 4u ? dDiffuse : dAmbient;
		else if (%d == 2) d = cell == 0u ? dAmbDecode.xxx : cell == 1u ? dCollZ.xxx : cell == 2u ? dR4 :
		                        cell == 3u ? dR5 : cell == 4u ? dR10 : dR11;
		else d = cell == 0u ? dCollector.rgb : cell == 1u ? dCollector.aaa : cell == 2u ? dAmbient2.rgb :
		         cell == 3u ? dAmbient2.aaa : cell == 4u ? dDiffuse2 : cbEnvironmentSettings(3).xyz;
		oC0 = float4(d, 1.0);
	}''' % ((MODE,) + (MODE % 10,) * 5))
open(dst, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write(s)
print('ok', dst)

```

```ps1
#   debug_composite_run.ps1 -Mode 4 [-Addr 0xfeef000]  -> port/shots/dbg<mode>.png
```

`port/tools/extract_iso.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Minimal XDVDFS extractor (Xbox 360 XGD2/XGD3 ISO), linear-scan directory tables.

Usage:
  python extract_iso.py <iso> list
  python extract_iso.py <iso> extract <outdir> [--only NAME ...]
"""
import os
import struct
import sys

SECTOR = 2048
MAGIC = b"MICROSOFT*XBOX*MEDIA"


def find_partition_base(fh, limit=128 * 1024 * 1024):
    fh.seek(0)
    off = 0
    chunk = 1 << 20
    while off < limit:
        data = fh.read(chunk)
        if not data:
            break
        i = data.find(MAGIC)
        if i >= 0:
            return off + i - 32 * SECTOR
        off += len(data)
    raise SystemExit("XDVDFS volume not found")


def read_at(fh, off, size):
    fh.seek(off)
    return fh.read(size)


def parse_table(buf, table_sector):
    """Linear scan of one directory table. Returns [(name, sector, size, attrs)]."""
    out = []
    off = 0
    n = len(buf)
    while off + 14 <= n:
        left, right, sector, size, attr, name_len = struct.unpack_from("<HHIIBB", buf, off)
        if name_len == 0 or name_len > 255 or off + 14 + name_len > n:
            off += 4
            continue
        raw = buf[off + 14: off + 14 + name_len]
        if any(c < 0x20 for c in raw) or b"/" in raw:
            off += 4
            continue
        name = raw.decode("latin-1")
        out.append((name, sector, size, attr))
        off += 14 + name_len
        off += (4 - (off % 4)) % 4
        _ = (left, right, table_sector)
    return out


def walk(fh, base, sector, size, prefix, acc, depth=0):
    if depth > 16 or size == 0:
        return
    span = max(size, SECTOR)
    buf = read_at(fh, base + sector * SECTOR, span)
    if not buf:
        return
    for name, fsector, fsize, attr in parse_table(buf, sector):
        if name in (".", ".."):
            continue
        path = f"{prefix}/{name}"
        if attr & 0x10:
            acc.append((path, fsector, fsize, True))
            walk(fh, base, fsector, fsize, path, acc, depth + 1)
        else:
            acc.append((path, fsector, fsize, False))


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) < 3:
        raise SystemExit(__doc__)
    iso, cmd = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    fh = open(iso, "rb", buffering=0)
    base = find_partition_base(fh)
    vd = read_at(fh, base + 32 * SECTOR, SECTOR)
    root_sector, root_size = struct.unpack_from("<II", vd, 20)
    entries = []
    walk(fh, base, root_sector, root_size, "", entries)

    if cmd == "list":
        for path, sector, size, is_dir in entries:
            print(f"{'d' if is_dir else '-'} {size:>13} {path}")
        print(f"\n{len(entries)} entries, partition base 0x{base:X}")
        return

    if cmd != "extract":
        raise SystemExit("unknown command")
    outdir = sys.argv[3]
    only = set()
    if "--only" in sys.argv:
        i = sys.argv.index("--only")
        only = set(sys.argv[i + 1:])
    files = [(p, s, z) for p, s, z, d in entries if not d]
    total = sum(z for _, _, z in files)
    print(f"partition base 0x{base:X}; {len(files)} files, {total/1e9:.2f} GB")
    done = 0
    for path, sector, size in files:
        rel = path.lstrip("/")
        if only and rel not in only:
            continue
        dest = os.path.join(outdir, *rel.split("/"))
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest), exist_ok=True)
        with open(dest, "wb") as out:
            remaining = size
            pos = base + sector * SECTOR
            while remaining > 0:
                chunk = read_at(fh, pos, min(8 << 20, remaining))
                if not chunk:
                    raise SystemExit(f"short read on {path}")
                out.write(chunk)
                pos += len(chunk)
                remaining -= len(chunk)
        done += size
        print(f"  {rel} ({size/1e6:.1f} MB)  [{done/1e9:.2f}/{total/1e9:.2f} GB]", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`port/tools/fps_bench.ps1`:

```ps1
# FPS of the desktop bench without RenderDoc: boots into the first gameplay scene (START presses), logs flips/s
# every 5 s (sdretail_fps_log). Extra command line through -Extra. -Sample: every 5 s after 50 s also prints the
# process 3D GPU engine load and the busiest threads (% of one core).
param([int]$Total = 170, [int]$StartKeyAt = 40, [string[]]$Extra = @(), [string]$Log = "D:\CODE\SDNX\port\fps_bench.log",
      [switch]$Sample, [string]$LogLevel = "info", [int]$Presses = 8, [string[]]$Keys = @(), [switch]$Post,
      [string]$Record = "", [string]$Replay = "")
# -Record keys.txt: the presses of the person at the PC (keyboard while the game window is in front, and XInput pad
#   buttons translated to the bound keys) are written as "<second> <vk hex> down|up"; no automatic START presses.
# -Replay keys.txt: those presses are posted to the game window at the same seconds (implies -Post, no START presses).
if ($Record -or $Replay) { $Presses = 0 }
if ($Replay) { $Post = $true }
# -Post: keys go to the game window with PostMessage (no focus change: works while the PC is in use).
# -Keys "60:0x28","62:0x26": extra key presses at second:virtual-key (arrows are bound to the D-pad).
Add-Type @"
using System; using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class K5 {
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern void keybd_event(byte vk, byte scan, uint flags, UIntPtr extra);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr h);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool PostMessage(IntPtr h, uint msg, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint MapVirtualKey(uint code, uint type);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern short GetAsyncKeyState(int vk);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct XS { public uint packet; public ushort buttons; public byte lt, rt; public short lx, ly, rx, ry; }
  [DllImport("xinput1_4.dll")] public static extern uint XInputGetState(uint user, out XS state);
}
"@
$wd = "D:\CODE\SDNX\nx\desktop\out"
Remove-Item "$Log*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
$args2 = @("--game_data_root", "D:\CODE\SDNX\port\testroot", "--log_file", $Log, "--log_level", $LogLevel, "--mnk_mode",
  "--keybind_start", "Return", "--keybind_a", "Space", "--keybind_b", "Escape", "--keybind_dpad_up", "Up",
  "--keybind_dpad_down", "Down", "--keybind_dpad_left", "Left", "--keybind_dpad_right", "Right", "--sdretail_fps_log", "true") + $Extra
$p = Start-Process -FilePath "$wd\sdretail.exe" -WorkingDirectory $wd -PassThru -ArgumentList $args2
function Post-Key([byte]$vk, [bool]$down) {
  $p.Refresh(); if ($p.MainWindowHandle -eq 0) { return }
  $sc = [K5]::MapVirtualKey($vk, 0); $ext = if ($vk -ge 0x25 -and $vk -le 0x28) { 0x01000000 } else { 0 }
  if ($down) { [K5]::PostMessage($p.MainWindowHandle, 0x100, [IntPtr][int]$vk, [IntPtr](1 -bor ($sc -shl 16) -bor $ext)) | Out-Null }
  else { [K5]::PostMessage($p.MainWindowHandle, 0x101, [IntPtr][int]$vk, [IntPtr]([int64]0xC0000001 -bor ($sc -shl 16) -bor $ext)) | Out-Null }
}
# Pad button -> bound key (see the --keybind_* arguments): dpad, START, A, B.
$padMap = @{ 1 = 0x26; 2 = 0x28; 4 = 0x25; 8 = 0x27; 16 = 0x0D; 4096 = 0x20; 8192 = 0x1B }  # plain hashtable: [ordered][int] is positional
$inv = [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture
$recDown = @{}; $padPrev = 0; $recLines = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
$replayEv = @(); $replayAt = 0
if ($Replay) { $replayEv = @(Get-Content $Replay | Where-Object { $_ -match "^\d" } | ForEach-Object { $a = $_ -split " "; ,@([double]::Parse($a[0], [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture), [Convert]::ToInt32($a[1], 16), ($a[2] -eq "down")) }) }
function Send-Key([byte]$vk) {
  $p.Refresh()
  if ($Post) {
    if ($p.MainWindowHandle -eq 0) { return }
    $sc = [K5]::MapVirtualKey($vk, 0); $ext = if ($vk -ge 0x25 -and $vk -le 0x28) { 0x01000000 } else { 0 }
    [K5]::PostMessage($p.MainWindowHandle, 0x100, [IntPtr][int]$vk, [IntPtr](1 -bor ($sc -shl 16) -bor $ext)) | Out-Null
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 80
    [K5]::PostMessage($p.MainWindowHandle, 0x101, [IntPtr][int]$vk, [IntPtr]([int64]0xC0000001 -bor ($sc -shl 16) -bor $ext)) | Out-Null
  } else {
    if ($p.MainWindowHandle -ne 0) { [K5]::SetForegroundWindow($p.MainWindowHandle) | Out-Null }
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 150
    [K5]::keybd_event($vk, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 80; [K5]::keybd_event($vk, 0, 2, [UIntPtr]::Zero)
  }
}
$start = Get-Date; $presses = 0; $lastSample = 0; $prev = $null; $keyDone = @{}
while (((Get-Date) - $start).TotalSeconds -lt $Total -and -not $p.HasExited) {
  $t = ((Get-Date) - $start).TotalSeconds
  if ($t -ge ($StartKeyAt + 4 * $presses) -and $presses -lt $Presses) {
    Send-Key 0x0D
    $presses++
  }
  for ($i = 0; $i -lt $Keys.Count; $i++) {
    $kt, $kv = $Keys[$i] -split ':'
    if (-not $keyDone[$i] -and $t -ge [double]$kt) {
      $keyDone[$i] = $true
      Send-Key ([byte][Convert]::ToInt32($kv, 16))
    }
  }
  if ($Record) {
    $p.Refresh(); $front = $p.MainWindowHandle -ne 0 -and [K5]::GetForegroundWindow() -eq $p.MainWindowHandle
    for ($vk = 8; $vk -lt 255; $vk++) {
      $d = $front -and (([K5]::GetAsyncKeyState($vk) -band 0x8000) -ne 0)
      if ($d -ne [bool]$recDown[$vk]) { $recDown[$vk] = $d; $recLines.Add([string]::Format($inv, "{0:F2} {1:X2} {2}", $t, $vk, $(if ($d) { "down" } else { "up" }))) }
    }
    $xs = New-Object K5+XS
    $pad = if ([K5]::XInputGetState(0, [ref]$xs) -eq 0) { [int]$xs.buttons } else { 0 }
    foreach ($b in $padMap.Keys) {
      if (($pad -band $b) -ne ($padPrev -band $b)) { $recLines.Add([string]::Format($inv, "{0:F2} {1:X2} {2}", $t, [int]$padMap[[int]$b], $(if ($pad -band $b) { "down" } else { "up" }))) }
    }
    $padPrev = $pad
  }
  while ($replayAt -lt $replayEv.Count -and $t -ge $replayEv[$replayAt][0]) {
    Post-Key ([byte]$replayEv[$replayAt][1]) $replayEv[$replayAt][2]; $replayAt++
  }
  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds $(if ($Record -or $Replay) { 15 } else { 250 })
  if ($Sample -and $t -ge 50 -and ($t - $lastSample) -ge 5) {
    $p.Refresh()
    $now = @{}
    foreach ($th in $p.Threads) { try { $now[$th.Id] = $th.TotalProcessorTime.TotalMilliseconds } catch {} }
    if ($prev) {
      $dt = ($t - $lastSample) * 1000
      $busy = foreach ($k in $now.Keys) { if ($prev.ContainsKey($k)) { [math]::Round(100 * ($now[$k] - $prev[$k]) / $dt) } }
      $gpu = 0
      try {
        $gpu = ((Get-Counter "\GPU Engine(pid_$($p.Id)*engtype_3D)\Utilization Percentage" -ErrorAction Stop).CounterSamples |
          Measure-Object CookedValue -Sum).Sum
      } catch {}
      "t={0,4:N0}s gpu3d={1,5:N1}% threads%: {2}" -f $t, $gpu, (($busy | Sort-Object -Descending | Select-Object -First 5) -join ' ')
    }
    $prev = $now; $lastSample = $t
  }
}
if (-not $p.HasExited) { Stop-Process -Id $p.Id -Force }
if ($Record) { $recLines | Set-Content -Encoding ascii $Record; "recorded $($recLines.Count) key events -> $Record" }
if ($Replay) { "replayed $replayAt of $($replayEv.Count) key events" }
$fl = @(Select-String -Path "$Log*" -Pattern "flips/s" | ForEach-Object { [double](($_.Line -split '\] ')[-1] -split ' ')[0] })
$dr = @(Select-String -Path "$Log*" -Pattern "sdnx_draws" | ForEach-Object { [double](($_.Line -split '5 s: ')[-1] -split ' ')[0] })
$k = [math]::Min(12, [math]::Min($fl.Count, [math]::Max($dr.Count, 1)))
$fl = $fl | Select-Object -Last $k
if ($dr.Count) {
  $dr = $dr | Select-Object -Last $k
  $fps = ($fl | Measure-Object -Average).Average; $dpf = (($dr | Measure-Object -Average).Average / 5) / $fps
  "fps avg {0:N1} (min {1:N1} max {2:N1}), draws/frame {3:N0}, draws/s {4:N0}" -f $fps, ($fl | Measure-Object -Minimum).Minimum, ($fl | Measure-Object -Maximum).Maximum, $dpf, ($dpf * $fps)
} else { "fps last: " + ($fl -join ' ') }

```

`port/tools/fps_menu_patch.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Add an "FPS LIMITER" row to the Display options screen (Scaleform movie Options_Display, UI.big 0xEBCA65F0).

The row uses the list's third slot, which in this movie is the PC resolution slot (arrows + a caption list):
captions "30", "60", "$COMMON_OFF_UPPERCASE" (the game translates it: OFF / ОТКЛ.), label "$OPTIONS_FPS_LIMITER" (FPS LIMITER / ОГРАНИЧЕНИЕ FPS — a PC string that is still
in all 7 languages). Its id is OPTIONS_FPS_LIMITER, so left/right sends FSCommand "OPTIONS_FPS_LIMITER" with the
index 0/1/2 to the screen's handleMessage (sub_827ECC70), where nx/sdretail/src/sdnx_fps_limit.cpp picks it up.
The NRO sets the shown value on open by calling SDNX_SetFpsLimit(index) (Invoke) right after the game's
OptionList_SetValues (hook on sub_827C9358).

ActionScript appended to the end of the main timeline frame script (after OptionList_PopulateData has run):

    OptionList_AddResolutionOption("OPTIONS_FPS_LIMITER", "$OPTIONS_FPS_LIMITER");
    OptionList_AddResolution("30"); OptionList_AddResolution("60"); OptionList_AddResolution("$COMMON_OFF_UPPERCASE");
    OptionList.NumSlots = 3;
    OptionList_SetResolutionIndex(0);
    function SDNX_SetFpsLimit(v) { OptionList_SetResolutionIndex(v); }
"""
import struct
import zlib

ENTRY_UID = 0xEBCA65F0
CHUNK_MOVIE = 0x442A39D9
MARKER = b"SDNX_SetFpsLimit"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- tiny AS2 assembler

def _act(code, payload=b""):
    if code >= 0x80:
        return bytes([code]) + struct.pack("<H", len(payload)) + payload
    return bytes([code])


def push(*vals):
    p = b""
    for v in vals:
        if isinstance(v, str):
            p += b"\x00" + v.encode("latin-1") + b"\x00"
        elif isinstance(v, bool):
            p += b"\x05" + bytes([1 if v else 0])
        elif isinstance(v, int):
            p += b"\x07" + struct.pack("<i", v)
        else:
            raise TypeError(v)
    return _act(0x96, p)


GETVAR, SETMEMBER, CALLMETHOD, CALLFUNC, POP, GETMEMBER = (_act(0x1C), _act(0x4F), _act(0x52), _act(0x3D),
                                                           _act(0x17), _act(0x4E))


def call_function(name, *args):          # result popped
    return push(*reversed(args), len(args), name) + CALLFUNC + POP


def option_list():
    return push("OptionList") + GETVAR


def get_item(i):                         # leaves the item on the stack
    return push(i, 1, "OptionList") + GETVAR + push("GetItem") + CALLMETHOD


def define_function(name, params, body):
    p = name.encode() + b"\0" + struct.pack("<H", len(params))
    for prm in params:
        p += prm.encode() + b"\0"
    p += struct.pack("<H", len(body))
    return _act(0x9B, p) + body


def build_code():
    # The list's slot clips have fixed classes from the PC layout: slot2 = OptionResolutionSlot (arrows + a list of
    # captions), slot3/4 = Txt2, slot5 = Spinner. The third row lands in slot2, so it must be a "resolution" item:
    # a spinner item there is not accepted by the slot (it showed "undefined").
    c = b""
    c += call_function("OptionList_AddResolutionOption", "OPTIONS_FPS_LIMITER", "$OPTIONS_FPS_LIMITER")
    for cap in ("30", "60", "$COMMON_OFF_UPPERCASE"):
        c += call_function("OptionList_AddResolution", cap)
    c += option_list() + push("NumSlots", 3) + SETMEMBER
    c += call_function("OptionList_SetResolutionIndex", 0)
    body = push("v") + GETVAR + push(1, "OptionList_SetResolutionIndex") + CALLFUNC + POP
    c += define_function("SDNX_SetFpsLimit", ["v"], body)
    return c


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- SWF editing

def _rect_len(b):
    return (5 + 4 * (b[0] >> 3) + 7) // 8


def _tags(body, p):
    while p < len(body):
        cl = struct.unpack_from("<H", body, p)[0]
        code, ln = cl >> 6, cl & 0x3F
        hl = 2
        if ln == 0x3F:
            ln = struct.unpack_from("<I", body, p + 2)[0]
            hl = 6
        yield code, p, hl, ln
        p += hl + ln
        if code == 0:
            break


def patch_swf_body(body: bytes) -> bytes:
    """Append the code to the main-timeline DoAction that calls OptionList_PopulateData."""
    if MARKER in body:
        raise ValueError("already patched")
    p0 = _rect_len(body) + 4
    target = None
    for code, p, hl, ln in _tags(body, p0):
        if code == 12 and b"OptionList_PopulateData" in body[p + hl:p + hl + ln] and ln > 1000:
            target = (p, hl, ln)
    if not target:
        raise ValueError("frame script not found")
    p, hl, ln = target
    action = body[p + hl:p + hl + ln]
    if action[-1] != 0:
        raise ValueError("frame script does not end with End")
    new_action = action[:-1] + build_code() + b"\x00"
    tag = struct.pack("<HI", (12 << 6) | 0x3F, len(new_action)) + new_action
    return body[:p] + tag + body[p + hl + ln:]


def patch_movie(movie: bytes) -> bytes:
    sig, ver = movie[:3], movie[3]
    if sig == b"CFX":
        body = zlib.decompress(movie[8:])
    elif sig == b"GFX":
        body = movie[8:]
    else:
        raise ValueError("not a GFx movie: %r" % sig)
    nb = patch_swf_body(body)
    return b"CFX" + bytes([ver]) + struct.pack("<I", len(nb) + 8) + zlib.compress(nb, 9)


def patch_entry(data: bytes) -> bytes:
    """Entry = one chunk 0x442A39D9: header(16) + resource header(0x40) + u32 movie size + u32 4 + movie + pad16."""
    uid, size, dsize, doff = struct.unpack_from(">IIII", data, 0)
    if uid != CHUNK_MOVIE:
        raise ValueError("not a movie chunk")
    msize, rel = struct.unpack_from(">II", data, 0x50)
    movie = data[0x58:0x58 + msize]
    nm = patch_movie(movie)
    out = bytearray(data[:0x50]) + struct.pack(">II", len(nm), rel) + nm
    while len(out) % 16:
        out.append(0)
    out += data[16 + size:]                  # anything after the chunk (normally nothing)
    struct.pack_into(">II", out, 4, len(out) - 16 - (len(data) - 16 - size), len(out) - 16 - (len(data) - 16 - size))
    return bytes(out)


def is_patched(data: bytes) -> bool:
    try:
        msize = struct.unpack_from(">I", data, 0x50)[0]
        m = data[0x58:0x58 + msize]
        body = zlib.decompress(m[8:]) if m[:3] == b"CFX" else m[8:]
        return MARKER in body
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        return False
```

```py
import struct

# Microcode of the run-time "internal D3D" pixel shader (key A4A965C189287B99), host-order words as the
# renderer logged them (SDNX_TRACE_PS), 9 words.
words = [
    0x00000000, 0x1001C400, 0x22000000,
    0xC80F8000, 0x00000000, 0xE2000000,
    0x00000000, 0x00000000, 0x00000000,
]

out = b"".join(struct.pack(">I", w) for w in words)
path = r"D:\CODE\SDNX\port\shaders\internal_ps.bin"
open(path, "wb").write(out)
print(f"wrote {path}: {len(out)} bytes ({len(words)} words)")
```

`port/tools/make_switch_buttons.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Draw Switch-style button glyphs for Sleeping Dogs' UI textures (BUTTON_* in UI/SeekFreeBoot.big).

All shapes and letters are drawn here (a tiny stroke font, no font file and no copied artwork).

Mapping of the game's texture names (Xbox meaning in brackets) to the Switch label:
  default = by position (the NRO sets SDK input_xbox_layout = true: the Switch button in the Xbox button's place
  does its job), so the glyph names the Switch button in that place:
    BUTTON_ACCEPT [A, bottom] -> B   BUTTON_BACK [B, right] -> A   BUTTON_BUTTON1 [X, left] -> Y
    BUTTON_BUTTON2 [Y, top] -> X
  --by-letter (input_xbox_layout = false): A -> A, B -> B, X -> X, Y -> Y
  always: L1 [LB] -> L, R1 [RB] -> R, L2 [LT] -> ZL, R2 [RT] -> ZR, SELECT [Back] -> -, START -> +,
          L1R1 -> L R, L2R2 -> ZL ZR.  Sticks, stick clicks and the D-pad keep the original pictures.

Usage: make_switch_buttons.py out_dir [--by-letter]     (writes PNGs + a contact sheet)
"""
import math
import os
import sys

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFilter

SS = 8  # supersampling

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- stroke font (unit box, y down)


def _arc(cx, cy, rx, ry, a0, a1, n=24):
    return [(cx + rx * math.cos(math.radians(a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n)),
             cy + ry * math.sin(math.radians(a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n))) for i in range(n + 1)]


GLYPHS = {
    "A": [[(0.08, 1.0), (0.5, 0.0), (0.92, 1.0)], [(0.24, 0.66), (0.76, 0.66)]],
    "B": [[(0.16, 0.0), (0.16, 1.0)],
          [(0.16, 0.0), (0.55, 0.0)] + _arc(0.55, 0.24, 0.26, 0.24, -90, 90) + [(0.16, 0.48)],
          [(0.16, 0.48), (0.58, 0.48)] + _arc(0.58, 0.74, 0.28, 0.26, -90, 90) + [(0.16, 1.0)]],
    "X": [[(0.1, 0.0), (0.9, 1.0)], [(0.9, 0.0), (0.1, 1.0)]],
    "Y": [[(0.08, 0.0), (0.5, 0.52), (0.92, 0.0)], [(0.5, 0.52), (0.5, 1.0)]],
    "L": [[(0.2, 0.0), (0.2, 1.0), (0.86, 1.0)]],
    "R": [[(0.16, 1.0), (0.16, 0.0), (0.55, 0.0)] + _arc(0.55, 0.25, 0.27, 0.25, -90, 90) + [(0.16, 0.5)],
          [(0.46, 0.5), (0.86, 1.0)]],
    "Z": [[(0.12, 0.0), (0.88, 0.0), (0.12, 1.0), (0.88, 1.0)]],
    "+": [[(0.5, 0.0), (0.5, 1.0)], [(0.0, 0.5), (1.0, 0.5)]],
    "-": [[(0.0, 0.5), (1.0, 0.5)]],
}
ADVANCE = {"A": 1.0, "B": 0.95, "X": 1.0, "Y": 1.0, "L": 0.9, "R": 0.95, "Z": 1.0, "+": 1.0, "-": 1.0}


def draw_text(d: ImageDraw.ImageDraw, text, cx, cy, h, width, fill):
    """Centre a stroke string at (cx, cy); h = cap height in pixels (supersampled)."""
    gap = 0.18
    total = sum(ADVANCE[c] for c in text) + gap * (len(text) - 1)
    x = cx - total * h / 2
    for c in text:
        for stroke in GLYPHS[c]:
            pts = [(x + px * h, cy - h / 2 + py * h) for px, py in stroke]
            d.line(pts, fill=fill, width=int(width), joint="curve")
            r = width / 2
            for px, py in (pts[0], pts[-1]):
                d.ellipse([px - r, py - r, px + r, py + r], fill=fill)
        x += (ADVANCE[c] + gap) * h


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- shapes

BODY = (46, 46, 48, 255)
BODY_HI = (88, 88, 92, 255)
RIM = (20, 20, 22, 255)
LABEL = (244, 244, 244, 255)
HOLD = (232, 62, 40, 255)  # the originals mark "hold" with a red ring; keep the idea


def _canvas(w, h):
    return Image.new("RGBA", (w * SS, h * SS), (0, 0, 0, 0))


def _finish(img, w, h, shadow=True):
    big = img
    if shadow:
        a = np.array(big)[:, :, 3]
        sh = Image.fromarray((a * 0.55).astype(np.uint8), "L").filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(2.2 * SS))
        base = Image.new("RGBA", big.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
        black = Image.new("RGBA", big.size, (0, 0, 0, 255))
        base.paste(black, (int(0.8 * SS), int(1.6 * SS)), sh)
        base.alpha_composite(big)
        big = base
    return big.resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)


def round_button(label, size=64, hold=False):
    img = _canvas(size, size)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    s = SS
    c = size * s / 2
    r = size * s * 0.40
    if hold:
        rr = size * s * 0.475
        d.ellipse([c - rr, c - rr, c + rr, c + rr], fill=HOLD)
        r = size * s * 0.39
    d.ellipse([c - r, c - r, c + r, c + r], fill=RIM)
    r2 = r - 1.6 * s
    d.ellipse([c - r2, c - r2, c + r2, c + r2], fill=BODY)
    # soft top highlight
    hl = _canvas(size, size)
    hd = ImageDraw.Draw(hl)
    hd.ellipse([c - r2 * 0.8, c - r2 * 0.95, c + r2 * 0.8, c + r2 * 0.1], fill=(255, 255, 255, 34))
    img.alpha_composite(hl.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(3 * s)))
    if label in ("+", "-"):
        draw_text(d, label, c, c, r * 0.95, 4.2 * s, LABEL)
    else:
        draw_text(d, label, c, c + 0.2 * s, r * 0.92, 4.6 * s, LABEL)
    return _finish(img, size, size)


def shoulder(label, w, h, trigger, box=None):
    """Rounded shoulder (L/R) or taller trigger (ZL/ZR) plate inside box=(x0, y0, x1, y1) of a w x h image."""
    img = _canvas(w, h)
    _shoulder_on(img, label, w, h, trigger, box or (0, 0, w, h))
    return _finish(img, w, h)


def _shoulder_on(img, label, w, h, trigger, box):
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    s = SS
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = box
    bw, bh = x1 - x0, y1 - y0
    if trigger:
        pw, ph = bw * 0.62, bh * 0.86
    else:
        pw, ph = bw * 0.90, bh * 0.56
    cx, cy = (x0 + bw / 2) * s, (y0 + bh / 2) * s
    rect = [cx - pw * s / 2, cy - ph * s / 2, cx + pw * s / 2, cy + ph * s / 2]
    rad = (ph if not trigger else pw) * s * 0.30
    d.rounded_rectangle(rect, radius=rad, fill=RIM)
    inner = [rect[0] + 1.6 * s, rect[1] + 1.6 * s, rect[2] - 1.6 * s, rect[3] - 1.6 * s]
    d.rounded_rectangle(inner, radius=max(1, rad - 1.6 * s), fill=BODY)
    hl = Image.new("RGBA", img.size, (0, 0, 0, 0))
    ImageDraw.Draw(hl).rounded_rectangle([inner[0], inner[1], inner[2], (inner[1] + inner[3]) / 2],
                                         radius=max(1, rad - 1.6 * s), fill=(255, 255, 255, 30))
    img.alpha_composite(hl.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(2 * s)))
    if len(label) == 1:
        th = min(ph * 0.52, pw * 0.5)
    else:
        th = min(ph * 0.36, pw * 0.36)
    draw_text(d, label, cx, cy, th * s, 4.2 * s, LABEL)


def pair(labels, w, h, trigger):
    img = _canvas(w, h)
    _shoulder_on(img, labels[0], w, h, trigger, (0, 0, w / 2, h))
    _shoulder_on(img, labels[1], w, h, trigger, (w / 2, 0, w, h))
    return _finish(img, w, h)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- set

def build(by_letter=False):
    face = {"BUTTON_ACCEPT": "B", "BUTTON_BACK": "A", "BUTTON_BUTTON1": "Y", "BUTTON_BUTTON2": "X"}
    if by_letter:
        face = {"BUTTON_ACCEPT": "A", "BUTTON_BACK": "B", "BUTTON_BUTTON1": "X", "BUTTON_BUTTON2": "Y"}
    out = {}
    for name, lab in face.items():
        out[name] = round_button(lab)
    out["BUTTON_BACK_HOLD"] = round_button(face["BUTTON_BACK"], hold=True)
    out["BUTTON_BUTTON1_HOLD"] = round_button(face["BUTTON_BUTTON1"], hold=True)
    out["BUTTON_BUTTON2_HOLD"] = round_button(face["BUTTON_BUTTON2"], hold=True)
    out["BUTTON_L1"] = shoulder("L", 64, 64, False)
    out["BUTTON_R1"] = shoulder("R", 64, 64, False)
    out["BUTTON_L2"] = shoulder("ZL", 64, 64, True)
    out["BUTTON_R2"] = shoulder("ZR", 64, 64, True)
    out["BUTTON_SELECT"] = round_button("-")
    out["BUTTON_START"] = round_button("+")
    out["BUTTON_L1R1"] = pair(("L", "R"), 128, 64, False)
    out["BUTTON_L2R2"] = pair(("ZL", "ZR"), 128, 64, True)
    return {k: np.array(v.convert("RGBA")) for k, v in out.items()}


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print(__doc__)
        return 1
    outdir = sys.argv[1]
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
    imgs = build("--by-letter" in sys.argv)
    for k, v in imgs.items():
        Image.fromarray(v, "RGBA").save(os.path.join(outdir, k + ".png"))
    cols, cell = 6, 136
    rows = (len(imgs) + cols - 1) // cols
    sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (cols * cell, rows * cell), (90, 90, 90, 255))
    for i, (k, v) in enumerate(imgs.items()):
        sheet.alpha_composite(Image.fromarray(v, "RGBA"), ((i % cols) * cell + 4, (i // cols) * cell + 4))
    sheet.save(os.path.join(outdir, "_sheet.png"))
    print("%d glyphs -> %s" % (len(imgs), outdir))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`port/tools/native_shot.ps1`:

```ps1
# One gameplay frame of the native renderer on the bench (keys posted to the window, no focus needed):
#   native_shot.ps1 -Name x [-At 50] [-Addr 0x10bff000] [-Env @{SDNX_NO_CULL="1"}] [-LogFrame]
# Writes port/shots/<Name>.png (the front buffer, or the resolved image at -Addr) and port/shots/<Name>.log.
param([string]$Name = "shot", [int]$At = 50, [string]$Addr = "", [hashtable]$Env = @{}, [switch]$LogFrame,
      [int]$Presses = 2, [string[]]$Keys = @(),
      [string]$Replay = "$PSScriptRoot\keys_continue.txt")
# -Replay: recorded presses that take the game from boot to gameplay (intros skipped, "Continue", ~20 s; recorded
# with fps_bench.ps1 -Record, pad A as Space). -Replay "" falls back to the old START presses.
$dir = "D:\CODE\SDNX\port\shots"; New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $dir | Out-Null
$vars = @{ SDNX_NATIVE = "1"; SDNX_DUMP_AT = "$At"; SDNX_DUMP_ADDR = $Addr; SDNX_LOG_AT = $(if ($LogFrame) { "$At" } else { "" }) }
foreach ($k in $Env.Keys) { $vars[$k] = $Env[$k] }
$old = @{}; foreach ($k in $vars.Keys) { $old[$k] = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($k); [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable($k, $vars[$k]) }
Remove-Item D:\CODE\SDNX\nx\desktop\out\front_dump.ppm -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
& "$PSScriptRoot\fps_bench.ps1" -Post -Total ($At + 10) -StartKeyAt 40 -Presses $Presses -Keys $Keys -Replay $Replay -Log "$dir\$Name.log" | Select-Object -Last 1
foreach ($k in $vars.Keys) { [Environment]::SetEnvironmentVariable($k, $old[$k]) }
python -c "from PIL import Image; Image.open(r'D:\CODE\SDNX\nx\desktop\out\front_dump.ppm').convert('RGB').save(r'$dir\$Name.png')"
```

`port/tools/prof_symbolize.py`:

```py
# Top samples per thread of the last N blocks of a Switch rex_profile.log, symbolized with addr2line on the ELF of the
# same build (nx/elf/). addr2line runs in the devkitA64 Docker image through WSL (SUDO_PW: the WSL sudo password, or be in the docker group).
#   python prof_symbolize.py <rex_profile.log> <nx/elf/sdretail_XXXX.elf> [blocks=4] [top=14] [min_cpu=5]
import collections
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys

log, elf = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
nblocks = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 4
top = int(sys.argv[4]) if len(sys.argv) > 4 else 14
min_cpu = float(sys.argv[5]) if len(sys.argv) > 5 else 5.0
root = 'D:/CODE/SDNX/'

parts = open(log, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n==== ')
blocks = parts[-nblocks - 1:-1] if len(parts) > nblocks + 1 else parts[1:]
threads = collections.OrderedDict()
for b in blocks:
    cur = None
    for line in b.split('\n'):
        m = re.match(r'-- thread \d+ "([^"]*)": CPU ([\d.]+)%', line)
        if m:
            cur = m.group(1)
            t = threads.setdefault(cur, {'cpu': 0.0, 'pc': collections.Counter(), 'lr': collections.Counter()})
            t['cpu'] += float(m.group(2)) / len(blocks)
            continue
        m = re.match(r'\s+([\d.]+)%\s+(pc|lr) image\+(0x[0-9a-f]+)', line)
        if m and cur:
            threads[cur][m.group(2)][m.group(3)] += float(m.group(1))

sel = [(k, v) for k, v in threads.items() if v['cpu'] >= min_cpu]
addrs = sorted({a for _, v in sel for kind in ('pc', 'lr') for a, _ in v[kind].most_common(top)})
open(root + 'port/pcs_prof.txt', 'w').write(' '.join(addrs) + '\n')
elf_rel = elf.replace(chr(92), '/').split('SDNX/')[-1]
script = ('/opt/devkitpro/devkitA64/bin/aarch64-none-elf-addr2line -f -C -e /w/' + elf_rel +
          ' $(cat /w/port/pcs_prof.txt)')
open(root + 'port/pcs_prof.sh', 'w', newline='\n').write(script + '\n')
cmd = ('echo "' + os.environ.get('SUDO_PW', '') + '" | sudo -S -p "" docker run --rm -v /mnt/d/CODE/SDNX:/w devkitpro/devkita64:latest '
       'bash /w/port/pcs_prof.sh')
r = subprocess.run(['wsl', '-e', 'bash', '-lc', cmd], capture_output=True, text=True)
out = r.stdout.split('\n')
if len(out) < 2 * len(addrs):
    print('addr2line failed:', r.stderr[:400])
names = {a: (out[2 * i].strip()[:80] if 2 * i < len(out) else '?') for i, a in enumerate(addrs)}
for k, v in sel:
    print('== %s  CPU %.1f%%' % (k, v['cpu']))
    for kind in ('pc', 'lr'):
        for a, p in v[kind].most_common(top):
            print('   %s %5.1f%%  %s' % (kind, p / len(blocks), names.get(a, '?')))

```

`port/tools/sdbig.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Sleeping Dogs (Xbox 360) BIG/BIX archive tool.

Format (checked on the retail X360 data, see docs/branding.md):

  NAME.bix  one qChunk (big-endian):  u32 chunk_uid, u32 size, u32 data_size, u32 data_offset,
            then BIGFileIndex (32-bit build): qResourceData header (0x40 bytes, name at +0x18),
            s64 sort_key, u32 entry_count, u32 rel_offset_to_entries (relative to its own field),
            u32 file, u16 state, u16 mount, u32 pad0, u32 pad1, char big_name[32], entries...
  entry     u32 uid, u32 offset_div_4, u32 load_offset, u32 compressed_size, u32 compressed_extra,
            u32 uncompressed_size                                             (24 bytes, sorted by uid)
  NAME.big  data of entry = file[offset*4 + (load_offset & 0xFFF) : ...]
            compressed_size == 0  -> stored, uncompressed_size bytes
            compressed_size != 0  -> UFG LZ stream with a little-endian 'PMCQ' (QCMP) header

Usage:
  sdbig.py list   UI                       (path without extension, or .bix/.big)
  sdbig.py extract UI out_dir [uid ...]
  sdbig.py grep   UI pattern               (search decompressed entries, ASCII/UTF-8)
"""
import os
import struct
import sys

HDR_ID = 0x51434D50


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- UFG LZ (qDecompressLZ)

def lz_decompress(buf: bytes) -> bytes:
    """quark/compresslz.hh::qDecompressLZ. Header fields are little-endian on every platform."""
    hid, typ, ver, data_off, extra, csize, usize, chk = struct.unpack_from("<IHHIIqqq", buf, 0)
    if hid != HDR_ID:
        raise ValueError("not a QCMP stream (id %08X)" % hid)
    out = bytearray()
    cache = [(0, 0)] * 32
    cur = 0
    i = data_off
    end = csize
    while i < end:
        bits = buf[i]
        i += 1
        if bits < 32:
            n = bits + 1
            out += buf[i:i + n]
            i += n
            continue
        idx = bits & 31
        et = bits >> 5
        if et == 1:
            off, ln = cache[idx]
        else:
            off = buf[i] | (idx << 8)
            i += 1
            if et == 7:
                ln = buf[i] + 1
                i += 1
            else:
                ln = et + 1
            cache[cur] = (off, ln)
            cur = (cur + 1) % 32
        s = len(out) - off
        for k in range(ln):  # overlapping copy
            out.append(out[s + k])
    if len(out) != usize:
        raise ValueError("size mismatch %d != %d" % (len(out), usize))
    return bytes(out)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- index

class Entry:
    __slots__ = ("index", "uid", "off4", "load_offset", "csize", "cextra", "usize", "pos")

    def __init__(self, index, pos, uid, off4, load_offset, csize, cextra, usize):
        self.index, self.pos = index, pos
        self.uid, self.off4, self.load_offset = uid, off4, load_offset
        self.csize, self.cextra, self.usize = csize, cextra, usize

    @property
    def data_pos(self):
        return self.off4 * 4 + (self.load_offset & 0xFFF)

    @property
    def stored_size(self):
        return self.csize if self.csize else self.usize


class BigIndex:
    ENTRY_FMT = ">IIIIII"

    def __init__(self, base):
        for ext in (".bix", ".big"):
            if base.lower().endswith(ext):
                base = base[: -len(ext)]
        self.base = base
        self.bix_path = base + ".bix"
        self.big_path = base + ".big"
        self.raw = bytearray(open(self.bix_path, "rb").read())
        r = self.raw
        # chunk header (16) + resource header; the entry count/offset pair sits at 0x58/0x5C
        self.count, rel = struct.unpack_from(">II", r, 0x58)
        self.entries_pos = 0x5C + rel
        self.name = r[0x70:0x90].split(b"\0", 1)[0].decode("ascii", "replace")
        self.entries = []
        for n in range(self.count):
            p = self.entries_pos + n * 24
            self.entries.append(Entry(n, p, *struct.unpack_from(self.ENTRY_FMT, r, p)))
        self.by_uid = {e.uid: e for e in self.entries}

    def read(self, e: Entry, f=None) -> bytes:
        own = f is None
        if own:
            f = open(self.big_path, "rb")
        try:
            f.seek(e.data_pos)
            d = f.read(e.stored_size)
        finally:
            if own:
                f.close()
        return lz_decompress(d) if e.csize else d

    def write_entry(self, e: Entry):
        struct.pack_into(self.ENTRY_FMT, self.raw, e.pos, e.uid, e.off4, e.load_offset, e.csize,
                         e.cextra, e.usize)

    def save_bix(self, path=None):
        open(path or self.bix_path, "wb").write(self.raw)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- perm-file chunks

def iter_chunks(data: bytes):
    """Top-level qChunks of a .perm resource stream: u32 uid, u32 size, u32 data_size, u32 data_off."""
    p = 0
    n = len(data)
    while p + 16 <= n:
        uid, size, dsize, doff = struct.unpack_from(">IIII", data, p)
        if size == 0 and uid == 0:
            break
        yield p, uid, size, dsize, doff
        p += 16 + size
        if size == 0:
            break


def chunk_name(data: bytes, p: int) -> str:
    """Debug name of the resource in a chunk (qResourceData::mDebugName at +0x1C of the payload)."""
    s = data[p + 16 + 0x1C: p + 16 + 0x1C + 36]
    s = s.split(b"\0", 1)[0]
    try:
        t = s.decode("ascii")
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        return ""
    return t if t.isprintable() else ""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- CLI

def main(argv):
    if len(argv) < 3:
        print(__doc__)
        return 1
    cmd, base = argv[1], argv[2]
    bi = BigIndex(base)
    if cmd == "list":
        print("%s: %d entries, name %r" % (bi.bix_path, bi.count, bi.name))
        with open(bi.big_path, "rb") as f:
            for e in bi.entries:
                name = ""
                try:
                    d = bi.read(e, f)
                    for p, uid, size, dsize, doff in iter_chunks(d):
                        name = chunk_name(d, p)
                        if name:
                            break
                except Exception as ex:  # noqa: BLE001
                    name = "<%s>" % ex
                print("%08X @%08X c=%7d u=%7d %s" % (e.uid, e.data_pos, e.csize, e.usize, name))
    elif cmd == "chunks":
        # every top-level chunk of every entry: entry uid, chunk offset, chunk type uid, size, name
        with open(bi.big_path, "rb") as f:
            for e in bi.entries:
                try:
                    d = bi.read(e, f)
                except Exception as ex:  # noqa: BLE001
                    print("%08X <%s>" % (e.uid, ex))
                    continue
                for p, uid, size, dsize, doff in iter_chunks(d):
                    print("%08X +%06X %08X %7d %s" % (e.uid, p, uid, size, chunk_name(d, p)))
    elif cmd == "extract":
        out = argv[3]
        os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
        want = {int(x, 16) for x in argv[4:]}
        with open(bi.big_path, "rb") as f:
            for e in bi.entries:
                if want and e.uid not in want:
                    continue
                open(os.path.join(out, "%08X.bin" % e.uid), "wb").write(bi.read(e, f))
    elif cmd == "grep":
        pat = argv[3].encode("utf-8")
        low = pat.lower()
        with open(bi.big_path, "rb") as f:
            for e in bi.entries:
                d = bi.read(e, f)
                dl = d.lower()
                k = dl.find(low)
                hits = 0
                while k >= 0:
                    hits += 1
                    k = dl.find(low, k + 1)
                if hits:
                    print("%08X hits=%d size=%d" % (e.uid, hits, len(d)))
    else:
        print(__doc__)
        return 1
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

```

`port/tools/sdcontainer.py`:

```py
"""Xenos shader containers of Sleeping Dogs: parse the header the same way port/tools/shaderlib/ucode_hash.c
does, show the microcode, and (with --make) build a container for a raw microcode blob so the offline
translator (XenosRecomp -> HLSL -> DXC -> SPIR-V) can be run on shaders that the game creates internally.

Used for the run-time "internal D3D" vertex shader (key B6C9863F710683EC, 24 words, no fetches).
"""
import argparse
import struct
import sys


def be32(d, o):
    return (d[o] << 24) | (d[o + 1] << 16) | (d[o + 2] << 8) | d[o + 3]


def parse(path):
    d = open(path, "rb").read()
    magic = be32(d, 0)
    virt = be32(d, 4)
    hdr = be32(d, 24)
    start = virt + be32(d, hdr)
    size = be32(d, hdr + 4)
    return d, dict(magic=magic, virt=virt, hdr=hdr, start=start, size=size, total=len(d))


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("path")
    ap.add_argument("--words", action="store_true", help="print the microcode words")
    ap.add_argument("--make", metavar="TEMPLATE", help="write a container made from TEMPLATE with the microcode of PATH")
    ap.add_argument("--out")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if args.make:
        # args.path is a raw microcode blob (no container header): wrap it into a copy of TEMPLATE.
        code = open(args.path, "rb").read()
        t, tf = parse(args.make)
        print(f"template {args.make}: magic=0x{tf['magic']:08X} start={tf['start']} size={tf['size']} total={tf['total']}")
        words = len(code) // 4
        print(f"raw microcode: {len(code)} bytes, {words} words")
        out = bytearray(t)
        hdr = tf["hdr"]
        struct.pack_into(">I", out, hdr + 4, len(code))
        struct.pack_into(">I", out, 8, words)
        # Direct3D's own shaders carry no constant table: XenosRecomp then links vertex exports to pixel
        # inputs by register (export N -> TEXCOORDN), which is what these internal shaders rely on.
        struct.pack_into(">I", out, 0x10, 0)  # ShaderContainer::constantTableOffset = NULL
        struct.pack_into(">I", out, hdr + 0x14, 0)  # Shader::interpolatorInfo (no declared interpolators)
        start = tf["start"]
        out[start:start + len(code)] = code
        del out[start + len(code):]
        dest = args.out or ("made_" + args.path.rsplit("\\", 1)[-1])
        open(dest, "wb").write(bytes(out))
        print(f"written {dest}: {len(out)} bytes")
        return

    d, f = parse(args.path)
    for k, v in f.items():
        print(f"{k:6} = {v} (0x{v:X})" if isinstance(v, int) else f"{k} = {v}")
    if f["start"] + f["size"] > f["total"]:
        print("WARNING: microcode range exceeds the file")
    code = d[f["start"]:f["start"] + f["size"]]
    print(f"microcode: {len(code)} bytes, {len(code)//4} words")
    if args.words:
        for i in range(0, len(code) // 4):
            print(f"  [{i:3}] {be32(code, i*4):08X}")

    if args.make:
        t, tf = parse(args.make)
        words = len(code) // 4
        out = bytearray(t)
        hdr = tf["hdr"]
        struct.pack_into(">I", out, hdr + 4, len(code))
        # dword 8 of the header is the microcode size in dwords for these containers.
        struct.pack_into(">I", out, 8, words)
        start = tf["start"]
        out[start:start + len(code)] = code
        del out[start + len(code):]
        dest = args.out or ("made_" + args.path.rsplit("\\", 1)[-1])
        open(dest, "wb").write(bytes(out))
        print(f"written {dest}: {len(out)} bytes")


main()

```

`port/tools/sdshaders.py`:

```py
"""Sleeping Dogs (X360): extract every Xenos shader container (signature 0x102A1100 pixel / 0x102A1101 vertex)
from the game's .big archives, for the offline shader library of the native renderer (docs/native-renderer-plan.md).

Each archive entry is decompressed (sdbig.BigIndex) and scanned at 4-byte alignment for the signature. A hit is
kept only if it passes the layout checks the masseffect-nx runtime makes (shaders/tools/container_check.h):
shader header and microcode inside the container. Size = virtualSize + physicalSize. Output, deduplicated by
content: <out>/vs_<sha1:16>.bin, <out>/ps_<sha1:16>.bin, and <out>/containers.tsv (name, archive, entry, offset).
Nothing from the game is meant to leave the user's machine.

Usage: sdshaders.py <game_root> <out_dir>
"""
import hashlib
import os
import struct
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from sdbig import BigIndex  # noqa: E402


def be32(d, o):
    return struct.unpack_from(">I", d, o)[0]


def container_at(d, o):
    """Container bytes at offset o, or None if the layout checks fail."""
    if o + 36 > len(d):
        return None
    sig = be32(d, o)
    vertex = sig & 1
    virt, phys = be32(d, o + 4), be32(d, o + 8)
    size = virt + phys
    if size < 36 or o + size > len(d) or size > 4 << 20:
        return None
    c = d[o:o + size]
    header = be32(c, 24)
    if header + 8 > size:
        return None
    micro_start = virt + be32(c, header)
    micro_size = be32(c, header + 4)
    if micro_size == 0 or micro_size % 4 or micro_start + micro_size > size:
        return None
    if header >= virt or header + (40 if vertex else 32) > virt:
        return None
    return c


def main(root, out):
    os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
    seen = {}
    rows = []
    hits = 0
    for name in sorted(os.listdir(root)):
        if not name.endswith(".bix") or name.endswith(".orig"):
            continue
        base = os.path.join(root, name[:-4])
        try:
            bi = BigIndex(base)
        except Exception as ex:  # noqa: BLE001
            print("skip %s: %s" % (name, ex))
            continue
        n_arch = 0
        with open(bi.big_path, "rb") as f:
            for e in bi.entries:
                try:
                    d = bi.read(e, f)
                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
                    continue
                o = d.find(b"\x10\x2A\x11")
                while o >= 0:
                    if o % 4 == 0 and d[o + 3] in (0, 1):
                        c = container_at(d, o)
                        if c is not None:
                            hits += 1
                            n_arch += 1
                            kind = "vs" if c[3] & 1 else "ps"
                            fn = "%s_%s.bin" % (kind, hashlib.sha1(c).hexdigest()[:16])
                            if fn not in seen:
                                seen[fn] = len(c)
                                open(os.path.join(out, fn), "wb").write(c)
                            rows.append("%s\t%s\t%08X\t%X" % (fn, name[:-4], e.uid, o))
                    o = d.find(b"\x10\x2A\x11", o + 1)
        print("%-14s %6d containers" % (name[:-4], n_arch))
    with open(os.path.join(out, "containers.tsv"), "w") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(rows) + "\n")
    vs = sum(1 for k in seen if k.startswith("vs"))
    print("total hits %d, distinct %d (vs %d, ps %d) -> %s" % (hits, len(seen), vs, len(seen) - vs, out))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 3:
        sys.exit(__doc__)
    main(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2])

```

`port/tools/sdtex.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Illusion::Texture headers (chunk 0xCDBFA090) in a Sleeping Dogs X360 .perm stream.

Layout of the 0x120-byte payload (offsets from the chunk start, big-endian), found on the retail data:
  +0x2C name[36]       +0x50 flags        +0x54 format (s8: 0 ARGB8, 1 DXT1, 2 DXT3, 3 DXT5, ...)
  +0x5C u16 width, u16 height             +0x60 s8 mips
  +0x80 u32 image data position (in the paired .temp stream)   +0x84 u32 image data byte size
  +0xA0.. Xenos texture fetch constant (GPUTEXTURE_FETCH_CONSTANT, 6 dwords)

Usage: sdtex.py perm.bin
"""
import struct
import sys

CHUNK_TEXTURE = 0xCDBFA090
FORMATS = {0: "A8R8G8B8", 1: "DXT1", 2: "DXT3", 3: "DXT5", 4: "R5G6B5", 5: "A1R5G5B5", 6: "X8", 7: "X16",
           8: "CXT1", 9: "DXN"}


def iter_textures(d):
    p = 0
    while p + 16 <= len(d):
        uid, size, dsize, doff = struct.unpack_from(">IIII", d, p)
        if uid == 0 and size == 0:
            break
        if uid == CHUNK_TEXTURE:
            name = d[p + 0x2C:p + 0x2C + 36].split(b"\0", 1)[0].decode("ascii", "replace")
            flags = struct.unpack_from(">I", d, p + 0x50)[0]
            fmt = d[p + 0x54]
            w, h = struct.unpack_from(">HH", d, p + 0x5C)
            mips = d[p + 0x60]
            pos, nbytes = struct.unpack_from(">II", d, p + 0x80)
            fetch = struct.unpack_from(">6I", d, p + 0xA0)
            yield dict(off=p, name=name, flags=flags, fmt=fmt, w=w, h=h, mips=mips, pos=pos, size=nbytes,
                       fetch=fetch)
        p += 16 + size


def main():
    d = open(sys.argv[1], "rb").read()
    end = 0
    for t in iter_textures(d):
        end = max(end, t["pos"] + t["size"])
        print("+%05X %-24s %-8s %4dx%-4d mips=%d flags=%X data=%08X+%06X fetch=%s" % (
            t["off"], t["name"], FORMATS.get(t["fmt"], t["fmt"]), t["w"], t["h"], t["mips"], t["flags"], t["pos"],
            t["size"], " ".join("%08X" % x for x in t["fetch"])))
    print("temp stream end: 0x%X (%d)" % (end, end))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`port/tools/sdtext.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Sleeping Dogs X360 localisation tables (chunk type 0x90CE6B7A, names like 'russian_Global').

Chunk layout (big-endian, offsets from the chunk start):
  +0x00 qChunk {uid 0x90CE6B7A, size, data_size, data_offset}      size = total - 16
  +0x10 qResourceData header (0x40 bytes, debug name at +0x2C)
  +0x50 u32 count
  +0x54 u32 rel  -> entries at 0x54 + rel (always 0x58)
  entries: {u32 hash, u32 rel} sorted by hash; text at (&rel) + rel, zero-terminated UTF-8,
           each string zero-padded so the next one starts on a multiple of 4

Usage:
  sdtext.py dump  ARCHIVE [pattern]     every string of every table (optionally filtered, case-insensitive)
"""
import struct
import sys

CHUNK_TEXT = 0x90CE6B7A


class TextTable:
    def __init__(self, chunk: bytes):
        uid, size, dsize, doff = struct.unpack_from(">IIII", chunk, 0)
        if uid != CHUNK_TEXT:
            raise ValueError("not a text chunk")
        self.header = chunk[:0x50]
        self.name = chunk[0x2C:0x50].split(b"\0", 1)[0].decode("latin-1")
        count, rel = struct.unpack_from(">II", chunk, 0x50)
        eo = 0x54 + rel
        self.entries = []  # [hash, bytes]
        for i in range(count):
            h, r = struct.unpack_from(">II", chunk, eo + i * 8)
            p = eo + i * 8 + 4 + r
            e = chunk.index(b"\0", p)
            self.entries.append([h, chunk[p:e]])
        self.orig_size = 16 + size

    def build(self) -> bytes:
        n = len(self.entries)
        eo = 0x58
        strings_at = eo + n * 8
        table = bytearray()
        blob = bytearray()
        for i, (h, s) in enumerate(self.entries):
            field = eo + i * 8 + 4
            table += struct.pack(">II", h, strings_at + len(blob) - field)
            blob += s + b"\0"
            while (strings_at + len(blob)) % 4:
                blob.append(0)
        body = bytearray(self.header) + struct.pack(">II", n, 4) + table + blob
        while len(body) % 4:
            body.append(0)
        struct.pack_into(">II", body, 4, len(body) - 16, len(body) - 16)
        return bytes(body)


def iter_text_chunks(data: bytes):
    p = 0
    while p + 16 <= len(data):
        uid, size, dsize, doff = struct.unpack_from(">IIII", data, p)
        if uid == 0 and size == 0:
            break
        if uid == CHUNK_TEXT:
            yield p, 16 + size
        p += 16 + size


def main(argv):
    import sdbig
    if len(argv) < 3 or argv[1] != "dump":
        print(__doc__)
        return 1
    bi = sdbig.BigIndex(argv[2])
    pat = argv[3].lower() if len(argv) > 3 else None
    out = sys.stdout.buffer
    with open(bi.big_path, "rb") as f:
        for e in bi.entries:
            d = bi.read(e, f)
            for p, ln in iter_text_chunks(d):
                t = TextTable(d[p:p + ln])
                for h, s in t.entries:
                    u = s.decode("utf-8", "replace")
                    if pat and pat not in u.lower():
                        continue
                    out.write(("%08X %s %08X\t%s\n" % (e.uid, t.name, h, u.replace("\n", "\\n"))).encode("utf-8"))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

```

`port/tools/seam.py`:

```py
# Vertical seam finder: mean absolute step between adjacent columns of a PNG, printed around the columns where it is
# largest (relative to its neighbours). Usage: python seam.py image.png [x_from x_to]
import sys
from PIL import Image

im = Image.open(sys.argv[1]).convert('L')
px = im.load()
w, h = im.size
x0 = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 1
x1 = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else w - 1
steps = {}
for x in range(max(1, x0), min(w, x1)):
    steps[x] = sum(abs(px[x, y] - px[x - 1, y]) for y in range(0, h, 2)) / (h / 2)
mean = sum(steps.values()) / len(steps)
top = sorted(steps, key=lambda x: -steps[x])[:6]
print(im.size, 'mean step %.2f' % mean, 'largest:', ', '.join('x=%d %.1f' % (x, steps[x]) for x in sorted(top)))

```

`port/tools/seam_bands.py`:

```py
# Seam per horizontal band of a debug_composite band image: mean column step around x (default 1100..1140).
import sys
from PIL import Image
im = Image.open(sys.argv[1]).convert('L'); px = im.load()
names = sys.argv[2].split(',') if len(sys.argv) > 2 else [str(i) for i in range(6)]
for b in range(6):
    y0, y1 = b * 118 + 4, min(b * 118 + 114, 704)
    st = {x: sum(abs(px[x, y] - px[x - 1, y]) for y in range(y0, y1)) / (y1 - y0) for x in range(1001, 1199)}
    mean = sum(st.values()) / len(st); avg_l = sum(px[x, y] for x in range(1060, 1115) for y in range(y0, y1, 4)) / (55 * len(range(y0, y1, 4)))
    avg_r = sum(px[x, y] for x in range(1125, 1180) for y in range(y0, y1, 4)) / (55 * len(range(y0, y1, 4)))
    print('%-10s mean %.2f  x1118..1123: %s  left %.1f right %.1f' % (names[b], mean, ' '.join('%.1f' % st[x] for x in range(1118, 1124)), avg_l, avg_r))
```

`port/tools/shaderlib/build_shaderlib.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Sleeping Dogs shader library, offline (WSL). Steps:
#   1. containers:  py port/tools/sdshaders.py <game_root> port/shaders/containers     (Windows side)
#   2. HLSL:        XenosRecomp from masseffect-nx with the SDNX run-time vertex fetch swizzle (nx/shaders, built
#                   by nx/shaders/tools/build_xenos_hlsl.sh -> ~/tools/xenos_hlsl_sdnx)
#   3. SPIR-V:      DXC 1.9 (~/tools/dxc) + spirv-val (built from sdk/thirdparty/spirv-tools -> ~/tools/spvtools)
#   4. hashes:      XXH3-64 of each container's microcode, as the SDK hashes guest memory (ucode_hash.c)
# Results in ~/sd-shaders/{containers,hlsl,spv}, port/shaders/ucode_hashes.txt. Game data never leaves the PC.
set -e
SRC=/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/port/shaders/containers
W=$HOME/sd-shaders
mkdir -p $W && rm -rf $W/containers && cp -r $SRC $W/containers && rm -f $W/containers/containers.tsv
R=/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/nx
XXHASH_DIR=$HOME/masseffect-nx/sdk/thirdparty/xxHash FMT_DIR=$HOME/masseffect-nx/sdk/thirdparty/fmt/include bash $R/shaders/tools/build_xenos_hlsl.sh
cp $R/out/tools/xenos_hlsl $HOME/tools/xenos_hlsl_sdnx
rm -rf $W/hlsl $W/spv; mkdir -p $W/hlsl
for f in $W/containers/*.bin; do b=$(basename $f .bin); t=$(mktemp -d); ln -s $f $t/$b.bin
  $HOME/tools/xenos_hlsl_sdnx $t $t/o $R/shaders/XenosRecomp/shader_common.h >/dev/null 2>&1 && mv $t/o/$b.hlsl $W/hlsl/; rm -rf $t; done
echo "hlsl: $(ls $W/hlsl | wc -l) of $(ls $W/containers | wc -l)"
export DXC=$HOME/tools/dxc/bin/dxc VAL=$HOME/tools/spvtools/tools/spirv-val OUT=$W/spv LD_LIBRARY_PATH=$HOME/tools/dxc/lib
mkdir -p $OUT
ls $W/hlsl/*.hlsl | xargs -P 12 -n 1 sh $HOME/me-shaders/shaders/tools/compile_spirv_one.sh
echo "spv: $(ls $OUT/*.spv | wc -l), errors: $(ls $OUT/*.err 2>/dev/null | wc -l)"
gcc -O2 -I$HOME/masseffect-nx/sdk/thirdparty/xxHash -o $HOME/tools/uhash /mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/port/tools/shaderlib/ucode_hash.c
(cd $W/containers && $HOME/tools/uhash *.bin | sed 's/\.bin$//') > /mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/port/shaders/ucode_hashes.txt

# 5. Run-time package for the native renderer: <out>/index.txt + <out>/<name>.spv
#    index line: "<ps|vs> <key hex> <name> [K:addr:reg:mask ...]"; ps key = raw microcode XXH3, vs key = XXH3 with the
#    vertex fetches zeroed (one entry serves every Direct3D variant); the fetch order comes from the HLSL.
PKG=${PKG:-/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/nx/desktop/out/sdnx_shaders}
rm -rf "$PKG"; mkdir -p "$PKG"
(cd $W/containers && $HOME/tools/uhash *.bin) > $W/keys.txt
python3 - "$W" "$PKG" <<'PY'
import os, sys, shutil
w, pkg = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
seen = set(); lines = []; dup = 0; skipped_computed = []
for line in open(os.path.join(w, 'keys.txt')):
    raw, masked, fn = line.split()
    name = fn[:-4]; kind = name[:2]
    spv = os.path.join(w, 'spv', name + '.spv')
    if not os.path.exists(spv): continue
    key = raw if kind == 'ps' else masked
    if (kind, key) in seen: dup += 1; continue
    seen.add((kind, key))
    order = ''
    computed = False
    if kind == 'vs':
        for l in open(os.path.join(w, 'hlsl', name + '.hlsl')):
            if l.startswith('// SDNX_FETCH_ORDER:'): order = l.split(':', 1)[1].strip()
            # Hand-made instancing (vertex index read from a stream): the shader pulls its vertices itself.
            if l.startswith('// SDNX_COMPUTED_INDEX_FETCH'): computed = True
    if computed: skipped_computed.append(name); order = (order + ' pull').strip()
    shutil.copy(spv, os.path.join(pkg, name + '.spv'))
    lines.append(('%s %s %s %s' % (kind, key, name, order)).rstrip())
open(os.path.join(pkg, 'index.txt'), 'w').write('\n'.join(lines) + '\n')
print('package: %d entries (%d duplicate keys skipped) -> %s' % (len(lines), dup, pkg))
print('pulling their vertices (computed-index instancing): %s' % ' '.join(skipped_computed))
PY

# 6. Run-time Direct3D shaders that are not in the game containers (clears through rectangle lists):
#    port/shaders/internal/index.txt lists them; a .hlsl is compiled (compile_internal_hlsl.sh), otherwise the
#    prebuilt .spv is copied.
INT=/mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/port/shaders/internal
while read -r kind key name rest; do
  [ -n "$name" ] || continue
  if [ -f "$INT/$name.hlsl" ]; then
    PKG_OUT="$PKG" bash /mnt/d/CODE/SDNX/port/tools/compile_internal_hlsl.sh "$name" "$kind" >/dev/null
  else
    cp "$INT/$name.spv" "$PKG/$name.spv"
  fi
  echo "$kind $key $name $rest" | sed 's/ *$//' >> "$PKG/index.txt"
done < <(tr -d '\r' < "$INT/index.txt")
echo "internal shaders added from $INT"
```

`port/tools/shaderlib/ucode_hash.c`:

```c
// Keys of Xenos shader containers for the SDNX shader package (XXH3-64, as the SDK hashes guest memory):
//   raw:    the microcode bytes as stored (big-endian) - pixel shaders are looked up by it;
//   masked: the same with every vertex fetch instruction (3 words) zeroed - vertex shaders are looked up by it,
//           because Direct3D rewrites the fetches at bind (the renderer computes the same key at run time).
// Vertex fetches are found from the control flow: exec clauses, sequence bit 0 = fetch, word 0 opcode 0.
// Output per container: "<raw hex> <masked hex> <file>".
#define XXH_INLINE_ALL
#include "xxhash.h"
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static uint32_t be32(const unsigned char* p) { return (uint32_t)p[0] << 24 | p[1] << 16 | p[2] << 8 | p[3]; }

static void mask_vertex_fetches(unsigned char* m, uint32_t words) {
  uint32_t first_instr = UINT32_MAX;
  for (uint32_t i = 0;; i++) {
    if (first_instr != UINT32_MAX && i >= first_instr) break;
    if (i * 3 + 2 >= words) break;
    uint32_t a = be32(m + i * 12), b = be32(m + i * 12 + 4), c = be32(m + i * 12 + 8);
    uint64_t cfs[2] = {(uint64_t)a | ((uint64_t)(b & 0xFFFF) << 32), (uint64_t)(b >> 16) | ((uint64_t)c << 16)};
    for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
      uint32_t op = (uint32_t)(cfs[k] >> 44) & 0xF;
      if (!(op >= 1 && op <= 6) && op != 13 && op != 14) continue;
      uint32_t addr = (uint32_t)cfs[k] & 0xFFF, count = (uint32_t)(cfs[k] >> 12) & 7,
               seq = (uint32_t)(cfs[k] >> 16) & 0xFFF;
      if (addr < first_instr) first_instr = addr;
      for (uint32_t j = 0; j < count; j++) {
        if (!((seq >> (2 * j)) & 1)) continue;
        uint32_t g = (addr + j) * 3;
        if (g + 2 < words && (be32(m + g * 4) & 0x1F) == 0) memset(m + g * 4, 0, 12);
      }
    }
  }
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
  for (int i = 1; i < argc; i++) {
    FILE* f = fopen(argv[i], "rb");
    if (!f) continue;
    fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
    long n = ftell(f);
    fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET);
    unsigned char* d = malloc(n);
    if (fread(d, 1, n, f) != (size_t)n) { fclose(f); free(d); continue; }
    fclose(f);
    uint32_t virt = be32(d + 4), hdr = be32(d + 24);
    uint32_t start = virt + be32(d + hdr), size = be32(d + hdr + 4);
    unsigned char* m = malloc(size);
    memcpy(m, d + start, size);
    if (be32(d) & 1) mask_vertex_fetches(m, size / 4);
    printf("%016llX %016llX %s\n", (unsigned long long)XXH3_64bits(d + start, size),
           (unsigned long long)XXH3_64bits(m, size), argv[i]);
    free(m);
    free(d);
  }
  return 0;
}

```

`port/tools/shaderlib/vsmatch.py`:

```py
"""Match run-time vertex shader microcode (SDK dump, SDNX_DUMP_UCODE) to Xenos containers.

Direct3D rewrites the vertex fetch instructions of a vertex shader at bind (destination swizzle, format,
stride, offset, prefetch / mini-fetch flags). The fetch instructions are found exactly by parsing the
control flow (exec clauses, sequence bit 0 = fetch, word 0 opcode 0 = vertex fetch); every other instruction
must match bit for bit, and in vertex fetches the opcode, source and destination registers and constant index
(word 0 bits 0-26) must match. Prints how many run-time shaders have a candidate and whether the candidates
are interchangeable for translation.
Usage: vsmatch.py <containers dir> <runtime dir> [variants out dir]
"""
import collections
import glob
import os
import struct
import sys


def be32(d, o):
    return struct.unpack_from(">I", d, o)[0]


EXEC_OPS = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 13, 14}


def vfetch_addresses(w):
    """Instruction addresses (units of 3 words) of the vertex fetches, from the control flow."""
    out = set()
```
