```py
    first_instr = None
    i = 0
    while True:
        if first_instr is not None and i * 3 >= first_instr * 3:
            break
        if i * 3 + 2 >= len(w):
            break
        a, b, c = w[i * 3:i * 3 + 3]
        for cf in (a | ((b & 0xFFFF) << 32), (b >> 16) | (c << 16)):
            op = (cf >> 44) & 0xF
            if op in EXEC_OPS:
                addr, count, seq = cf & 0xFFF, (cf >> 12) & 7, (cf >> 16) & 0xFFF
                if first_instr is None or addr < first_instr:
                    first_instr = addr
                for k in range(count):
                    if (seq >> (2 * k)) & 1:
                        g = (addr + k) * 3
                        if g + 2 < len(w) and (w[g] & 0x1F) == 0:
                            out.add(addr + k)
        i += 1
    return out


def micro(d):
    virt, hdr = be32(d, 4), be32(d, 24)
    st, sz = virt + be32(d, hdr), be32(d, hdr + 4)
    return st, sz, struct.unpack(">%dI" % (sz // 4), d[st:st + sz])


def matches(cw, fetches, w, word0_mask):
    if len(cw) != len(w):
        return False
    for g in range(0, len(w), 3):
        a, b = cw[g:g + 3], w[g:g + 3]
        if a == b:
            continue
        if g // 3 not in fetches:
            return False
        if (a[0] & word0_mask) != (b[0] & word0_mask):
            return False
    return True


def main(cdir, rdir, vout=None):
    lib = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(cdir, "vs_*.bin")):
        d = open(p, "rb").read()
        st, sz, w = micro(d)
        lib[len(w)].append((os.path.basename(p)[:-4], w, vfetch_addresses(w), d, st, sz))
    stats = collections.Counter()
    pairs = []
    for p in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(rdir, "*.ucode.bin.vert"))):
        r = open(p, "rb").read()
        n = len(r) // 4
        w = struct.unpack("<%dI" % n, r)
        h = os.path.basename(p).split("_")[1].split(".")[0]
        strict = [x for x in lib[n] if matches(x[1], x[2], w, (1 << 27) - 1)]
        regs = [x for x in lib[n] if matches(x[1], x[2], w, (1 << 20) - 1)]
        loose = [x for x in lib[n] if matches(x[1], x[2], w, 0x1F)]
        m = strict or regs or loose
        stats["strict" if strict else "regs" if regs else "loose" if loose else "none"] += 1
        if m:
            name, cw, fetches, d, st, sz = m[0]
            pairs.append((h, name))
            if vout:
                dd = bytearray(d)
                dd[st:st + sz] = struct.pack(">%dI" % n, *w)
                open(os.path.join(vout, "vs_%s.bin" % h), "wb").write(dd)
    print(dict(stats))
    if vout:
        with open(os.path.join(vout, "pairs.txt"), "w", newline="\n") as f:
            f.write("".join("%s %s\n" % x for x in pairs))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 3:
        sys.exit(__doc__)
    main(*sys.argv[1:4])

```

`port/tools/xtex.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Xbox 360 (Xenos) 2D texture helpers: tiling, 8-in-16 endian swap, DXT1/DXT5 decode and DXT5 encode.

Tiling is XGAddress2DTiledX/Y (the formula Xenia uses), applied on 4x4 blocks for DXT formats. Tiled
surfaces are stored with both dimensions aligned to 32 blocks.
"""
import numpy as np


def _log_bpp(texel_pitch):
    return (texel_pitch >> 2) + ((texel_pitch >> 1) >> (texel_pitch >> 2))


def tiled_xy(offset, width, texel_pitch):
    aligned_w = (width + 31) & ~31
    lb = _log_bpp(texel_pitch)
    ob = offset << lb
    ot = ((ob & ~4095) >> 3) + ((ob & 1792) >> 2) + (ob & 63)
    om = ot >> (7 + lb)
    macro_x = (om % (aligned_w >> 5)) << 2
    tile = ((((ot >> (5 + lb)) & 2) + (ob >> 6)) & 3)
    macro = (macro_x + tile) << 3
    micro = ((((ot >> 1) & ~15) + (ot & 15)) & ((texel_pitch << 3) - 1)) >> lb
    x = macro + micro

    macro_y = (om // (aligned_w >> 5)) << 2
    tile = ((ot >> (6 + lb)) & 1) + ((ob & 2048) >> 10)
    macro = (macro_y + tile) << 3
    micro = ((((ot & (((texel_pitch << 6) - 1) & ~31)) + ((ot & 15) << 1)) >> (3 + lb)) & ~1)
    y = macro + micro + ((ot & 16) >> 4)
    return x, y


def swap16(b: bytes) -> bytes:
    a = bytearray(b)
    a[0::2], a[1::2] = b[1::2], b[0::2]
    return bytes(a)


def untile_blocks(data: bytes, wb: int, hb: int, bsize: int) -> bytes:
    """Tiled block stream (aligned to 32x32 blocks) -> linear wb x hb blocks."""
    aw = (wb + 31) & ~31
    ah = (hb + 31) & ~31
    out = bytearray(wb * hb * bsize)
    for i in range(aw * ah):
        x, y = tiled_xy(i, aw, bsize)
        if x < wb and y < hb:
            out[(y * wb + x) * bsize:(y * wb + x + 1) * bsize] = data[i * bsize:(i + 1) * bsize]
    return bytes(out)


def tile_blocks(lin: bytes, wb: int, hb: int, bsize: int, total: int, base: bytes = None) -> bytes:
    """Linear blocks -> tiled stream of `total` bytes; `base` (an existing tiled stream) keeps its padding."""
    aw = (wb + 31) & ~31
    ah = (hb + 31) & ~31
    out = bytearray(base[:total]) if base is not None else bytearray(total)
    for i in range(aw * ah):
        x, y = tiled_xy(i, aw, bsize)
        if x < wb and y < hb and (i + 1) * bsize <= total:
            out[i * bsize:(i + 1) * bsize] = lin[(y * wb + x) * bsize:(y * wb + x + 1) * bsize]
    return bytes(out)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- DXT (little-endian blocks)

def _rgb565(c):
    r = (c >> 11) & 31
    g = (c >> 5) & 63
    b = c & 31
    return np.array([(r << 3) | (r >> 2), (g << 2) | (g >> 4), (b << 3) | (b >> 2)], dtype=np.int32)


def _color_block(blk, out, dxt1):
    c0 = blk[0] | (blk[1] << 8)
    c1 = blk[2] | (blk[3] << 8)
    p0, p1 = _rgb565(c0), _rgb565(c1)
    pal = [p0, p1]
    alpha = [255, 255, 255, 255]
    if c0 > c1 or not dxt1:
        pal += [(2 * p0 + p1) // 3, (p0 + 2 * p1) // 3]
    else:
        pal += [(p0 + p1) // 2, np.zeros(3, np.int32)]
        alpha[3] = 0
    bits = blk[4] | (blk[5] << 8) | (blk[6] << 16) | (blk[7] << 24)
    for i in range(16):
        k = (bits >> (2 * i)) & 3
        out[i // 4, i % 4, :3] = pal[k]
        if dxt1:
            out[i // 4, i % 4, 3] = alpha[k]


def _alpha_block(blk, out):
    a0, a1 = blk[0], blk[1]
    pal = [a0, a1]
    if a0 > a1:
        pal += [((6 - i) * a0 + (1 + i) * a1) // 7 for i in range(6)]
    else:
        pal += [((4 - i) * a0 + (1 + i) * a1) // 5 for i in range(4)] + [0, 255]
    bits = int.from_bytes(blk[2:8], "little")
    for i in range(16):
        out[i // 4, i % 4, 3] = pal[(bits >> (3 * i)) & 7]


def decode_dxt(lin: bytes, w: int, h: int, fmt: str) -> np.ndarray:
    bsize = 8 if fmt == "DXT1" else 16
    wb, hb = (w + 3) // 4, (h + 3) // 4
    img = np.zeros((hb * 4, wb * 4, 4), np.uint8)
    tmp = np.zeros((4, 4, 4), np.int32)
    for by in range(hb):
        for bx in range(wb):
            o = (by * wb + bx) * bsize
            blk = lin[o:o + bsize]
            tmp[...] = 255
            if fmt == "DXT1":
                _color_block(blk, tmp, True)
            else:
                _alpha_block(blk[:8], tmp)
                _color_block(blk[8:], tmp, False)
            img[by * 4:by * 4 + 4, bx * 4:bx * 4 + 4] = tmp
    return img[:h, :w]


def _to565(c):
    r, g, b = [int(v) for v in c]
    return ((r * 31 + 127) // 255 << 11) | ((g * 63 + 127) // 255 << 5) | ((b * 31 + 127) // 255)


def encode_dxt5(img: np.ndarray) -> bytes:
    """Straightforward DXT5 encoder (bounding-box endpoints, nearest palette entry). Good enough for flat UI icons."""
    h, w = img.shape[:2]
    wb, hb = (w + 3) // 4, (h + 3) // 4
    out = bytearray()
    for by in range(hb):
        for bx in range(wb):
            blk = img[by * 4:by * 4 + 4, bx * 4:bx * 4 + 4].reshape(-1, 4).astype(np.int32)
            # alpha
            a = blk[:, 3]
            a0, a1 = int(a.max()), int(a.min())
            if a0 == a1:
                out += bytes([a0, a1]) + b"\0" * 6
            else:
                pal = [a0, a1] + [((6 - i) * a0 + (1 + i) * a1) // 7 for i in range(6)]
                bits = 0
                for i, v in enumerate(a):
                    k = min(range(8), key=lambda j: abs(pal[j] - v))
                    bits |= k << (3 * i)
                out += bytes([a0, a1]) + bits.to_bytes(6, "little")
            # colour: weight by alpha so transparent texels do not pull the endpoints
            rgb = blk[:, :3]
            vis = rgb[a > 16] if (a > 16).any() else rgb
            lo, hi = vis.min(axis=0), vis.max(axis=0)
            c0, c1 = _to565(hi), _to565(lo)
            if c0 < c1:
                c0, c1 = c1, c0
            if c0 == c1:
                out += c0.to_bytes(2, "little") + c1.to_bytes(2, "little") + b"\0" * 4
                continue
            p0, p1 = _rgb565(c0), _rgb565(c1)
            pal = [p0, p1, (2 * p0 + p1) // 3, (p0 + 2 * p1) // 3]
            bits = 0
            for i, v in enumerate(rgb):
                k = min(range(4), key=lambda j: int(((pal[j] - v) ** 2).sum()))
                bits |= k << (2 * i)
            out += c0.to_bytes(2, "little") + c1.to_bytes(2, "little") + bits.to_bytes(4, "little")
    return bytes(out)

```

`renderer/include/native_command_processor.h`:

```h
/**
 * Sleeping Dogs port (SDNX): native renderer command processor (stage 2b: EDRAM-lite, resolves, output).
 */
#pragma once

#include <array>
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <type_traits>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <vector>

#include <rex/graphics/command_processor.h>
#include <rex/graphics/pipeline/shader/shader.h>
#include <rex/graphics/util/draw.h>
#include <rex/graphics/xenos.h>
#include <rex/ui/vulkan/api.h>

namespace rex::ui::vulkan {
class VulkanDevice;
}

namespace rex::graphics::sdnx {

class NativeCommandProcessor : public CommandProcessor {
 public:
  NativeCommandProcessor(GraphicsSystem* graphics_system, system::KernelState* kernel_state);
  ~NativeCommandProcessor() override;

  void IssueSwap(uint32_t frontbuffer_ptr, uint32_t frontbuffer_width,
                 uint32_t frontbuffer_height) override;

 protected:
  bool SetupContext() override;
  void ShutdownContext() override;

  Shader* LoadShader(xenos::ShaderType shader_type, uint32_t guest_address,
                     const uint32_t* host_address, uint32_t dword_count) override;
  // Registers from 0x2000 up (context state, ALU / fetch / bool / loop constants) have no side effects in the base
  // WriteRegister (its special cases are all below 0x2000): stored directly, without its per-register lookup.
  void WriteRegister(uint32_t index, uint32_t value) override;
  void WriteRegistersFromMem(uint32_t start_index, uint32_t* base, uint32_t num_registers) override;
  bool IssueDraw(xenos::PrimitiveType prim_type, uint32_t index_count,
                 IndexBufferInfo* index_buffer_info, bool major_mode_explicit) override;
  bool IssueCopy() override;
  bool ExecutePacketType3_EVENT_WRITE_ZPD(memory::RingBuffer* reader, uint32_t packet, uint32_t count) override;

 private:
  static constexpr uint32_t kFramesInFlight = 3;

  // A host image (all kept in VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL in this stage).
  struct Image {
    VkImage image = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkDeviceMemory memory = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkImageView view = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkFormat format = VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED;
    VkImageAspectFlags aspect = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
    uint32_t width = 0, height = 0;
    bool initialized = false;  // transitioned from UNDEFINED to GENERAL
    bool drawn = false;        // some draw targeted this image (diagnostic)
  };

  // EDRAM-lite: one host image per (base, pitch, format, MSAA, depth); pixel (x, y) of the surface is at
  // (x, y) of the image (MSAA kept at one sample per pixel in this stage).
  static uint64_t RenderTargetKey(uint32_t base_tiles, uint32_t pitch_pixels, uint32_t format,
                                  xenos::MsaaSamples msaa, bool is_depth);

  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* vulkan_device() const;
  bool CreateImage(Image& image, VkFormat format, uint32_t width, uint32_t height, bool depth);
  void DestroyImage(Image& image);
  // Image in GENERAL layout, at least width x height (recreated bigger if needed, contents lost).
  Image* GetRenderTarget(uint32_t base_tiles, uint32_t pitch_pixels, uint32_t format, xenos::MsaaSamples msaa,
                         bool is_depth, uint32_t min_height);
  Image* GetResolved(uint32_t address, VkFormat format, uint32_t width, uint32_t height);
  void InitializeLayout(Image& image);
  // Images replaced while the current frame may still reference them are destroyed once that frame's
  // fence has passed (destroying them at once is a use-after-free in the command buffer).
  void RetireImage(Image& image);
  std::array<std::vector<Image>, kFramesInFlight> retired_images_;
  bool dummies_initialized_ = false;
  void FullBarrier();
  // Depth resolve (k_24_8 / k_24_8_FLOAT textures): the depth aspect of the render target goes through the
  // upload buffer into an R32_SFLOAT resolved image (the [0, 1] depth, as the guest texture reads it); MSAA
  // targets (in samples) are reduced with a nearest blit, which picks one sample per pixel.
  void ResolveDepth(const Image& source, const draw_util::ResolveInfo& info, uint32_t x0, uint32_t y0,
                    uint32_t width, uint32_t height, uint32_t sx, uint32_t sy);
  Image depth_resolve_temp_;
  // Stencil of depth resolves (R8), by destination base: needed to restore the depth buffer from the texture.
  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, Image> resolved_stencil_;
  // The game restores depth / stencil after the tiled G-buffer by drawing the resolved depth texture as
  // k_8_8_8_8 colour into the EDRAM range of the depth buffer (colour and depth alias there). Colour and depth
  // are separate images here, so that draw is replaced by a copy into the depth image. True if handled.
  bool TryRestoreDepth(Shader* ps);

  void EnsureFrame();
  bool SubmitFrame();
  bool CreatePresentPipeline();
  void ReportStats();

  VkCommandPool command_pool_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  std::array<VkCommandBuffer, kFramesInFlight> command_buffers_{};
  std::array<VkFence, kFramesInFlight> fences_{};
  uint64_t frame_ = 0;
  bool frame_open_ = false;
  VkCommandBuffer cb_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;

  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, Image> render_targets_;
  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, Image> resolved_;  // by physical destination base

  // Output: compute copy of the front buffer into the guest output image.
  VkDescriptorSetLayout present_set_layout_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  VkPipelineLayout present_pipeline_layout_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  VkPipeline present_pipeline_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  VkDescriptorPool present_descriptor_pool_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  std::array<VkDescriptorSet, kFramesInFlight> present_sets_{};
  VkSampler linear_sampler_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;

  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, std::unique_ptr<Shader>> shaders_;

  // ---- Draws (native_draw.cpp) ----
  struct FetchSlot {
    uint16_t address;   // container instruction address of vertex fetch K
    uint8_t reg, mask;  // destination register, written components
    uint8_t location;   // vertex input location (0xFF: none)
  };
  struct LibraryEntry {
    std::string name;
    bool vertex = false;
    std::vector<FetchSlot> fetches;
    VkShaderModule module = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    bool load_failed = false;
    // "pull" in the index line: the shader reads its vertices itself by a computed index (hand-made instancing),
    // from the upload buffer (set 4 binding 3) with the per-fetch offset / stride / format of the shared block.
    bool pull = false;
  };
  struct VertexFetchRuntime {
    uint32_t address;  // run-time instruction address
    uint32_t w0, w1, w2;
  };
  struct VertexShaderInfo {
    uint64_t masked_key = 0;
    std::vector<VertexFetchRuntime> fetches;
  };
  struct UploadBuffer {
    VkBuffer buffer = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkDeviceMemory memory = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    uint8_t* mapping = nullptr;
    VkDeviceSize size = 0, offset = 0;
  };
  // 64 MB overflowed in gameplay (22-42 MB of vertex copies + ~20 MB of constants per frame): draws were skipped.
  // TODO: back to 64 MB or less once the geometry cache and the constant uploads are efficient (Switch memory).
#ifdef __SWITCH__
  // Switch: 3 x 128 MB (a gameplay frame takes ~40 MB: ~14 MB of vertex copies + constants).
  static constexpr VkDeviceSize kUploadBufferSize = VkDeviceSize(128) << 20;
#else
  static constexpr VkDeviceSize kUploadBufferSize = VkDeviceSize(256) << 20;
#endif
  // 688 + 16 vertex fetches x {byte offset in the upload buffer, stride in bytes, VkFormat, vertex count} for the
  // shaders that pull their vertices (bytes 688-943, see sdnxPullFetch in shader_common.h).
  static constexpr uint32_t kSharedBlockSize = 944;
  static constexpr uint32_t kPullInfoOffset = 688;
  VkDeviceSize pull_range_ = 0;  // bytes of the upload buffer the pulling shaders can read (set 4 binding 3)

  bool InitializeDraws();
  void ShutdownDraws();
  bool LoadLibrary();
  LibraryEntry* FindVertexShader(Shader* shader, const VertexShaderInfo*& info);
  LibraryEntry* FindPixelShader(Shader* shader);
  VkShaderModule GetModule(LibraryEntry& entry);
  // Bytes in the frame's upload buffer; returns the offset or UINT64_MAX when full.
  VkDeviceSize Upload(const void* data, VkDeviceSize size, VkDeviceSize alignment, uint8_t** mapping_out = nullptr);
  void EndRendering();
  bool DrawNative(xenos::PrimitiveType prim_type, uint32_t index_count, IndexBufferInfo* index_buffer_info);

  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, LibraryEntry> library_vs_, library_ps_;
  std::unordered_map<const Shader*, VertexShaderInfo> vs_info_;
  std::array<UploadBuffer, kFramesInFlight> upload_{};
  VkDescriptorSetLayout heap_layouts_[4] = {};  // Texture2D, Texture3D, TextureCube, samplers (sets 0-3)
  VkDescriptorSetLayout constants_layout_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;  // set 4: 3 dynamic UBOs
  VkDescriptorPool draw_descriptor_pool_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  VkDescriptorSet heap_sets_[4] = {};
  std::array<VkDescriptorSet, kFramesInFlight> constants_sets_{};
  VkPipelineLayout draw_pipeline_layout_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  Image dummy_2d_, dummy_3d_, dummy_cube_;
  VkSampler dummy_sampler_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, VkPipeline> pipelines_;
  // Current dynamic rendering pass.
  bool rendering_ = false;
  uint64_t rendering_key_ = 0;

  // ---- Textures (native_texture.cpp) ----
  // A guest texture loaded into a host image: all its stored levels (base and mips), 2D or cube. Reloaded when
  // the guest writes to its pages (physical memory write watch, per-page version counters).
  struct Texture {
    Image image;
    uint32_t levels = 1, layers = 1, depth = 1;
    uint32_t page_first = 0, page_count = 0;  // base pages
    uint32_t mip_page_first = 0, mip_page_count = 0;
    uint64_t version = 0;              // sum of the page versions when it was loaded
    uint64_t validated_frame = ~0ull;  // frame of the last version check
    bool loaded = false, failed = false;
    // Cube maps the game renders (6 faces resolved to its memory, which the native renderer never writes):
    // level 0 of each face is copied from the resolved images (see OverlayResolvedFaces).
    uint64_t face_generation = ~0ull;
    bool faces_resolved = false, faces_swapped = false;
    // XXH3 of the guest pages it was loaded from: a write elsewhere in those pages changes the version without
    // changing the texture (~4 needless reloads a frame in gameplay, 12 % of the GPU thread on the console).
    uint64_t content_hash = 0;
  };
  uint64_t TextureContentHash(const Texture& texture);
  std::vector<uint8_t> texture_scratch_;  // LoadTexture: the byte-swapped guest data of one slice
  // Graphics state already recorded in the current command buffer (it survives rendering passes, copies and
  // clears; the present uses the compute bind point): only changes are recorded. Each vkCmdSet* costs CPU time in
  // the driver (NVK copies and flushes its dynamic state). Reset by vkBeginCommandBuffer.
  struct BoundState {
    VkPipeline pipeline = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkViewport viewport = {};
    VkRect2D scissor = {};
    float blend[4] = {-1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f, -1.0f};
    uint32_t stencil[6] = {~0u, ~0u, ~0u, ~0u, ~0u, ~0u};
    float bias[2] = {-1e30f, -1e30f};
    bool heaps = false, viewport_set = false, scissor_set = false;
    VkBuffer index_buffer = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    VkDeviceSize index_offset = 0;
    VkBuffer vertex_buffers[16] = {};
    VkDeviceSize vertex_offsets[16] = {};
    uint32_t vertex_count = 0;
  } bound_;
  // Format of the view GetTextureDescriptor chose last (for the sampler: no linear filter where unsupported).
  VkFormat last_texture_format_ = VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED;
  std::unordered_map<int, bool> filterable_formats_;
  bool FormatFilterable(VkFormat format);
  // Copies the resolved images of the faces of a cube texture into it; true if the cube comes from resolves.
  bool OverlayResolvedFaces(Texture& texture, const xenos::xe_gpu_texture_fetch_t& fetch);
  struct TextureView {
    VkImageView view = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    uint32_t heap = 0, index = 0;  // heap 0 2D, 1 3D, 2 cube
  };
  void InitializeTextures();
  void ShutdownTextures();
  // Fills the texture and sampler parts of the shared block for the textures the shaders of the draw use.
  // May end the rendering pass (texture uploads are transfers).
  void BindTextures(Shader* vs, Shader* ps, uint32_t* shared);
  // Heap index (with the sign bits) of the view of a texture for a fetch constant, 0 for the placeholder.
  uint32_t GetTextureDescriptor(uint32_t fetch_constant, uint32_t& heap, float inv_size[2]);
  uint32_t GetSamplerDescriptor(const xenos::xe_gpu_texture_fetch_t& fetch,
                                const ParsedTextureFetchInstruction& instr);
  bool LoadTexture(Texture& texture, const xenos::xe_gpu_texture_fetch_t& fetch);
  uint64_t TexturePagesVersion(const Texture& texture) const;
  // view_format: VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED for the image format (an SNORM view of a mutable-format texture otherwise).
  uint32_t GetView(const Image& image, uint32_t heap, uint32_t levels, uint32_t layers, uint32_t swizzle,
                   uint32_t host_swizzle, VkFormat view_format = VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED);
  // Views of an image being retired go with it (their heap indices are reused after the frame completes).
  void RetireViews(VkImage image);
  void ReleaseRetiredViews(uint32_t slot);
  static std::pair<uint32_t, uint32_t> PhysicalWriteCallback(void* context, uint32_t start, uint32_t length,
                                                            bool exact);

  // Resolved images are allocated with the destination pitch alignment (32), textures read them with their exact
  // size: a texture-sized copy keeps the normalized coordinates right (the menu blur chain came out striped).
  // Also: the game renders the 1200x704 scene as two 1200x352 halves (the G-buffer does not fit the EDRAM) and
  // resolves the second half to the address of row 352 of the same texture - the copy is assembled from every
  // resolve that landed inside the texture's memory range (parts).
  struct ResolvedPart {
    uint32_t address, x, y;
  };
  struct ResolvedCopy {
    Image image;
    uint64_t generation = ~0ull;
    std::vector<ResolvedPart> parts;
    size_t parts_resolved_count = ~size_t(0);  // resolved_.size() when the parts were found
    uint64_t parts_frame = ~0ull;              // frame they were found in
  };
  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, ResolvedCopy> resolved_copies_;
  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, uint64_t> resolved_generation_;
  // RB_COPY_DEST_INFO.copy_dest_swap of the last colour resolve to that address: Xenos then writes red and blue
  // swapped. The image keeps them unswapped; texture views swap them back (the AO / shadow collector of the scene
  // composite is resolved that way, its .z read 0 and the whole frame came out black - 2026-11-12).
  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, bool> resolved_swap_;
  // Frame of the last resolve to each address: a texture assembled from several resolves (ResolvedCopy.parts)
  // takes only parts as fresh as its base. resolved_ keeps images from long ago (menus, other scenes); one of them
  // inside the scene texture's range was pasted over the right 1/16 of the frame (lighter strip, 2026-11-12).
  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, uint64_t> resolved_frame_;
  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, uint32_t> resolved_extent_;  // destination base -> bytes the resolve covers  // by destination base, bumped by every resolve
  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, Texture> textures_;
  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, TextureView> views_;                  // (image, heap, swizzle) -> view
  std::unordered_map<VkImage, std::vector<uint64_t>> image_views_;  // image -> keys in views_
  std::array<std::vector<TextureView>, kFramesInFlight> retired_views_;
  std::vector<uint32_t> free_indices_[3];
  uint32_t next_index_[3] = {1, 1, 1};
  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, uint32_t> samplers_;  // key -> sampler heap index
  std::vector<VkSampler> sampler_objects_;
  std::unique_ptr<std::atomic<uint32_t>[]> page_versions_;
  void* write_watch_handle_ = nullptr;
  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, uint32_t> unsupported_formats_;

  // ---- Vertex / index cache (native_geometry.cpp) ----
  bool InitializeGeometry();
  void ShutdownGeometry();
  // Offset in geometry_buffer_ of the host copy of guest memory [address, address + size) (fill writes host_size
  // bytes of it); UINT64_MAX when the caller has to upload it for this draw (dynamic data, cache full).
  // A non-owning reference to the fill callback: std::function allocated the lambda's captures on the heap on
  // every call (each binding and index buffer of every draw; malloc/free were 16 % of the GPU thread on Switch).
  class FillRef {
   public:
    template <typename F>
    FillRef(F&& f) : object_(const_cast<void*>(static_cast<const void*>(&f))),
                     call_([](void* object, uint8_t* out) { (*static_cast<std::remove_reference_t<F>*>(object))(out); }) {}
    void operator()(uint8_t* out) const { call_(object_, out); }

   private:
    void* object_;
    void (*call_)(void*, uint8_t*);
  };
  VkDeviceSize CachedGeometry(uint64_t key, uint32_t address, uint32_t size, VkDeviceSize host_size, FillRef fill);
  VkDeviceSize AllocateGeometry(VkDeviceSize size);
  void FreeGeometry(VkDeviceSize offset, VkDeviceSize size);
  void ReleaseRetiredGeometry(uint32_t slot);  // also evicts entries unused for a long time
  uint64_t PagesVersion(uint32_t first, uint32_t count) const;
  struct GeometryEntry {
    VkDeviceSize offset = UINT64_MAX, size = 0;
    uint32_t page_first = 0, page_count = 0;
    uint64_t version = 0, epoch = 0, last_change_frame = 0, dynamic_since = 0;
    uint64_t first_seen = 0, last_used = 0;
    uint32_t changes = 0;
    bool dynamic = false;
    uint64_t hot_until = 0;  // refused for hot pages: no page scan again before this frame (content hash path)
  };
  VkBuffer geometry_buffer_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  VkDeviceMemory geometry_memory_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  std::map<VkDeviceSize, VkDeviceSize> geometry_free_;  // offset -> size
  std::array<std::vector<std::pair<VkDeviceSize, VkDeviceSize>>, kFramesInFlight> geometry_retired_;
  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, GeometryEntry> geometry_;
  std::atomic<uint64_t> write_epoch_{0};  // bumped by every physical write callback
  // Frame (low 32 bits, 0 = never) of the last write the callbacks saw on each page: geometry on pages written
  // recently is dynamic (ring buffers) and is not cached or watched.
  std::unique_ptr<std::atomic<uint32_t>[]> page_write_frame_;
  std::atomic<uint32_t> callback_frame_{1};
  // Watched writes seen on the page (saturating): the hot period doubles with each one, because writes to pages
  // that are not watched are invisible - without it a ring buffer page was cached again every 120 frames.
  std::unique_ptr<std::atomic<uint8_t>[]> page_strikes_;
  std::atomic<uint64_t> write_callbacks_{0};
  uint64_t write_callbacks_reported_ = 0;
  bool PagesHot(uint32_t first, uint32_t count) const;

  // Content-hashed copies (second level, for what the page-watched cache refuses: data on pages written recently).
  // Most of a gameplay frame's geometry sat on such pages and was copied and converted again on every draw
  // (vertices + indices ~55 % of the draw's CPU). Key: the guest range and its format; value: XXH3 of the guest bytes
  // and the converted copy in a persistent host-visible buffer (written by the CPU directly: no copy commands, no
  // break of the rendering pass). For indices also their min / max (no scan for the vertex range).
  struct HashedGeometry {
    uint64_t content = 0;
    VkDeviceSize offset = UINT64_MAX, size = 0;
    uint64_t last_used = 0;
    uint32_t index_min = 0, index_max = 0;
    uint64_t hashed_frame = UINT64_MAX;  // last frame its content was hashed (and matched or was filled)
    uint64_t content_since = 0;          // frame the current content was filled
  };
  VkBuffer hashed_buffer_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  VkDeviceMemory hashed_memory_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  uint8_t* hashed_mapping_ = nullptr;
  std::map<VkDeviceSize, VkDeviceSize> hashed_free_;
  std::array<std::vector<std::pair<VkDeviceSize, VkDeviceSize>>, kFramesInFlight> hashed_retired_;
  std::unordered_map<uint64_t, HashedGeometry> hashed_;
  // The entry of `key` holding `content` (allocated and filled by `fill` when absent or different), or nullptr.
  HashedGeometry* HashedCopy(uint64_t key, uint64_t content, VkDeviceSize host_size, FillRef fill);
  // Same frame, stable content: the entry without hashing again (nullptr: hash and call HashedCopy).
  HashedGeometry* HashedReuse(uint64_t key, VkDeviceSize host_size);

  // ---- Occlusion queries (native_query.cpp) ----
  // Without stalls: the guest gets the last known count of that sample-count address at once (1000 = visible
  // before the first one), the real count is read back when the frame's fence has passed (1-3 frames later).
  // A guest query is a host query per rendering pass instance it spans (Vulkan queries cannot cross passes).
  static constexpr uint32_t kMaxQueries = 4096;
  bool InitializeQueries();
  void ShutdownQueries();
  void BeginHostQuery();  // inside a rendering pass, while a guest query is active
  void EndHostQuery();
  void ResetQueries(uint32_t slot);      // after the slot's fence: results to query_results_, pool reset
  void CopyQueryResults(uint32_t slot);  // before the frame's submit
  std::array<VkQueryPool, kFramesInFlight> query_pools_{};
  std::array<VkBuffer, kFramesInFlight> query_readback_{};
  std::array<VkDeviceMemory, kFramesInFlight> query_readback_memory_{};
  std::array<const uint64_t*, kFramesInFlight> query_readback_mapping_{};
  std::array<uint32_t, kFramesInFlight> query_used_{};
  struct PendingQuery {
    uint32_t address;
    std::vector<uint32_t> indices;
  };
  std::array<std::vector<PendingQuery>, kFramesInFlight> pending_queries_;
  bool guest_query_active_ = false, host_query_open_ = false;
  uint32_t guest_query_address_ = 0;
  std::vector<uint32_t> guest_query_indices_;             // in the current frame's pool
  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, uint32_t> query_results_;  // end sample-count address -> samples

  struct Stats {
    uint64_t swaps = 0, draws = 0, indices = 0, copies = 0, copies_depth = 0, clears = 0, shader_loads = 0,
             shader_new = 0, front_found = 0, drawn = 0, missing_vs = 0, missing_ps = 0, skipped = 0,
             new_pipelines = 0, copies_incompatible = 0, pulled = 0;
    uint64_t tex_loads = 0, tex_bytes = 0, tex_resolved = 0, tex_unsupported = 0, tex_signed = 0;
    uint64_t upload_full = 0, geo_first = 0, geo_hot = 0, geo_flagged = 0, geo_changed = 0;
    uint64_t geo_hits = 0, geo_uploads = 0, geo_bytes = 0, geo_dynamic = 0, geo_direct_bytes = 0;
    uint64_t hash_hits = 0, hash_fills = 0, hash_fill_bytes = 0, hash_bytes = 0, hash_whole_bytes = 0, hash_reuse = 0; uint64_t constants_reused = 0, constants_bind_skipped = 0; uint64_t prof_sub[3] = {};
    mutable uint64_t hot_checks = 0, hot_age = 0, hot_strikes = 0, hot_strikes_max = 0;  // PagesHot refusals (SDNX_PROF)  // content-hashed geometry copies
    uint64_t prof_ns[6] = {}, prof_draws = 0;  // SDNX_PROF: time of the parts of DrawNative (steady_clock ticks)
  } stats_;

  // Diagnostic front-buffer dump (SDNX_DUMP_FRONT=N): copies the presented image to a staging buffer once
  // per swap for N swaps, logs per-channel statistics, and writes a PPM next to the shader package.
  void BeginFrontDump(const Image& front);
  void FinishFrontDump();
  // SDNX_DUMP_NTH=N with SDNX_DUMP_ADDR / SDNX_DUMP_AT: dump the N-th resolve to that address within the frame,
  // at the moment of the resolve (an address is often resolved several times a frame).
  void DumpResolveIfRequested(uint32_t dest_base, const Image& dest);
  bool dump_nth_frame_ = false;
  uint32_t dump_nth_count_ = 0;
  VkBuffer dump_buffer_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  VkDeviceMemory dump_memory_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  void* dump_mapping_ = nullptr;
  VkDeviceSize dump_size_ = 0;
  uint32_t dump_width_ = 0, dump_height_ = 0, dump_bpp_ = 0;
  uint32_t front_address_ = 0, dump_address_ = 0;
  Image* last_color_rt_ = nullptr;  // diagnostic: the colour target of the last draw
  int dump_remaining_ = 0;
  bool dump_pending_ = false;
  uint32_t dump_index_ = 0;
  std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point stats_start_;
  // Diagnostic: SDNX_LOG_FRAME=N logs every draw, resolve and clear of swap N (counted from the first swap).
  uint64_t swap_count_ = 0;
  bool log_frame_ = false;
  uint32_t log_draw_ = 0;
};

}  // namespace rex::graphics::sdnx




```

`renderer/include/native_graphics_system.h`:

```h
/**
 * Sleeping Dogs port (SDNX): native renderer backend, skeleton (docs/native-renderer-plan.md in the port).
 *
 * The SDK's CommandProcessor keeps reading the PM4 ring (packets, registers, waits, interrupts, events); only
 * the expensive parts of the Xenos emulation are replaced: draws, resolves, swap, shader loads. No write-watched
 * shared memory, no run-time shader translation. Selected by the cvar sdnx_native_renderer or SDNX_NATIVE=1.
 */
#pragma once

#include <memory>
#include <string>

#include <rex/graphics/command_processor.h>
#include <rex/graphics/graphics_system.h>

namespace rex::graphics::sdnx {

// True when the native renderer is requested (cvar sdnx_native_renderer or environment SDNX_NATIVE=1; the
// cvar may not reach a plugin DLL on the desktop, the environment always does).
bool NativeRendererRequested();

class NativeGraphicsSystem : public GraphicsSystem {
 public:
  NativeGraphicsSystem();
  ~NativeGraphicsSystem() override;

  std::string name() const override;

 protected:
  void CreateProvider(bool with_presentation) override;

 private:
  std::unique_ptr<CommandProcessor> CreateCommandProcessor() override;
};

}  // namespace rex::graphics::sdnx

```

`renderer/src/native_command_processor.cpp`:

```cpp
/**
 * Sleeping Dogs port (SDNX): native renderer command processor (stage 2b).
 *
 * The base CommandProcessor parses the PM4 ring. Here:
 *  - EDRAM-lite: host images per render target configuration (no format aliasing yet);
 *  - resolves (IssueCopy): blit of the resolve rectangle into a host "resolved texture" keyed by the physical
 *    destination address, then the clears the resolve requests;
 *  - swap: the front buffer (texture fetch constant 0, as Direct3D sets it) is copied by a compute shader into
 *    the presenter's guest output image;
 *  - draws: counted only (next step);
 *  - statistics every 5 s.
 * All work of a frame goes into one command buffer, submitted at the swap.
 */
#include <rex/graphics/sdnx/native_command_processor.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <filesystem>
#include <limits>
#include <string>

#include <rex/graphics/graphics_system.h>
#include <rex/graphics/register_file.h>
#include <rex/graphics/registers.h>
#include <rex/graphics/util/draw.h>
#include <rex/hash.h>
#include <rex/logging.h>
#include <rex/platform.h>
#include <rex/string/buffer.h>
#include <rex/ui/vulkan/device.h>
#include <rex/ui/vulkan/presenter.h>
#include <rex/ui/vulkan/provider.h>
#include <rex/ui/vulkan/util.h>

#include "shaders/present_cs.h"

namespace rex::graphics::sdnx {

namespace {

VkFormat ColorTargetFormat(xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat format) {
  switch (format) {
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_8_8_8_8:
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_8_8_8_8_GAMMA:
      return VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM;
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_2_10_10_10:
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_2_10_10_10_AS_10_10_10_10:
      return VK_FORMAT_A2B10G10R10_UNORM_PACK32;
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_16_16:
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_16_16_FLOAT:
      return VK_FORMAT_R16G16_SFLOAT;
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_32_FLOAT:
      return VK_FORMAT_R32_SFLOAT;
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_32_32_FLOAT:
      return VK_FORMAT_R32G32_SFLOAT;
    default:  // 7e3, 16_16_16_16 (fixed and float)
      return VK_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT;
  }
}

VkFormat ResolveDestFormat(xenos::TextureFormat format, VkFormat source) {
  switch (format) {
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_8_8_8_8:
      return VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM;
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_2_10_10_10:
      return VK_FORMAT_A2B10G10R10_UNORM_PACK32;
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_16_16_16_16:
      return VK_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_UNORM;
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_16_16_16_16_FLOAT:
      return VK_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT;
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_16_16:
      return VK_FORMAT_R16G16_UNORM;
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_16_16_FLOAT:
      return VK_FORMAT_R16G16_SFLOAT;
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_32_FLOAT:
      return VK_FORMAT_R32_SFLOAT;
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_32_32_FLOAT:
      return VK_FORMAT_R32G32_SFLOAT;
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_8:
      return VK_FORMAT_R8_UNORM;
    case xenos::TextureFormat::k_8_8:
      return VK_FORMAT_R8G8_UNORM;
    default:
      return source;
  }
}

// RB_COLOR_CLEAR is the packed value in the render target format.
VkClearColorValue DecodeColorClear(xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat format, uint32_t lo, uint32_t hi) {
  VkClearColorValue c = {};
  switch (format) {
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_8_8_8_8:
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_8_8_8_8_GAMMA:
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) c.float32[i] = float((lo >> (8 * i)) & 0xFF) / 255.0f;
      break;
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_2_10_10_10:
    case xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_2_10_10_10_AS_10_10_10_10:
      for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) c.float32[i] = float((lo >> (10 * i)) & 0x3FF) / 1023.0f;
      c.float32[3] = float(lo >> 30) / 3.0f;
      break;
    default:
      // Float formats: zero is by far the most common clear; exact decoding comes with EDRAM aliasing.
      (void)hi;
      break;
  }
  return c;
}

}  // namespace

void NativeCommandProcessor::WriteRegister(uint32_t index, uint32_t value) {
  if (index >= 0x2000 && index < RegisterFile::kRegisterCount) {
    register_file_->values[index] = value;
    return;
  }
  CommandProcessor::WriteRegister(index, value);
}

void NativeCommandProcessor::WriteRegistersFromMem(uint32_t start_index, uint32_t* base, uint32_t num_registers) {
  if (start_index >= 0x2000 && start_index + num_registers <= RegisterFile::kRegisterCount) {
    uint32_t* dst = &register_file_->values[start_index];
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < num_registers; ++i) dst[i] = __builtin_bswap32(base[i]);
    return;
  }
  CommandProcessor::WriteRegistersFromMem(start_index, base, num_registers);
}

NativeCommandProcessor::NativeCommandProcessor(GraphicsSystem* graphics_system,
                                               system::KernelState* kernel_state)
    : CommandProcessor(graphics_system, kernel_state) {}

NativeCommandProcessor::~NativeCommandProcessor() = default;

const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* NativeCommandProcessor::vulkan_device() const {
  return static_cast<const ui::vulkan::VulkanProvider*>(graphics_system_->provider())->vulkan_device();
}

uint64_t NativeCommandProcessor::RenderTargetKey(uint32_t base_tiles, uint32_t pitch_pixels, uint32_t format,
                                                 xenos::MsaaSamples msaa, bool is_depth) {
  // Key by the *host* format and the depth flag, not by the guest enum: the guest has aliases that map to
  // one host format (k_8_8_8_8 and k_8_8_8_8_GAMMA both to R8G8B8A8, 7e3 and 16_16_16_16 both to
  // R16G16B16A16_SFLOAT). Keying by the guest value made the draws and the resolve use two separate images
  // with the same address, so the resolve read an empty one and the frame stayed black.
  uint32_t host_format = is_depth ? 0x3Fu : uint32_t(ColorTargetFormat(xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat(format)));
  // Fields: base 12 bits, pitch 16 bits, host format 8 bits (VkFormat values of the color formats used are
  // < 256), MSAA 2 bits, depth 1 bit - the format used to spill into the MSAA bits.
  return uint64_t(base_tiles & 0xFFF) | (uint64_t(pitch_pixels & 0xFFFF) << 12) |
         (uint64_t(host_format & 0xFF) << 28) | (uint64_t(msaa) << 36) | (uint64_t(is_depth) << 38);
}

bool NativeCommandProcessor::CreateImage(Image& image, VkFormat format, uint32_t width, uint32_t height,
                                         bool depth) {
  static const bool trace_image = std::getenv("SDNX_TRACE_DRAW") != nullptr;
  if (trace_image) {
    REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_trace] create image {}x{} format {} depth={}", width, height, int(format), depth);
  }
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* device = vulkan_device();
  VkImageCreateInfo info = {};
  info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_CREATE_INFO;
  info.imageType = VK_IMAGE_TYPE_2D;
  info.format = format;
  info.extent = {width, height, 1};
  info.mipLevels = 1;
  info.arrayLayers = 1;
  info.samples = VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT;
  info.tiling = VK_IMAGE_TILING_OPTIMAL;
  info.usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT | VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT | VK_IMAGE_USAGE_SAMPLED_BIT |
               (depth ? VK_IMAGE_USAGE_DEPTH_STENCIL_ATTACHMENT_BIT : VK_IMAGE_USAGE_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_BIT);
  info.sharingMode = VK_SHARING_MODE_EXCLUSIVE;
  info.initialLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
  if (trace_image) {
    REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_trace]   vkCreateImage {}x{} format {} usage {:#x}", width, height, int(format), info.usage);
  }
  if (!ui::vulkan::util::CreateDedicatedAllocationImage(device, info, ui::vulkan::util::MemoryPurpose::kDeviceLocal,
                                                        image.image, image.memory)) {
    REXGPU_ERROR("[sdnx_native] could not create a {}x{} image (format {})", width, height, int(format));
    return false;
  }
  image.format = format;
  image.width = width;
  image.height = height;
  image.aspect = depth ? (VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_DEPTH_BIT | VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_STENCIL_BIT) : VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
  image.initialized = false;
  VkImageViewCreateInfo view = {};
  view.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_VIEW_CREATE_INFO;
  view.image = image.image;
  view.viewType = VK_IMAGE_VIEW_TYPE_2D;
  view.format = format;
  view.subresourceRange = {
      depth ? VkImageAspectFlags(VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_DEPTH_BIT | VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_STENCIL_BIT)
            : VkImageAspectFlags(VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT),
      0, 1, 0, 1};
  if (trace_image) {
    REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_trace]   vkCreateImageView aspect={:#x}", uint32_t(view.subresourceRange.aspectMask));
  }
  device->functions().vkCreateImageView(device->device(), &view, nullptr, &image.view);
  if (trace_image) {
    REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_trace]   image ready");
  }
  return true;
}

void NativeCommandProcessor::DestroyImage(Image& image) {
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* device = vulkan_device();
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = device->functions();
  if (image.view) dfn.vkDestroyImageView(device->device(), image.view, nullptr);
  if (image.image) dfn.vkDestroyImage(device->device(), image.image, nullptr);
  if (image.memory) dfn.vkFreeMemory(device->device(), image.memory, nullptr);
  image = Image();
}

void NativeCommandProcessor::InitializeLayout(Image& image) {
  if (image.initialized) return;
  VkImageMemoryBarrier barrier = {};
  barrier.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_MEMORY_BARRIER;
  barrier.dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_MEMORY_READ_BIT | VK_ACCESS_MEMORY_WRITE_BIT;
  barrier.oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
  barrier.newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL;
  barrier.srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
  barrier.dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
  barrier.image = image.image;
  barrier.subresourceRange = {image.aspect, 0, 1, 0, 1};
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = vulkan_device()->functions();
  dfn.vkCmdPipelineBarrier(cb_, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TOP_OF_PIPE_BIT, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_ALL_COMMANDS_BIT, 0, 0, nullptr,
                           0, nullptr, 1, &barrier);
  // New memory has undefined contents: start colour at 0 and depth at 1 (far), as after a clear.
  VkImageSubresourceRange range = {image.aspect, 0, 1, 0, 1};
  if (image.aspect & VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_DEPTH_BIT) {
    VkClearDepthStencilValue ds = {1.0f, 0};
    dfn.vkCmdClearDepthStencilImage(cb_, image.image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, &ds, 1, &range);
  } else {
    VkClearColorValue color = {};
    dfn.vkCmdClearColorImage(cb_, image.image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, &color, 1, &range);
  }
  FullBarrier();
  image.initialized = true;
}

void NativeCommandProcessor::RetireImage(Image& image) {
  if (image.image) {
    // Into the slot of the frame being recorded: open it first, otherwise the image would land in the slot
    // EnsureFrame empties right away while the previous frame may still be using it.
    EnsureFrame();
    RetireViews(image.image);
    retired_images_[uint32_t(frame_ % kFramesInFlight)].push_back(image);
  }
  image = Image();
}

void NativeCommandProcessor::FullBarrier() {
  VkMemoryBarrier barrier = {};
  barrier.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_MEMORY_BARRIER;
  barrier.srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_MEMORY_WRITE_BIT;
  barrier.dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_MEMORY_READ_BIT | VK_ACCESS_MEMORY_WRITE_BIT;
  vulkan_device()->functions().vkCmdPipelineBarrier(cb_, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_ALL_COMMANDS_BIT,
                                                    VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_ALL_COMMANDS_BIT, 0, 1, &barrier, 0, nullptr,
                                                    0, nullptr);
}

NativeCommandProcessor::Image* NativeCommandProcessor::GetRenderTarget(uint32_t base_tiles, uint32_t pitch_pixels,
                                                                       uint32_t format, xenos::MsaaSamples msaa,
                                                                       bool is_depth, uint32_t min_height) {
  // Images are in EDRAM samples: 2X MSAA doubles the height, 4X both dimensions. D3D clears at half the
  // resolution with 4X MSAA (pitch 640 for a 1280 surface) - the same EDRAM tiles, so the same image; keyed by
  // the pixel pitch and MSAA, those clears went to an image of their own and the UI stencil was never cleared.
  uint32_t sx = uint32_t(msaa >= xenos::MsaaSamples::k4X), sy = uint32_t(msaa >= xenos::MsaaSamples::k2X);
  pitch_pixels = std::max(pitch_pixels << sx, uint32_t(8));
  min_height = std::max(min_height << sy, uint32_t(8));
  uint64_t key = RenderTargetKey(base_tiles, pitch_pixels, format, xenos::MsaaSamples::k1X, is_depth);
  Image& image = render_targets_[key];
  if (image.image && image.height >= min_height) {
    return &image;
  }
  uint32_t height = std::max(min_height, image.height);
  height = (height + 15) & ~15u;
  if (image.image) {
    // Grow: the old contents are dropped (rare; the next stage keeps them). The image may still be used by
    // commands recorded in this frame, so it is destroyed later.
    RetireImage(image);
  }
  // Creating the image records an initialization barrier, and barriers must not be recorded inside a
  // render pass; also, after a submit cb_ is null until the next frame is opened, so make sure a command
  // buffer is recording first (recording into the null handle crashed the NVIDIA driver).
  EnsureFrame();
  EndRendering();
  VkFormat vk_format =
      is_depth ? VK_FORMAT_D32_SFLOAT_S8_UINT : ColorTargetFormat(xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat(format));
  static const bool trace_rt = std::getenv("SDNX_TRACE_DRAW") != nullptr;
  if (trace_rt) {
    REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_trace] render target base={} pitch={} guest_format={} depth={} height={} -> {}", base_tiles,
                pitch_pixels, format, is_depth, height, int(vk_format));
  }
  if (!CreateImage(image, vk_format, pitch_pixels, height, is_depth)) {
    render_targets_.erase(key);
    return nullptr;
  }
  InitializeLayout(image);
  return &image;
}

NativeCommandProcessor::Image* NativeCommandProcessor::GetResolved(uint32_t address, VkFormat format,
                                                                   uint32_t width, uint32_t height) {
  width = std::max(width, uint32_t(8));
  height = std::max(height, uint32_t(8));
  Image& image = resolved_[address];
  if (image.image && image.format == format && image.width >= width && image.height >= height) {
    return &image;
  }
  if (image.image) RetireImage(image);
  EnsureFrame();
  EndRendering();
  if (!CreateImage(image, format, width, height, false)) {
    resolved_.erase(address);
    return nullptr;
  }
  InitializeLayout(image);
  return &image;
}

bool NativeCommandProcessor::CreatePresentPipeline() {
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* device = vulkan_device();
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = device->functions();
  VkDescriptorSetLayoutBinding bindings[3] = {};
  bindings[0] = {0, VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_SAMPLED_IMAGE, 1, VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT, nullptr};
  bindings[1] = {1, VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_SAMPLER, 1, VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT, nullptr};
  bindings[2] = {2, VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_IMAGE, 1, VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT, nullptr};
  VkDescriptorSetLayoutCreateInfo set_info = {};
  set_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_SET_LAYOUT_CREATE_INFO;
  set_info.bindingCount = 3;
  set_info.pBindings = bindings;
  if (dfn.vkCreateDescriptorSetLayout(device->device(), &set_info, nullptr, &present_set_layout_) != VK_SUCCESS)
    return false;
  VkPushConstantRange push = {VK_SHADER_STAGE_COMPUTE_BIT, 0, 16};
  VkPipelineLayoutCreateInfo layout_info = {};
  layout_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_PIPELINE_LAYOUT_CREATE_INFO;
  layout_info.setLayoutCount = 1;
  layout_info.pSetLayouts = &present_set_layout_;
  layout_info.pushConstantRangeCount = 1;
  layout_info.pPushConstantRanges = &push;
  if (dfn.vkCreatePipelineLayout(device->device(), &layout_info, nullptr, &present_pipeline_layout_) != VK_SUCCESS)
    return false;
  present_pipeline_ = ui::vulkan::util::CreateComputePipeline(device, present_pipeline_layout_, shaders::kPresentCs,
                                                              sizeof(shaders::kPresentCs));
  if (present_pipeline_ == VK_NULL_HANDLE) return false;
  VkDescriptorPoolSize sizes[3] = {{VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_SAMPLED_IMAGE, kFramesInFlight},
                                   {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_SAMPLER, kFramesInFlight},
                                   {VK_DESCRIPTOR_TYPE_STORAGE_IMAGE, kFramesInFlight}};
  VkDescriptorPoolCreateInfo pool_info = {};
  pool_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_POOL_CREATE_INFO;
  pool_info.maxSets = kFramesInFlight;
  pool_info.poolSizeCount = 3;
  pool_info.pPoolSizes = sizes;
  if (dfn.vkCreateDescriptorPool(device->device(), &pool_info, nullptr, &present_descriptor_pool_) != VK_SUCCESS)
    return false;
  std::array<VkDescriptorSetLayout, kFramesInFlight> layouts;
  layouts.fill(present_set_layout_);
  VkDescriptorSetAllocateInfo alloc = {};
  alloc.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DESCRIPTOR_SET_ALLOCATE_INFO;
  alloc.descriptorPool = present_descriptor_pool_;
  alloc.descriptorSetCount = kFramesInFlight;
  alloc.pSetLayouts = layouts.data();
  if (dfn.vkAllocateDescriptorSets(device->device(), &alloc, present_sets_.data()) != VK_SUCCESS) return false;
  VkSamplerCreateInfo sampler = {};
  sampler.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_SAMPLER_CREATE_INFO;
  sampler.magFilter = VK_FILTER_LINEAR;
  sampler.minFilter = VK_FILTER_LINEAR;
  sampler.addressModeU = sampler.addressModeV = sampler.addressModeW = VK_SAMPLER_ADDRESS_MODE_CLAMP_TO_EDGE;
  sampler.maxLod = 0.0f;
  return dfn.vkCreateSampler(device->device(), &sampler, nullptr, &linear_sampler_) == VK_SUCCESS;
}

bool NativeCommandProcessor::SetupContext() {
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* device = vulkan_device();
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = device->functions();
  VkCommandPoolCreateInfo pool_info = {};
  pool_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMMAND_POOL_CREATE_INFO;
  pool_info.flags = VK_COMMAND_POOL_CREATE_RESET_COMMAND_BUFFER_BIT;
  pool_info.queueFamilyIndex = device->queue_family_graphics_compute();
  if (dfn.vkCreateCommandPool(device->device(), &pool_info, nullptr, &command_pool_) != VK_SUCCESS) return false;
  VkCommandBufferAllocateInfo alloc_info = {};
  alloc_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMMAND_BUFFER_ALLOCATE_INFO;
  alloc_info.commandPool = command_pool_;
  alloc_info.level = VK_COMMAND_BUFFER_LEVEL_PRIMARY;
  alloc_info.commandBufferCount = kFramesInFlight;
  if (dfn.vkAllocateCommandBuffers(device->device(), &alloc_info, command_buffers_.data()) != VK_SUCCESS)
    return false;
  VkFenceCreateInfo fence_info = {};
  fence_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_FENCE_CREATE_INFO;
  fence_info.flags = VK_FENCE_CREATE_SIGNALED_BIT;
  for (VkFence& fence : fences_) {
    if (dfn.vkCreateFence(device->device(), &fence_info, nullptr, &fence) != VK_SUCCESS) return false;
  }
  if (!CreatePresentPipeline()) {
    REXGPU_ERROR("[sdnx_native] could not create the output pipeline");
    return false;
  }
  if (!InitializeDraws()) {
    REXGPU_ERROR("[sdnx_native] could not create the draw resources");
    return false;
  }
  stats_start_ = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
  REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_native] context ready ({} frames in flight, EDRAM-lite, resolves, output)", kFramesInFlight);
  return true;
}

void NativeCommandProcessor::ShutdownContext() {
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* device = vulkan_device();
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = device->functions();
  dfn.vkDeviceWaitIdle(device->device());
  ShutdownQueries();
  ShutdownGeometry();
  ShutdownDraws();
  for (auto& slot_images : retired_images_) {
    for (Image& retired : slot_images) DestroyImage(retired);
    slot_images.clear();
  }
  for (auto& it : render_targets_) DestroyImage(it.second);
  render_targets_.clear();
  DestroyImage(depth_resolve_temp_);
  for (auto& it : resolved_stencil_) DestroyImage(it.second);
  resolved_stencil_.clear();
  for (auto& it : resolved_) DestroyImage(it.second);
  resolved_.clear();
  if (linear_sampler_) dfn.vkDestroySampler(device->device(), linear_sampler_, nullptr);
  if (present_descriptor_pool_) dfn.vkDestroyDescriptorPool(device->device(), present_descriptor_pool_, nullptr);
  if (present_pipeline_) dfn.vkDestroyPipeline(device->device(), present_pipeline_, nullptr);
  if (present_pipeline_layout_) dfn.vkDestroyPipelineLayout(device->device(), present_pipeline_layout_, nullptr);
  if (present_set_layout_) dfn.vkDestroyDescriptorSetLayout(device->device(), present_set_layout_, nullptr);
  for (VkFence& fence : fences_) {
    if (fence) dfn.vkDestroyFence(device->device(), fence, nullptr);
    fence = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  }
  if (command_pool_) dfn.vkDestroyCommandPool(device->device(), command_pool_, nullptr);
  command_pool_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  shaders_.clear();
}

void NativeCommandProcessor::EnsureFrame() {
  if (frame_open_) return;
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* device = vulkan_device();
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = device->functions();
  uint32_t slot = uint32_t(frame_ % kFramesInFlight);
  dfn.vkWaitForFences(device->device(), 1, &fences_[slot], VK_TRUE, UINT64_MAX);
  dfn.vkResetFences(device->device(), 1, &fences_[slot]);
  cb_ = command_buffers_[slot];
  VkCommandBufferBeginInfo begin = {};
  begin.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_COMMAND_BUFFER_BEGIN_INFO;
  begin.flags = VK_COMMAND_BUFFER_USAGE_ONE_TIME_SUBMIT_BIT;
  dfn.vkBeginCommandBuffer(cb_, &begin);  // resets it (pool created with RESET_COMMAND_BUFFER_BIT)
  bound_ = {};  // nothing is bound in a new command buffer
  upload_[slot].offset = 0;
  frame_open_ = true;
  ResetQueries(slot);  // the slot's previous frame has completed: its occlusion counts are readable
  ReleaseRetiredGeometry(slot);
  callback_frame_.store(uint32_t(frame_) + 1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
  ReleaseRetiredViews(slot);
  for (Image& retired : retired_images_[slot]) DestroyImage(retired);
  retired_images_[slot].clear();
  if (!dummies_initialized_) {
    // The placeholder textures: GENERAL layout (as their descriptors say) and white. They were left in
    // UNDEFINED, so the shaders sampled undefined data (zero on NVIDIA): textured UI came out black.
    dummies_initialized_ = true;
    for (Image* dummy : {&dummy_2d_, &dummy_3d_, &dummy_cube_}) {
      uint32_t layers = dummy == &dummy_cube_ ? 6 : 1;
      VkImageMemoryBarrier barrier = {};
      barrier.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_MEMORY_BARRIER;
      barrier.dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT;
      barrier.oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
      barrier.newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL;
      barrier.srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
      barrier.dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
      barrier.image = dummy->image;
      barrier.subresourceRange = {VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT, 0, 1, 0, layers};
      dfn.vkCmdPipelineBarrier(cb_, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TOP_OF_PIPE_BIT, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT, 0, 0, nullptr,
                               0, nullptr, 1, &barrier);
      VkClearColorValue white = {{1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f}};
      dfn.vkCmdClearColorImage(cb_, dummy->image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, &white, 1, &barrier.subresourceRange);
      dummy->initialized = true;
    }
    FullBarrier();
  }
}

bool NativeCommandProcessor::SubmitFrame() {
  if (!frame_open_) return true;
  EndRendering();
  CopyQueryResults(uint32_t(frame_ % kFramesInFlight));
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* device = vulkan_device();
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = device->functions();
  dfn.vkEndCommandBuffer(cb_);
  VkSubmitInfo submit = {};
  submit.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_SUBMIT_INFO;
  submit.commandBufferCount = 1;
  submit.pCommandBuffers = &cb_;
  VkResult result;
  {
    auto queue = device->AcquireQueue(device->queue_family_graphics_compute(), 0);
    result = dfn.vkQueueSubmit(queue.queue(), 1, &submit, fences_[uint32_t(frame_ % kFramesInFlight)]);
  }
  frame_open_ = false;
  cb_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  ++frame_;
  return result == VK_SUCCESS;
}

Shader* NativeCommandProcessor::LoadShader(xenos::ShaderType shader_type, uint32_t guest_address,
                                           const uint32_t* host_address, uint32_t dword_count) {
  (void)guest_address;
  ++stats_.shader_loads;
  uint64_t hash = XXH3_64bits(host_address, dword_count * sizeof(uint32_t));
  auto it = shaders_.find(hash);
  if (it != shaders_.end()) return it->second.get();
  ++stats_.shader_new;
  auto shader = std::make_unique<Shader>(shader_type, hash, host_address, dword_count);
  Shader* result = shader.get();
  shaders_.emplace(hash, std::move(shader));
  return result;
}

bool NativeCommandProcessor::IssueDraw(xenos::PrimitiveType prim_type, uint32_t index_count,
                                       IndexBufferInfo* index_buffer_info, bool major_mode_explicit) {
  (void)prim_type;
  (void)index_buffer_info;
  (void)major_mode_explicit;
  // A resolve arrives as a draw in the EDRAM copy mode (as in the Vulkan backend).
  if (register_file_->Get<reg::RB_MODECONTROL>().edram_mode == xenos::EdramMode::kCopy) {
    return IssueCopy();
  }
  ++stats_.draws;
  stats_.indices += index_count;
  return DrawNative(prim_type, index_count, index_buffer_info);
}

bool NativeCommandProcessor::IssueCopy() {
  ++stats_.copies;
  static const bool skip_resolves = std::getenv("SDNX_NO_RESOLVE") != nullptr;
  if (skip_resolves) {
    return true;
  }
  draw_util::ResolveInfo info;
  if (!draw_util::GetResolveInfo(*register_file_, *memory_, 1, 1, false, false, info)) {
    return true;
  }
  if (!info.coordinate_info.width_div_8 || !info.height_div_8) {
    return true;
  }
  EnsureFrame();
  EndRendering();
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = vulkan_device()->functions();
  const RegisterFile& regs = *register_file_;
  auto surface = regs.Get<reg::RB_SURFACE_INFO>();
  uint32_t pitch = surface.surface_pitch;
  xenos::MsaaSamples msaa = surface.msaa_samples;
  bool copy_depth = info.IsCopyingDepth();

  // The rectangle in surface pixels, from the packed EDRAM base offset (see draw_util::GetResolveInfo).
  const draw_util::ResolveEdramInfo& edram = copy_depth ? info.depth_edram_info : info.color_edram_info;
  uint32_t original_base = copy_depth ? info.depth_original_base : info.color_original_base;
  uint32_t sx = uint32_t(msaa >= xenos::MsaaSamples::k4X), sy = uint32_t(msaa >= xenos::MsaaSamples::k2X);
  uint32_t pitch_tiles = std::max(xenos::GetSurfacePitchTiles(pitch, msaa, false), 1u);
  uint32_t offset_tiles = (edram.base_tiles - original_base) >> (copy_depth ? 0 : edram.format_is_64bpp);
  uint32_t x0 = ((offset_tiles % pitch_tiles) * xenos::kEdramTileWidthSamples +
                 (info.coordinate_info.edram_offset_x_div_8 << (sx + 3))) >> sx;
  uint32_t y0 = ((offset_tiles / pitch_tiles) * xenos::kEdramTileHeightSamples +
                 (info.coordinate_info.edram_offset_y_div_8 << (sy + 3))) >> sy;
  uint32_t width = info.coordinate_info.width_div_8 * 8, height = info.height_div_8 * 8;

  uint32_t format =
      copy_depth ? uint32_t(regs.Get<reg::RB_DEPTH_INFO>().depth_format) : uint32_t(edram.format);
  Image* source = GetRenderTarget(original_base, pitch, format, msaa, copy_depth, y0 + height);

  // Copy.
  if (source && info.copy_dest_extent_length) {
    if (copy_depth) {
      ++stats_.copies_depth;
      ResolveDepth(*source, info, x0, y0, width, height, sx, sy);
    } else {
      uint32_t dest_base = regs[XE_GPU_REG_RB_COPY_DEST_BASE] & 0x1FFFFFFF;
      uint32_t dest_w = info.copy_dest_coordinate_info.pitch_aligned_div_32 * 32;
      uint32_t dest_h = info.copy_dest_coordinate_info.height_aligned_div_32 * 32;
      VkFormat dest_format =
          ResolveDestFormat(xenos::TextureFormat(info.copy_dest_info.copy_dest_format), source->format);
      // The guest asks for a different format here (HDR 7e3 -> 16_16_16_16), but vkCmdBlitImage only
      // accepts blit-compatible formats. Allocate the resolved texture in the source format so the copy is
      // legal; the value encoding of the guest format is a separate conversion step (compute shader).
      // The rectangle lands at the destination offset (not at its render target position).
      uint32_t dx = info.copy_dest_coordinate_info.offset_x_div_8 * 8;
      uint32_t dy = info.copy_dest_coordinate_info.offset_y_div_8 * 8;
      Image* dest =
          GetResolved(dest_base, source->format, std::max(dest_w, dx + width), std::max(dest_h, dy + height));
      resolved_extent_[dest_base] = info.copy_dest_extent_start + info.copy_dest_extent_length - dest_base;
      if (log_frame_) {
        REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_frame] resolve rt base={} fmt={} {}x{} at {},{} -> {:#x} at {},{} fmt={} swap={} clear "
                    "color={} depth={}",
                    original_base, format, width, height, x0, y0, dest_base, dx, dy,
                    uint32_t(info.copy_dest_info.copy_dest_format), uint32_t(info.copy_dest_info.copy_dest_swap),
                    uint32_t(info.rb_copy_control.color_clear_enable), uint32_t(info.rb_copy_control.depth_clear_enable));
      }
      static const bool trace_resolve = std::getenv("SDNX_TRACE_RESOLVE") != nullptr;
      static std::unordered_map<uint64_t, int> traced_resolves;
      uint64_t trace_key = uint64_t(dest_base) ^ (uint64_t(format) << 32) ^ (uint64_t(width) << 40) ^
                           (uint64_t(uint32_t(info.copy_dest_info.value)) << 20);
      if (trace_resolve && traced_resolves[trace_key]++ == 0) {
        REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_resolve] rt base={} fmt={} host={} {}x{} at {},{} -> dest {:#x} fmt={} number={} "
                    "exp_bias={} dest {}x{} img {}x{} dest offset {},{}",
                    original_base, format, int(source->format), width, height, x0, y0, dest_base,
                    uint32_t(info.copy_dest_info.copy_dest_format), uint32_t(info.copy_dest_info.copy_dest_number),
                    int32_t(info.copy_dest_info.copy_dest_exp_bias), dest_w, dest_h, dest ? dest->width : 0,
                    dest ? dest->height : 0, dx, dy);
      }
      (void)dest_format;
      ++resolved_generation_[dest_base];
      resolved_swap_[dest_base] = info.copy_dest_info.copy_dest_swap != 0;
      resolved_frame_[dest_base] = frame_;
      if (dest) {
        // vkCmdBlitImage needs blit-compatible formats; the HDR 7e3 -> 16F resolve is not, and an
        // incompatible blit is invalid usage (it took the NVIDIA driver down). Skip just the copy and count
        // it; the compute-shader conversion is the proper fix (next stage).
        static const bool trace_copy = std::getenv("SDNX_TRACE_DRAW") != nullptr;
        if (source->format != dest->format) {
          ++stats_.copies_incompatible;
          if (trace_copy) {
            REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_trace] resolve skipped: {} -> {} ({}x{} at {},{}), dest base {:#x}",
                        int(source->format), int(dest->format), width, height, x0, y0, dest_base);
          }
        } else {
          FullBarrier();
          if (trace_copy) {
            REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_trace] resolve src img={} base={:#x} fmt={} pitch={} msaa={} drawn={} -> dest={:#x} "
                        "{}x{}",
                        reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(source), original_base, int(source->format), pitch,
                        uint32_t(msaa), uint32_t(source->drawn), dest_base, width, height);
          }
          VkImageBlit blit = {};
          blit.srcSubresource = {VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT, 0, 0, 1};
          // The source is in samples (MSAA surfaces are larger): the blit downsamples.
          blit.srcOffsets[0] = {int32_t(x0 << sx), int32_t(y0 << sy), 0};
          blit.srcOffsets[1] = {int32_t(std::min((x0 + width) << sx, source->width)),
                                int32_t(std::min((y0 + height) << sy, source->height)), 1};
          blit.dstSubresource = {VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT, 0, 0, 1};
          blit.dstOffsets[0] = {int32_t(dx), int32_t(dy), 0};
          uint32_t copy_w = uint32_t(blit.srcOffsets[1].x - blit.srcOffsets[0].x) >> sx;
          uint32_t copy_h = uint32_t(blit.srcOffsets[1].y - blit.srcOffsets[0].y) >> sy;
          copy_w = std::min(copy_w, dest->width > dx ? dest->width - dx : 0u);
          copy_h = std::min(copy_h, dest->height > dy ? dest->height - dy : 0u);
          blit.dstOffsets[1] = {int32_t(dx + copy_w), int32_t(dy + copy_h), 1};
          blit.srcOffsets[1].x = blit.srcOffsets[0].x + int32_t(copy_w << sx);
          blit.srcOffsets[1].y = blit.srcOffsets[0].y + int32_t(copy_h << sy);
          if (copy_w && copy_h) {
            dfn.vkCmdBlitImage(cb_, source->image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, dest->image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL,
                               1, &blit, (sx || sy) ? VK_FILTER_LINEAR : VK_FILTER_NEAREST);
          }
        }
        DumpResolveIfRequested(dest_base, *dest);
      }
    }
  }

  // Clears requested by the resolve.
  auto copy_control = info.rb_copy_control;
  if (copy_control.color_clear_enable && !copy_depth) {
    Image* target = GetRenderTarget(info.color_original_base, pitch, uint32_t(info.color_edram_info.format), msaa,
                                    false, y0 + height);
    if (target) {
      FullBarrier();
      VkClearColorValue color = DecodeColorClear(xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat(info.color_edram_info.format),
                                                 info.rb_color_clear, info.rb_color_clear_lo);
      VkImageSubresourceRange range = {VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT, 0, 1, 0, 1};
      // vkCmdClearColorImage clears the whole image; the resolve rectangle is the whole target in practice.
      // EDRAM is one memory: a clear through one format also clears the surfaces of other formats at the same
      // base and pitch. Images are per format here, so clear all of them - otherwise what was drawn through
      // another format stays (the menu selection highlight accumulated on every item it passed over).
      uint64_t base_pitch = uint64_t(info.color_original_base & 0xFFF) | (uint64_t((pitch << sx) & 0xFFFF) << 12);
      for (auto& it : render_targets_) {
        bool is_depth = (it.first >> 38) & 1;
        if (is_depth || (it.first & 0xFFFFFFF) != base_pitch || !it.second.image) continue;
        dfn.vkCmdClearColorImage(cb_, it.second.image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, &color, 1, &range);
        ++stats_.clears;
      }
    }
  }
  if (copy_control.depth_clear_enable) {
    Image* target = GetRenderTarget(info.depth_original_base, pitch,
                                    uint32_t(regs.Get<reg::RB_DEPTH_INFO>().depth_format), msaa, true, y0 + height);
    if (target) {
      FullBarrier();
      VkClearDepthStencilValue ds = {float(info.rb_depth_clear >> 8) / float(0xFFFFFF),
                                     info.rb_depth_clear & 0xFF};
      VkImageSubresourceRange range = {VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_DEPTH_BIT | VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_STENCIL_BIT, 0, 1, 0, 1};
      dfn.vkCmdClearDepthStencilImage(cb_, target->image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, &ds, 1, &range);
      ++stats_.clears;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

void NativeCommandProcessor::ResolveDepth(const Image& source, const draw_util::ResolveInfo& info, uint32_t x0,
                                          uint32_t y0, uint32_t width, uint32_t height, uint32_t sx,
                                          uint32_t sy) {
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* device = vulkan_device();
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = device->functions();
  uint32_t dest_base = (*register_file_)[XE_GPU_REG_RB_COPY_DEST_BASE] & 0x1FFFFFFF;
  uint32_t dest_w = info.copy_dest_coordinate_info.pitch_aligned_div_32 * 32;
  uint32_t dest_h = info.copy_dest_coordinate_info.height_aligned_div_32 * 32;
  uint32_t dx = info.copy_dest_coordinate_info.offset_x_div_8 * 8;
  uint32_t dy = info.copy_dest_coordinate_info.offset_y_div_8 * 8;
  Image* dest = GetResolved(dest_base, VK_FORMAT_R32_SFLOAT, std::max(dest_w, dx + width),
                            std::max(dest_h, dy + height));
  ++resolved_generation_[dest_base];
  resolved_frame_[dest_base] = frame_;
  resolved_extent_[dest_base] = info.copy_dest_extent_start + info.copy_dest_extent_length - dest_base;
  if (log_frame_) {
    REXGPU_INFO("[sdnx_frame] depth resolve {}x{} at {},{} (samples x{} y{}) -> {:#x} at {},{}", width, height, x0,
                y0, 1u << sx, 1u << sy, dest_base, dx, dy);
  }
  if (!dest) return;
  // Source rectangle in samples, clamped to the image.
  uint32_t src_x = x0 << sx, src_y = y0 << sy;
  if (src_x >= source.width || src_y >= source.height) return;
  uint32_t src_w = std::min(width << sx, source.width - src_x), src_h = std::min(height << sy, source.height - src_y);
  uint32_t out_w = std::min(src_w >> sx, dest->width > dx ? dest->width - dx : 0u);
  uint32_t out_h = std::min(src_h >> sy, dest->height > dy ? dest->height - dy : 0u);
  if (!out_w || !out_h) return;
  src_w = out_w << sx;
  src_h = out_h << sy;
  VkDeviceSize offset = Upload(nullptr, VkDeviceSize(src_w) * src_h * 4, 16);
  if (offset == UINT64_MAX) return;
  VkBuffer buffer = upload_[uint32_t(frame_ % kFramesInFlight)].buffer;
  EndRendering();
  FullBarrier();
  VkBufferImageCopy region = {};
  region.bufferOffset = offset;
  region.imageSubresource = {VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_DEPTH_BIT, 0, 0, 1};
  region.imageOffset = {int32_t(src_x), int32_t(src_y), 0};
  region.imageExtent = {src_w, src_h, 1};
  dfn.vkCmdCopyImageToBuffer(cb_, source.image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, buffer, 1, &region);
  FullBarrier();
  region.imageSubresource.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
  if (!sx && !sy) {
    region.imageOffset = {int32_t(dx), int32_t(dy), 0};
    dfn.vkCmdCopyBufferToImage(cb_, buffer, dest->image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, 1, &region);
    // Stencil, for TryRestoreDepth.
    Image& stencil = resolved_stencil_[dest_base];
    if (stencil.image && (stencil.width < dest->width || stencil.height < dest->height)) RetireImage(stencil);
    if (!stencil.image && CreateImage(stencil, VK_FORMAT_R8_UNORM, dest->width, dest->height, false)) {
      InitializeLayout(stencil);
    }
    VkDeviceSize stencil_offset = stencil.image ? Upload(nullptr, VkDeviceSize(src_w) * src_h, 16) : UINT64_MAX;
    if (stencil_offset != UINT64_MAX) {
      FullBarrier();
      VkBufferImageCopy s_region = {};
      s_region.bufferOffset = stencil_offset;
      s_region.imageSubresource = {VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_STENCIL_BIT, 0, 0, 1};
      s_region.imageOffset = {int32_t(src_x), int32_t(src_y), 0};
      s_region.imageExtent = {src_w, src_h, 1};
      dfn.vkCmdCopyImageToBuffer(cb_, source.image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, buffer, 1, &s_region);
      FullBarrier();
      s_region.imageSubresource.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
      s_region.imageOffset = {int32_t(dx), int32_t(dy), 0};
      dfn.vkCmdCopyBufferToImage(cb_, buffer, stencil.image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, 1, &s_region);
    }
  } else {
    if (!depth_resolve_temp_.image || depth_resolve_temp_.width < src_w || depth_resolve_temp_.height < src_h) {
      uint32_t tw = std::max(src_w, depth_resolve_temp_.width), th = std::max(src_h, depth_resolve_temp_.height);
      if (depth_resolve_temp_.image) RetireImage(depth_resolve_temp_);
      if (!CreateImage(depth_resolve_temp_, VK_FORMAT_R32_SFLOAT, tw, th, false)) return;
      InitializeLayout(depth_resolve_temp_);
    }
    region.imageOffset = {0, 0, 0};
    dfn.vkCmdCopyBufferToImage(cb_, buffer, depth_resolve_temp_.image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, 1, &region);
    FullBarrier();
    VkImageBlit blit = {};
    blit.srcSubresource = {VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT, 0, 0, 1};
    blit.srcOffsets[1] = {int32_t(src_w), int32_t(src_h), 1};
    blit.dstSubresource = blit.srcSubresource;
    blit.dstOffsets[0] = {int32_t(dx), int32_t(dy), 0};
    blit.dstOffsets[1] = {int32_t(dx + out_w), int32_t(dy + out_h), 1};
    dfn.vkCmdBlitImage(cb_, depth_resolve_temp_.image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, dest->image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL,
                       1, &blit, VK_FILTER_NEAREST);
  }
  FullBarrier();
}

bool NativeCommandProcessor::TryRestoreDepth(Shader* ps) {
  const RegisterFile& regs = *register_file_;
  if (!ps || regs.Get<reg::RB_MODECONTROL>().edram_mode != xenos::EdramMode::kColorDepth) return false;
  if ((regs[XE_GPU_REG_RB_COLOR_MASK] & 0xFFFF) != 0xF) return false;
  auto depth_control = regs.Get<reg::RB_DEPTHCONTROL>();
  if (depth_control.z_enable || depth_control.stencil_enable) return false;
  auto color_info = regs.Get<reg::RB_COLOR_INFO>(reg::RB_COLOR_INFO::rt_register_indices[0]);
  if (color_info.color_format != xenos::ColorRenderTargetFormat::k_8_8_8_8) return false;
  auto surface = regs.Get<reg::RB_SURFACE_INFO>();
  if (surface.msaa_samples != xenos::MsaaSamples::k1X || !surface.surface_pitch) return false;
  static string::StringBuffer disassembly;
  if (!ps->is_ucode_analyzed()) ps->AnalyzeUcode(disassembly);
  if (ps->texture_bindings().size() != 1) return false;
  xenos::xe_gpu_texture_fetch_t fetch = regs.GetTextureFetch(ps->texture_bindings()[0].fetch_constant);
  if (fetch.type != xenos::FetchConstantType::kTexture || fetch.format != xenos::TextureFormat::k_8_8_8_8) return false;
  uint32_t base = (uint32_t(fetch.base_address) << 12) & 0x1FFFFFFF;
  auto first = resolved_.find(base);
  if (first == resolved_.end() || first->second.format != VK_FORMAT_R32_SFLOAT) return false;
  // The colour target must alias a depth buffer drawn to before (same EDRAM base and pitch).
  uint32_t pitch = surface.surface_pitch;
  if (render_targets_.find(RenderTargetKey(color_info.color_base, std::max(pitch, 8u), 0, xenos::MsaaSamples::k1X,
                                           true)) == render_targets_.end()) {
    return false;
  }
  uint32_t tw = fetch.size_2d.width + 1, th = fetch.size_2d.height + 1;
  Image* target = GetRenderTarget(color_info.color_base, pitch, 0, xenos::MsaaSamples::k1X, true, th);
  if (!target) return false;
  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = vulkan_device()->functions();
  EnsureFrame();
  EndRendering();
  VkBuffer buffer = upload_[uint32_t(frame_ % kFramesInFlight)].buffer;
  uint32_t pitch_pixels = fetch.pitch ? uint32_t(fetch.pitch) << 5 : ((tw + 31) & ~31u);
  uint64_t row_bytes = uint64_t(pitch_pixels) * 4;
  for (const auto& part : resolved_) {
    if (!part.second.image || part.second.format != VK_FORMAT_R32_SFLOAT) continue;
    if (part.first < base || part.first >= base + row_bytes * th) continue;
    uint64_t delta = part.first - base;
    uint32_t y = uint32_t(delta / row_bytes), x = uint32_t((delta % row_bytes) / 4);
    if (x >= tw || y >= th || x >= target->width || y >= target->height) continue;
    uint32_t w = std::min({tw - x, part.second.width, target->width - x});
    uint32_t h = std::min({th - y, part.second.height, target->height - y});
    VkDeviceSize offset = Upload(nullptr, VkDeviceSize(w) * h * 4, 16);
    if (offset == UINT64_MAX) continue;
    FullBarrier();
    VkBufferImageCopy region = {};
    region.bufferOffset = offset;
    region.imageSubresource = {VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT, 0, 0, 1};
    region.imageExtent = {w, h, 1};
    dfn.vkCmdCopyImageToBuffer(cb_, part.second.image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, buffer, 1, &region);
    FullBarrier();
    region.imageSubresource.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_DEPTH_BIT;
    region.imageOffset = {int32_t(x), int32_t(y), 0};
    dfn.vkCmdCopyBufferToImage(cb_, buffer, target->image, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, 1, &region);
    auto stencil = resolved_stencil_.find(part.first);
    if (stencil == resolved_stencil_.end() || !stencil->second.image) continue;
    w = std::min(w, stencil->second.width);
    h = std::min(h, stencil->second.height);
    offset = Upload(nullptr, VkDeviceSize(w) * h, 16);
    if (offset == UINT64_MAX) continue;
    region.bufferOffset = offset;
```
